karīyati kattukiriyāya sambandhīyatīti kammaṃ, tasmiṃ kammakārakai dutiyāvibhatti hauti| taṃ tividhaṃ nibbatti, vikati, patti bhaidaina, tattha nibbattikammai mātā puttaṃ vijāyati, āhārau sukhaṃ janayati, kaṭaṃ karauti dattau| vikatiyaṃ kaṭṭha+maṅgāraṃ karauti, suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ karauti, vīhayau lunāti| pattiyaṃ dattau gharaṃ pavisati, ādic‍caṃ passati, dhammaṃ suṇāti, paṇḍitai payirupāsati|

vuttañ‍ca  –

nibbatti+vikati+patti-bhaidā kammaṃ tidhā mataṃ|

kattukriyābhigammantaṃ, sukha+maṅgāraṃ+gharaṃ yathāti||

kaṭaṃ karauti vipulaṃ dassanīyanti atthaiva guṇayuttassa kammatā, vipulaṃ karauti, dassanīyaṃ karautīti kriyāya sambandhiyamānattā| audanau pac‍cataiti audanasaddatau kammatā nappatīyatai, kiñ‍carahi ākhyātatau|

icchitakammaṃ yathā-gāvuṃ payau dauhati, gaumantaṃ gāvaṃ yācati, gāva+mava rundhati vajaṃ, māṇavakaṃ maggaṃ pucchati, gaumantaṃ gāvaṃ bhikkhatai, rukkha+mavacināti phalāni, rukkhātyatthau| sissaṃ dhammaṃ brūtai| aittha payau, gāvaṃ ic‍cādi icchitaṃ, gāvuṃ, gaumanta+mic‍cādi anicchitaṃ| aiva+manicchitaipi kaṇṭakaṃ maddati, visaṃ gilati| yaṃ naivi+cchitaṃ, nāpi anicchitaṃ, tatrāpi gāmaṃ gacchantau rukkhamūla+mupasappati|

ādhārai adhisi+ṭhā+sānaṃ payaugai ca, pathaviṃ adhisaissati, gāma+madhitiṭṭhati, rukkha+majjhāsataiti| aittha pathavinti pathaviyanti atthau| aiva+mabhi, nipubbavisassāpi, dhamma+mabhinivisatai, dhammai vā| tathā upa, nva+jjhā+pubbavasatissa, gāma+mupavasati, gāma+manuvasati, vihāra+madhivasati, gāma+māvasati, agāraṃ ajjhavasati, aittha gāmanti gāmaityatthau| tathā tappānā+cāraipi, nadiṃ pibati, gāmaṃ carati, nadiyaṃtyatthau| sacai maṃ nālapissatīti mayā saddhiṃtyatthau| vihitāva paṭi-yaugaipi dutiyā, paṭibhantu taṃ cunda baujjhaṅgā, tampaṭi baujjhaṅgā bhāsantūti atthau| upamā maṃ patibhāti, upamā maṃ upaṭṭhātityatthau| dhātunāyuttai ‘‘tassa nappaṭibhātī’’ti chaṭṭhī|

3. kāladdhāna+mac‍cantasaṃyaugai

kāladdhānaṃ dabba+guṇa+kriyāhi ac‍cantasaṃyaugai taihi kāladdhānavācīhi dutiyā hauti| kālai-sattāhaṃ gavapānaṃ, māsaṃ maṃsaudanaṃ, saradaṃ ramaṇīyā nadī, sabbakālaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ nandanaṃ, tayau māsai abhidhammaṃ daisaiti| addhani-yaujanaṃ vanarāji, yaujanaṃ dīghau pabbatau, kausaṃ sajjhāyati|

pubbanhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā, aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā, imaṃ rattiṃ cattārau mahārājānauti aivamādīsu kālavācīhi ac‍cantasaṃyaugai dutiyā, bahulaṃvidhānā vibhattivipal‍lāsau vā| māsainā+nuvākau+dhītau, kausainā+nuvākau+dhītauti karaṇatthai tatiyā|

4. gatibaudhāhārasaddatthākammakabhaj‍jādīnaṃ payauj‍jai

gatyatthānaṃ baudhatthānaṃ āhāratthānaṃ saddatthāna+makammakānaṃ bhaj‍jādīnañ‍ca payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā hauti| dattau gamayati māṇavakaṃ gāmaṃ, yāpayati vā| guru baudhayati māṇavakaṃ dhammaṃ, vaidayati vā| mātā bhaujayati putta+maudanaṃ, āsayati vā| guru ajjhāpayati sissaṃ vaidaṃ, pāṭhayati vā| payauj‍jatau+ñ‍ñatra kammai dutiyā| pausau āsayati dattaṃ, sāyayati vā| pausau añ‍ñaṃ bhaj‍jāpaiti, añ‍ñaṃ kauṭṭāpaiti, añ‍ñaṃ santharāpaiti|

5. harādīnaṃ vā

harādīnaṃ  payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā hauti vā| pausau hāraiti bhāraṃ dattaṃ, dattainaiti vā| dassayatai janaṃ rājā, janainaiti vā| abhivādayatai guruṃ dattaṃ, dattainaiti vā| ajjhauhāraiti sattuṃ dattaṃ, dattainaiti vā| kāraiti dattau dattaṃ, dattainaiti vā| pakkhai sabbatra kattari tatiyā|

6. na khādādīnaṃ

khādādīnaṃ payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā na hauti| gatyatthādīsu k‍vaci paṭisaidhattha+midaṃ| khādayati dattau dattaina, ādayati dattaina, avhāpayati datthaina, saddāyayati dattaina, nāyayati dattaina, kandayati dattaina|

7. jhādīhi yuttā

dhiādīhi yuttatau dutiyā hauti| dhi+ratthu+maṃ putikāyaṃ, antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nālandaṃ, rājagahassa ca nālandassa ca vivarabhūtai majjhaiti atthau| samādhāna+mantaraina, mucalinda+mabhitau saraṃ| chaṭṭhyatthai+yaṃ dutiyā|

8. lakkhaṇitthambhūtavicchāsva+bhinā

lakkhaṇādīsva+tthaisu abhinā yuttamhā dutiyā hauti| rukkha+mabhivij‍jautatai candau, aittha rukkhau lakkhaṇaṃ, candau lakkhitabbau, tatra abhinā rukkhassa lakkhaṇavuttitā pakāsīyatīti rukkhau abhinā yuttau nāma| sādhu daivadattau mātara+mabhi, mātari sādhuttaṃ pattautyatthau| rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃabhi vij‍jautatai candau, rukkhai rukkhaityatthau|

9. patiparīhi bhāgai ca

patiparīhi  yuttatau lakkhaṇādīsu bhāgai ca+tthai dutiyā hauti| rukkhaṃpati vij‍jautatai, sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃpati, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃpati tiṭṭhati, ya+daittha maṃ pati siyā, yau mama bhāgau, sau dīyatutyatthau| aivaṃ rukkhaṃparityādipi|

10. anunāti

lakkhaṇādīsu dutiyā| rukkhamanu vij‍jautatai, sac‍cakiriya+manu vassi, haitu ca lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, sādhu daivadattau mātara+manu, rukkhaṃ rukkha+manu vij‍jautatai, yadaittha maṃanu siyā|

11. sahatthai

sahatthai anunā yuttamhā dutiyā| pabbata+manu tiṭṭhati, pabbataina saha tyatthau|

12. hīnai

hīnatthai anunā yuttamhā dutiyā| anusāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau bhikkhū, sāriputtatau pañ‍ñāya hīnātyatthau|

13. upaina

hīnatthai upaina yuttamhā dutiyā| upasāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau|

16. kattukaraṇaisu tatiyā

kattari karaṇai ca kārakai tatiyā hauti| jinaina daisitau dhammau, buddhaina jitau mārau, ahinā daṭṭhau narau| yattha  kāraṇakāraṇampi kāraṇavasaina vuc‍cati, tatthāpi tatiyā, cauraihi gāmau daḍḍhau, tiṇaihi bhattaṃ siddhaṃ, saddhaihi kāritā vihārā|

attapadhānau kiriyaṃ, yau nibbattaiti kārakau|

apayuttau payuttau vā, sa kattāti pavuc‍cati||

karaṇai-taṃ pana duvidhaṃ ajjhattikabāhiravasaina, yathā hatthaina kammaṃ karauti, cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāyati| dattaina vihayau lunāti, agginā kuṭiṃ jhāpaiti|

bāhirañ‍ca tathā+jjhattaṃ, karaṇaṃ duvidhaṃ yathā|

vīhiṃ lunāti dattaina, naittaina canda+mikkhatai||

pakatiyā abhirūpau, gauttaina gautamau ic‍cādi bhūdhātussa sambhavā karaṇai tatiyā| tathā sāriputtauti nāmaina vissutau, jātiyā khattiyau buddhau, jātiyā sattavassikau, sippaina naḷakārau sau, aikūnatiṃsau vayasā, aivaṃ samaina dhāvati, visamaina dhāvati, dvidauṇaina dhañ‍ñaṃ kiṇāti|

17. sahatthaina

sahatthaina yaugai tatiyā siyā| tatiyāpi chaṭṭhīva appadhānai aiva bhavati| puttaina sahā+gatau, puttaina saddhiṃ āgatau, vitak‍kaina saha vattati, puttaina saha thūlau, nisīdi bhagavā saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina, sahassaina samaṃ mitā, sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau|

18. lakkhaṇai

lakkhaṇai  vattamānatau tatiyā| bhin‍naina sīsaina paggharantaina lauhitaina paṭīvissakai ujjhāpaisi, ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiyya, tidaṇḍakaina paribbājaka+maddakkhi, akkhinā kāṇau, hatthaina kuṇī, pādaina khañ‍jau, piṭṭhiyā khuj‍jau| taina hi vikalaṅgaina vikalaṅginau vikārau lakkhiyatai|

19. haitumhi

vāsādilakkhaṇakriyāya haitutau tatiyā| an‍naina vasati, vij‍jāya vasati, na jac‍cā vasalau hauti, kammunā vasalau hauti, dānaina bhaugavā, ācāraina kulaṃ, taina pāṇi kāmadadau|

20. pañ‍camī+ṇai vā

iṇai haitumhi pañ‍camī vā| satasmā baddhau, sataina vā|

21. guṇai

paraṅgabhūtai haitumhi pañ‍camī hauti vā| jaḷattā baddhau, jaḷattainavā, pañ‍ñāya muttau, hutvā abhāvatau anic‍cā, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau| bahulaṃvidhānā sattamyatthaihipi, taina samayaina, kālaina dhammasākacchā, puratthimaina dhataraṭṭhau, dakkhiṇaina virūḷhakau, uttaraina kapilavatthu, yaina bhagavā, tainupasaṅkami, sau vau mama+c‍cayaina satthāti| māsaina bhuñ‍jati, aikāhainaiva bārāṇasiṃ pāvisi, navahi māsaihi vihāraṃ kārāpaisi, kahāpaṇaina ūnau, dhanaina vikalau, asinā kalahau, ācāraina nipuṇau, guḷaina missakaṃ, vācāya sakhilau, maṇinā atthau, dhanaina atthau, yaujanaina gacchati ic‍cādi haitumhi karaṇai vā tatiyā| attanāva attānaṃ samman‍natīti pac‍cattai bahulaṃvidhānā kattari tatiyā| aivaṃ tilaihi khaittai vapatīti kammatthai, sumuttā mayaṃ taina mahāsamaṇaināti pañ‍camyatthai ca|

24. catutthī sampadānai

acaitanaṃ sacaitanaṃ vā paṭiggāhakabhāvainā+paikkhitaṃ, taṃ sampadānaṃ, tañ‍ca dā-dānaiti dhātutau byappaina yuttamaiva, tattha catutthī hauti| tañ‍ca dīyamānassa vatthunau anivāraṇa+jjhaisanā+numatitau tividhau, yathā buddhassa pupphaṃ daiti, baudhirukkhassa jalaṃ daiti, yācakassa dhanaṃ daiti, bhikkhūnaṃ dānaṃ daiti|

anirākaraṇā+rādha-na+bbhanuñ‍ñavasaina hi|

sampadānaṃ tidhā vuttaṃ, rukkha+yācaka+bhikkhavau||

ādhāravivakkhāyaṃ sattamīpi, saṅghai gautami daihi, saṅghai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi, yā palālamayaṃ mālaṃ, nārī datvāna caitiyaiti|

25. tādatthyai

tādatthyai catutthī siyā| tadatthassa bhāvai, tādatthyaṃ, tasmiṃ tadatthabhāvai jautanīyaiva catutthī siyā| tadatthassa bhāvauti nimittanimittīsambandhai  chaṭṭhī, tasmā chaṭṭhāpavādau+yaṃ| samaipi nimittanimittīnaṃ sambandhai nimittabhūtayūpatauva catutthī, nau nimittībhūtadārutau| yūpāya dāru, pākāya vajati, buddhassatthāya jīvitaṃ paric‍cajāmi, naiva davāya, na madāya, ūnassa pāripūriyā, atthāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati, laukānukampāya, phāsu vihārāya|

ic‍cāyaṃ kac‍cāyanai upari vakkhamānassa catutthīti sādhitattā idha chaṭṭhīti dīpanatthaṃ vuc‍catai –

kassa sādu naruc‍cati, mā ayasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, khamati saṅghassa, bhatta+massa nacchādaitīti chaṭṭhī sambandhavacanicchāyaṃ, na cai+vaṃ viraudhau siyā, sadisarūpattā, aivaṃvidhaisu ca sambandhassa saddikānumatattā| tathā hi bhāgavuttiyā ‘‘upapadavibhatti chaṭṭhiyā+pavādā’’ti vuttaṃ, saddantarai vihitā vibhatti upapadavibhatti|

kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisīti atthamattai paṭhamā, kassa vā tuyhanti avatvā kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisīti atthamattai paṭhamāvasaina byabhicāradassanā| aiva+mañ‍ñāpi viñ‍ñaiyyā, parataupi yathāgamaṃ|

rañ‍ñau sataṃ dhāraiti rañ‍ñau chattaṃ dhāraitīti sambandhai chaṭṭhīva| silāgha=kathanai, aivaṃ rañ‍ñau silāghatai ic‍cādi, thutiṃ karautītyatthau| hanu=apanayanai, rañ‍ñau hanutai, vañ‍caitītyatthau| upatiṭṭhaiyya sakyaputtānaṃ vaḍḍhakī, upagacchaiyyātyatthau| sapa=ak‍kausai, mayhaṃ sapatai, sac‍caṃ kurutaityatthau| dhara=dhāraṇai, suvaṇṇaṃ tai dhārayatai, iṇaṃ tai dhārayati, assa rañ‍ñau nāgaṃ dhārayāma| piha=icchāyaṃ, daivāpi tassa pihayanti tādinau, taisaṃ pihayanti sambuddhānaṃ satīmataṃ, pihayanti=patthainti| tassa kujjha mahāvīra, yadi+haṃ tassa pakuppaiyyaṃ, dubhayati disānaṃ maighau, yau mittānaṃ na dūbhati, yau appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati| issa=issāyaṃ, titthiyā issayanti samaṇānaṃ| usūya=dausāvikaraṇai, duj‍janā guṇavantānaṃ ussūyanti, kā usuyā vijānataṃ| idha+sidha+rādha+sādha=saṃsiddhiyaṃ, ārādhau mai rañ‍ñau, rañ‍ñau bhāgya+mārajjhati, kyā+haṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi, āyasmatau upālitthairassa upasampadāpaikkhau upatissau| bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ, āsuṇanti buddhassa bhikkhū, tassa bhikkhunau janauanugiṇāti, patigiṇāti, sādhukāradānādinā taṃ ussahatītyatthau| āraucayāmi vau bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau, āmantayāmi tai mahārāja, dhammaṃ tai daisissāmi, daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya, niruttiṃ tai pavakkhāmi, alaṃ mai raj‍jaṃ, alaṃ bhikkhu pattassa, alaṃ mal‍lau mal‍lassa, arahati mal‍lau mal‍lassa, alaṃ tai idha vāsaina, kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaināti sabbattha sambandhai chaṭṭhī|

aivaṃ āyu bhautau hautu, ciraṃ jīvitaṃ, bhaddaṃ, kalyāṇaṃ, atthaṃ, payaujanaṃ, kusalaṃ, anāmayaṃ, hitaṃ, patthaṃ, sukhaṃ, sātaṃ, bhautau hautu, sādhu sammuti mai tassa, puttassā+vikaraiyya guyha+matthaṃ, tassa mai sak‍kau pāturahausi, tassa pahiṇaiyya, bhikkhūnaṃ dūtaṃ pāhaisi, kappati samaṇānaṃ āyaugau, aikassa dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā pahauti, upamaṃ tai karissāmi, añ‍jaliṃtai paggaṇhāmi, tassa phāsu, laukassa+tthau, namau tai purisājañ‍ña, sautthi tassa, samatthau mal‍lau mal‍lassa, tassa hitaṃ, tassa sukhaṃ, svāgataṃ tai mahārājāti sabbattha sambandhai chaṭṭhī|

26. pañ‍camya+vadhismā

padatthāvadhismā pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti|

samaipya+pagamai dvin‍naṃ, pubbarūpā ya+dac‍cutaṃ|

vuc‍catai ta+dapādānaṃ, taṃ calācalatau dvidhā|

yathā+ssā dhāvatā pausau, patau, rukkhāphalanti ca||

tattha calāvadhi dhāvatā assā purisau patati, acalāvadhi pabbatā autaranti vanacārakāti|

tañ‍ca avadhi visayakriyāvisaisassa niddiṭṭhattā niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ, yattha apa apagamanakriyaṃ upāttaṃ=ajjhāhaṭaṃ visayaṃ katvā pavattati, taṃ upāttaṃ| yaṃ kainaci guṇaina uk‍kaṃsiyati, taṃ anumaiyyaṃ| yathā gāmā apainti munayau, nagarā niggatau rājā, pāpā cittaṃ nivārainti| valāhakā vij‍jautatai, kusulatau pacatīti| aittha ca vaḷāhakā nikkhamma, kusulatau apanaitvāti ca pubbakriyā ajjhāharīyati| mathurā pāṭaliputtakaihi abhirūpāti anumīyati| vuttañhi –

niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ kiñ‍ci, upāttavisayaṃ tathā|

anumaiyyavisayañ‍caiti, tidhā+hu avadhiṃ budhāti||

bhayahaitumhi-caurā  bhayaṃ jāyati, taṇhāya jāyati bhayaṃ, pāpatau uttasati, natthi saukau kutau bhayaṃ| akkhātari-upajjhāyā sikkhaṃ gaṇhāti, ācariyamhā adhītau suṇāti vā| buddhasmā parājainti añ‍ñatitthiyā, parājitā bhavantītyatthau| himavatā pabhavati gaṅgā, aciravatiyā pabhavanti kun‍nadiyau| urasmā jātau puttau, kammatau jātaṃ indriyaṃ, upajjhāyā antaradhāyati sissau, mātāpitūhi antaradhārayati puttau, nilīyatītyatthau| dūratthayaugaikīvadūrau itau naḷakāragāmau, tatau havai dūrataraṃ vadanti, gāmatau nātidūrai, ārakā tai maughapurisā imasmā dhammavinayā, ārakā taihi bhagavā dūratauva namassanti, addasa dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| antikatthayaugai-antikaṃ gāmā, āsan‍naṃ gāmā, samīpaṃ gāmā| parimāṇai-itau mathurāya catūsu yaujanaisu saṃkassaṃ, rājagahatau pañ‍cacattālīsayaujanamatthakai sāvatthi| kālaparimāṇai-itau aikanavutikappamatthakai, itau vassasatasahassassa ac‍cayaina buddhau laukai uppaj‍jissati| pāsādā saṃkamaiyya, pāsādaṃ abhiruhitvā saṃkamaiyyāti atthau, tathā hatthikkhandhā saṃkamaiyya, abhidhammā pucchanti, abhidhammaṃ sutvāti atthau, āsanā vuṭṭhahaiyya| disatthavācīhi yaugai-itau sā purimā disā, itau sā dakkhiṇā disā, avīcitau yāva bhavaggaṃ, uddhaṃ pādatalā, adhau kaisamatthakā| vibhajanai-yatau paṇītatarau vā visiṭṭhatarau vā natthi, attadantau tatau varaṃ, kiñ‍cāpi dānatau sīlaṃ varaṃ, tatau mayā bahutaraṃ sutaṃ, sīlamaiva sutā saiyyau| āratippayaugai-ārati virati pāpā, pāṇātipātā  vairamaṇi, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau, appaṭiviratā musāvādā| suddhatthayaugai-laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi suddhau, mātutau ca pitutau ca suddhau anupak‍kuṭṭhau| pamaucanatthayaugai-parimuttau dukkhasmāti vadāmi, muttau+smi mārabandhanā, na tai muc‍canti mac‍cunā, muttau+haṃ+sabbapāsaihi| vivaicanai-vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi| pamāṇatthai-āyāmatau ca vitthāratau ca yaujanaṃ, gambhīratau ca puthulatau ca yaujanaṃ candabhāgāya parimāṇaṃ, parikkhaipatau navayaujanasataparimāṇau majjhimadaisau| pubbādiyaugai-pubbaiva mai bhikkhavai sambaudhā, itau pubbai nāhausi, tatau paraṃ pac‍cantimā janapadā, tatau aparaina samayaina| pañhai-kutau+si tvaṃ, kutau bhavaṃ, pāṭaliputtatau| kicchā laddhanti guṇai pañ‍camī, kicchaina mai adhigatanti haitumhi karaṇai vā tatiyā, aivaṃ thaukā muttau, thaukaina muttauti|

kathaṃ ‘‘thaukaṃ calatī’’ti, kriyāvisaisanai kammani dutiyā, thaukaṃ calanaṃ karautītyatthau, thaukanti calanakriyāya visaisanattā kriyāvisaisanaṃ|

kriyāvisaisanaṃ nāma, kammattai+kattasaṇṭhitā|

nyāyasiddhaṃ yatau tasmā, tadatthaṃ na visuṃ vidhi||

nyāyasiddhaṃva=calananti yasmā bhāvai anau, tasmā bhāvassai+kattā aikavacananti ñāyā aikattañ‍ca, bhāvai anattā napuṃsakattañ‍ca, karautikriyāya sambandhaina kammattañ‍ca sijjhatīti| kara+bhūdhātavau ca –

kāriyarūpābhidhātvatthā, sabbai sattāya yuj‍jarai|

tatau kriyā ca bhāvau ca, sāmañ‍ñaṃ taisu gamyatai –

ti vuttattā yuj‍janti|

thaukatthai-thaukā muc‍cati| sabbattha savisayai pañ‍camī| aittha ‘‘vivakkhā laukikā sā ca, na sak‍kā anivattituṃ’’ti vuttattā aniṭṭhappasaṅgau na siyā| mariyādāyaṃ-āpabbatā khaittaṃ| abhividhimhi-ābrahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchati, pabbataṃ vaj‍jaitvā, brahmalaukamhi byāpaitvāti ca atthau| aittha vaj‍jamānasīmā mariyādā, gayhamānasīmā abhividhi|

27. apaparīhi vaj‍janai

vaj‍janai vattamānaihī apaparīhi yaugai pañ‍camī hauti| apasālāya āyanti vāṇijā, parisālāya āyanti vāṇijā, sālaṃ vaj‍jaitvāti atthau|

28. paṭinidhipatidānaisu patinā

paṭinidhimhi patidānai ca vattamānaina patinā yaugai nāmasmā pañ‍camī hauti| buddhasmā pati sāriputtau, ghata+massa tailasmā pati dadāti|

29. ritai dutiyā ca

ritaisaddayaugai nāmasmā dutiyā hauti pañ‍camī ca| ritai saddhammā, ritai saddhammaṃ|

30. vinā+ñ‍ñatra tatiyā ca

vinā+ñ‍ñatrayaugai  nāmasmā tatiyā dutiyā pañ‍camī ca| vinā vātaina, vinā vātaṃ, vinā vātasmā| añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina, añ‍ñatra dhammaṃ, añ‍ñatra dhammā|

31. puthanānāhi

aitaihi yaugai tatiyā hauti pañ‍camī ca| bhin‍nayaugakaraṇaṃ dutiyānivattanatthaṃ| puthagaiva janaina, puthagaiva janasmā, janaina nānā, janasmā nānā|

39. chaṭṭhī sambandhai

sambandhai chaṭṭhī hauti| rañ‍ñau purisauti vuttai yasmā rājā dadāti, purisau gaṇhāti, tasmā rājapurisauti viñ‍ñāyati| aivamaiva yau yassa āyattau saivakādibhāvaina bhaṇḍabhāvaina vā samīpa+samūhā+vayava+vikāra+kāriya+avatthā+jāti+guṇa+kriyādivasaina vā, ṭhānīvasaina vā, āgamīvasaina vā, sau tividhaupi atthau sambandhau nāma| vuttañhi –

kriyākārakasañ‍jātau, assai+daṃbhāvahaitukau|

sambandhau nāma sau atthau, tattha chaṭṭhī vidhīyatai||

pāratantyañhi sambandhau, tattha chaṭṭhī bhavai titau|

upādhi+ṭhānyā+gamitau, na visaissāditau titauti||

upādhisaṅkhātavisaisanatau tāva-rañ‍ñau purisau| aittha ca brāhmaṇādisāmitau nivattaitīti rājā visaisanaṃ, purisau taina  visaisiyatīti visaissau| bhaṇḍasambandhatau-pahutaṃ mai dhanaṃ sak‍ka, aikassa paṭivīsau, bhikkhussa pattacīvaraṃ| samīpatau-ambavanassa avidūrai, nibbānassaiva santikai| samūhai-suvaṇṇassa rāsi, bhikkhūnaṃ samūhau| avayavai-manussassaiva tai sīsaṃ, rukkhassa sākhā| vikārai-suvaṇṇassa vikati, bhaṭṭhadhañ‍ñānaṃ sattu| kāriyai-yavassa aṅkurau, maighassa saddau, puttāpi tassa bahavau, kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau| avatthāyaṃ-khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau, jarā, bhaidau vā| jātiyaṃ-manussassa bhāvau, manussānaṃ jāti| guṇai-suvaṇṇassa vaṇṇau, vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassa buddhassa guṇaghausau, pupphānaṃ gandhau, phalānaṃ rasau, cittassa phusanā, sippikānaṃ sataṃ natthi, tilānaṃ muṭṭhi, taisaṃ samāyaugau, sandhinau vimaukkhau, tathāgatassa pañ‍ñāpāramiṃ ārabbha, sukhaṃ tai, dukkhaṃ tai, caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi, pañ‍ñāya paṭubhāvau, rūpassa lahutā, mudutā vā, upacayau vā| kriyāsambandhai-pādassa ukkhaipanaṃ, avakkhaipanaṃ vā, hatthassa samiñ‍janaṃ, dānaṃ, pasāraṇaṃ, dhātūnaṃ gamanaṃ, ṭhānaṃ, nisaj‍jā, sayanaṃ vā, tathāgatassa nāmagauttādi, tassa kāraṇaṃ, tassa mātāpitarau, tassa puratau pāturahausi, nagarassa dakkhiṇatau, vassānaṃ tatiyai māsai, na tassa upamā, kuvairassa bali ic‍cādi| api ca –

gāvassa jāti, dhavalau, gati, siṅgaṃ, nāmanti+dha|

dabbassāpi ca jātyādi, visaissā haunti kāmatau||

gauttañ‍ca  sabalaudissa, pāka+man‍nassa suk‍katā|

paṭassa, siṅgaṃmaiṇḍassa, nā+ñ‍ñaisaṃti visaissatai||

ṭhānitau-yuvaṇṇāna+maiau luttā| āgamitau-suña sassa| sāmiyaugai-daivāna+mindau, migānaṃ rājā| rujādiyaugai-daivadattassa rujati, tassa raugau uppaj‍ji, mahāsaināpatīnaṃ ujjhāpaitabbaṃ, rajakassa vatthaṃ dadāti, musāvādassa auttappaṃ ic‍cādi| yajassa yaugai-pupphassa buddhassa yajati, pupphainātyatthau| aivaṃ ghatassa aggiṃ juhati| suhitatthai-pattaṃ audanassa pūraitvā, pūraṃ nānāpakārassa asucinau, pūraṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇassa, pūrati bālau pāpassa| kitakappayaugai-bahulaṃvidhānā chaṭṭhī, rañ‍ñau sammatau, pūjitau, sak‍katau, apacitau, mānitau vā, amataṃ taisaṃ bhikkhavai aparibhuttaṃ, yaisaṃ kāyagatā sati aparibhuttā, sādhusammatau bahujanassa, suppaṭividdhā buddhānaṃ dhammadhātu, dhammassa guttau maidhāvī| kammatthai-tassa bhavanti vattārau, sahasā kammassa kattārau, amatassa dātā, bhin‍nānaṃ sandhātā, sahitānaṃ anuppadātā, baudhaitā pajāya, kammassa kārakau natthi, vipākassa ca vaidakau, avisaṃvādakau laukassa, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau| sarati+cchāyaugai-mātu sarati, na taisaṃ kauci sarati, sattānaṃ kammapac‍cayā, puttassa icchati| karautissa (yaugai) – udakassa patikurutai, kaṇḍassa patikurutai, abhisaṅkharautītyatthau| pañ‍camiyatthai parihānibhayatthayaugaipi chaṭṭhī, assavanatāya dhammassa pariyāyanti, kin‍nu khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, sabbai tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbai bhāyanti  mac‍cunau, bhītau catun‍naṃ āsīvisānaṃ ghauravisānaṃ ic‍cādi|

40. tulyatthaina vā tatiyā

tulyatthaina yaugai vā chaṭṭhī hauti tatiyā ca| pitu tulyau, pitarā vā, mātu tulyau, mātarā vā|

32. sattamyā+dhārai

kattukammaṭṭhānaṃ nisaj‍ja+pacanādikriyānaṃ yau ādhārau, taṃ ādhārakārakaṃ nāma| kathaṃ kaṭai nisīdati daivadattau, thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati, daivadatta+taṇḍulānaṃ kattu+kammānaṃ dhāraṇatau tadaṭṭhaāsana+pacanakriyaṃ kaṭa+thāliyau dhārainti nāma, tasmiṃ sattamīvibhatti hauti|

sau byāpikau, aupasilaisikau, vaisayikau, sāmīpikauti catubbidhau| tattha yau ādhaiyyassa nissaisādhārabhūtau, sau byāpikau, yathā tilaisu tailaṃ atthi, khīraisu jalaṃ, dadhimhi sappi| pac‍caikasiddhānaṃ bhāvānaṃ yaṃ ādhaiyyabhāvaina upasilaisanaṃ al‍līyanaṃ atthi, sau aupasilaisikau| yathā āsanai nisin‍nau saṅghau, thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati, ghaṭai udaka+matthi, dūrai ṭhitau, samīpai ṭhitau| yattha samīpai samīpīvauhāraṃ katvā tadāyattatādīpanatthaṃ ādhārabhāvau upacarīyati, taṃ sāmīpikaṃ, yathā gaṅgāyaṃ ghausau, gaṅgāya samīpai vajautyatthau| sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai| yattha añ‍ñathābhāvavasaina daisantarāvacchaidavasaina vā ādhāraparikappau, sau vaisayitau| yathā ākāsai sakuṇā caranti, bhūmiyaṃ manussā, jalai macchā, pāsādaisu patitau, pāpasmiṃ ramatī manau, pasan‍nau buddhasāsanai, pañ‍ñāya sādhu, vinayai nipuṇau, mātari sādhu, pitari nipuṇau|

kiriyā kattu+kammaṭṭhā, ādhārīyati yaina sau|

ādhārau catudhā vuttau, byāpakādippabhaidatau||

byāpakau tilakhīrādi, kaṭau aupasilaisikau|

sāmīpikau tu gaṅgādi, ākāsau visayau matau||

33. nimittai

nimittatthai sattamī hauti| ajinamhi hañ‍ñatai dīpi, kuñ‍jarau dantaisu hañ‍ñatai, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyaṃ| sabbattha nimityatthau|

34. yabbhāvau bhāvalakkhaṇaṃ

yassa bhāvau bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, tatau sattamī| kālai gāvīsu duyhamānāsugatau, duddhāsu āgatau| aitthagamanakriyāya dauhanakriyācihanaṃ, āgamanakriyāya duddhākriyācihanaṃ, aiva+muparipi| pubbaṇhasamayai gatau, sāyanhasamayai āgatau, jāyamānai khau sāriputta baudhisattai ayaṃ dasasahassī laukadhātu sampakampi|

pāsāṇā sakkharā caiva, kaṭhalā khāṇukaṇṭakā|

sabbai maggā vivaj‍janti, gacchantai laukanāyakai||

imasmiṃ  sati idaṃ hauti ic‍cādi| ‘‘akālai vassatī tassa, kālai tassa na vassatī’’ti visayasattamī|

35. chaṭṭhī cā+nādarai

yassa bhāvau bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, tatau chaṭṭhī hauti sattamī cā+nādarai gamyamānai| akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ rudantānaṃ pabbaji, mātāpitūsu rudantaisu vā| ‘‘ākauṭayantau sau naiti, sivirājassa paikkhatai’’, ‘‘mac‍cu ādāya gacchati, paikkhamānai mahājanai’’|

gun‍naṃ sāmīti sambandhai chaṭṭhī, gausu sāmīti visayasattamī| aivaṃ gun‍namissarau, gausvi+ssarau, gun‍naṃ adhipati, gausu adhipati, gun‍naṃ dāyādau, gausu dāyādau, gun‍naṃ sakkhī, gausu sakkhī, gun‍naṃ patibhū, gausu patibhū, gun‍naṃ pasutau, gausu pasutau, kusalā nac‍cagītassa, kusalā nac‍cagītai, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇassa, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇai| tathā ādhāravacanicchāyaṃ sattamī, bhikkhūsu abhivādainti, muddhani cumbitvā, bāhāsu gahaitvā, hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti, kadalīsu gajai rakkhanti, ñāṇasmiṃ pasan‍nau, ñāṇasmiṃ ussuk‍kauti visayasattamī|

36. yatau niddhāraṇaṃ

jātiguṇakriyāhi samudāyatai+kadaisassa puthak‍karaṇaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ, yatau taṃ karīyati, tatau chaṭṭhīsattamiyau haunti| sālayau sūkadhañ‍ñānaṃ pathyatamā, sūkadhañ‍ñaisu sālayau pathyatamā| kaṇhā gāvīnaṃ sampan‍nakhīratamā, kaṇhā gāvīsu  sampan‍nakhīratamā| gacchataṃ dhāvantau sīghatamā, gacchantaisu dhāvantau sīghatamā|

14. sattamyā+dhikyai

ādhikyai atthai upaina yuttamhā sattamī hauti| upakhāriyaṃ dauṇau, khāriyā dauṇau adhikautyatthau| tathā upanikkhai kahāpaṇaṃ|

15. sāmitthai+dhinā

sāmibhāvatthai adhinā yuttamhā sattamī hauti| adhibrahmadattai pañ‍cālā, adhipañ‍cālaisu brahmadattau, brahmadattissarā pañ‍cālāti atthau| adhidaivaisu buddhau, sammutidaivādīhi buddhau adhikautyatthau|

aittha ca yathāvuttaisu atthaisu ayaṃ bhaidau –

kārakaṃ sādhakaṃ nibbattakaṃ kriyānimittanti atthatau aikamaiva, tañ‍ca daṇḍau, dhavalau, pacanaṃ, cittau, gauti dabba+guṇa+kriyā+nāma+jātibhaidaina pañ‍cavidhampi kamma+kattādivasaina chabbidhaṃ, taṃ sattikārakaṃ, tadādhārabhūtadabbādayau tatraṭṭhakārakaṃ, na mukhyatau| mukhyatau cai haunti, taisaṃ sattidabbānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñabyāvaṭarūpattā yau ādhārau, sau ādhārauyaiva, kismiñ‍cikālai karaṇaṃ vā katturūpabhūtau vā na hauti, tasmā thāli pacati, thāliyā pacati, thāliyaṃ pacatītyādinā aikassa vatthunau kattu+karaṇā+dhārabhaidau na siyā| sattipakkhai pana dabbādīnaṃ anaikasattiyā ādhārattā sattiyā dabbādayau vivakkhitā taṃ taṃ kārakaṃ hautīti dabbādīnaṃ abhaidaipi kārakabhaidau yuj‍jatai| vuttañhi –

vicittakattuādīhi, saṃyaugā aikavatthunau|

nānāttaṃ yuj‍jatai nāṭya-bhaidaina naṭakassi+vāti||

thāli vatthutau aikā caidapi sattikārakabhūtakattu+karaṇā+dhārādīnaṃ bhaidaina bhaida+mupayāti, kimiva rāma+rāvaṇādivaisadhārīnaṭakau taisaṃ nāmavasaina rāmau, rāvaṇauti bhaida+mupayāti, ta+mivāti adhippāyau|

puna+rapi –

yathai+kaupi paṭau sutta, pītādiguṇasaṃyutau|

suk‍kau paṭauti pītauti, bhaidaṃ yātyai+vamaiva+yaṃ||

aittha ca sattibhūtakattādayau mukhyakārakaṃ, taṃyaugaina dabbabhūtathālī guṇakārakaṃ, tai ca kathaṃ kriyāya kārakā haunti| kattā attanā patiṭṭhitāya hasati+nac‍catic‍cādikriyāya nimittaṃ hauti| kammañ‍ca attanā patiṭṭhitakriyāya tadatthabhūtaindhanādīni pavattaintaṃ ‘‘audanaṃ pacatī’’tyādau nimittaṃ| ‘kaṭṭhaihi pac‍catī’tyādau kaṭṭhāni jalanakriyāya sādhaitabbapākassa aṅgabhāvaina nimittaṃ| ‘pharasunā chindatī’tyādau pharasu ca kaṭṭhānaṃ dvidhāpavattiyā nimittaṃ| kammañ‍ca kriyāya sambandhīyamānabyāpyai sati nimittabhāvaina kriyāya nimittaṃ| tathā sampadānā+vadhi+ādhārānaṃ ‘gāvau dadāti’ ‘gāmasmā apanayati’ ‘thāliyaṃ pacatī’tyādīsu gaudānā+panayana+pacanakriyānaṃ nimittattā kriyāya nimittāni haunti| taisaṃ yathāsakaṃ kriyāya pavattakau kattā, tasmā sa aiva padhānau kattāti vauhāraṃ  labhati, añ‍ñaisaṃ karaṇādīnaṃ kattubhāvai satipi appadhānattā taṃ na labhati|

caudakaina vuttañhi –

nanu sāmagyamīnāyaṃ, kriyāsiddhi kathaṃ vada|

aikassa kattunau aiva, sabbaisaṃ kattutaṃ vinā||

sabbaisaṃ karaṇādīnaṃ, kattutāya viyaugatau|

karaṇādīnaṃ abhāvattā, kārakaṃ na hi chabbidhanti||

vuc‍catai –

yadyapya+tthi hi kattuttaṃ, sabhāvā karaṇādisu|

kriyāsiddhyā tathāpyai+ta+mappadhānaṃ paraṅgatau||

aitaṃ sabhāvatau upalabbhamānaṃ karaṇādīsu kattuttaṃ paraisaṃ karaṇādīnaṃ jalana+dhāraṇādikriyāya aṅgaṃ upāyati appadhānanti adhippāyau|

kārakaṃ chabbidhaṃ sañ‍ñā-vasā chabbīsatividhaṃ|

pabhaidā sattadhā kammaṃ, kattā pañ‍cavidhau bhavai||

karaṇaṃ duvidhaṃ hauti, sampadānaṃ tidhā mataṃ|

apādānaṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ, ādhārau tu catubbidhau||

vibhattiyau pana –

pac‍catta+mupayaugañ‍ca, karaṇaṃ sampadāniyaṃ|

nissak‍kaṃ sāmivacanaṃ, bhumma+mālapana+ṭṭhamaṃti||

iti payaugasiddhiyaṃ kārakakaṇḍau tatiyau|

4. samāsakaṇḍa

atha  nāmānamaiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandhīnaṃ samāsauti nāmanissitattā, sayañ‍ca nāmikattā nāmānantaraṃ samāsau vuc‍catai|

1. syādi syādinai+katthanti

ida+madhikataṃ vaiditabbaṃ| pubbai vuttavidhiggahaṇañāyaina syādīti tadantassa gahaṇaṃ| sau ca bhin‍natthānaṃ nāmāna+maikatthībhāvau samāsauti vuc‍catai|

2. asaṅkhyaṃ vibhatti+sampatti+samīpa+sākalyā+bhāva+yathā+ pacchā+yugapadatthai

pubbassa+tthaparaṃ yassa, añ‍ñatthaparamañ‍ca yaṃ|

napuṃsakaṃ bhavai yañ‍ca, ta+dāsaṃkhya+mihai+ssatai||

satthantarai pasiddhaṃ yaṃ, abyayībhāvanāmatau|

upakumbhaṃ tiṭṭhagu ca, pātamaighaṃti taṃ yathā||

asaṅkhyaṃ syādyantaṃ vibhatyādīna+matthai vattamānaṃ syādyantaina sahai+katthaṃ bhavati| ‘‘aviggahau nic‍casamāsau, padantaraviggahau cai’’ti padantaraviggahau| itthīsu tathā pavattāti viggayha vibhatyatthai samāsai katai –

2,119. aikatthatāyaṃ

aikau atthau yassa pakatipac‍cayādisamudāyassa sau aikatthau| tassa bhāvau pavattinimittaṃ aikatthatā, īyādi+ṇādi+samāsavidhānaṃ, tasmiṃ sati syādilaupau hauti|

9. taṃ napuṃsakanti

napuṃsakaliṅgai  ca ‘‘pubbasmā+māditau’’ti sabbavibhattīnaṃ laupai ca katai adhitthi tiṭṭhati, bhau adhitthi, adhitthi passa, adhitthi kataṃ, adhitti carati, adhitthi daihi, adhitthi apaihi, adhitthi āyattaṃ, adhitthi patiṭṭhitaṃ| aivaṃ adhikumāri, antabhūtassa appadhānassa ghapassa ‘‘ghapassa+ntassā+ppadhānassā’’ti passa rassau|

saha=sampan‍naṃ brahmaṃ sabrahmaṃ| vuttanayaina aittha ca upari ca samāsādayau haunti, aittha ‘‘akālai satatthai’’ti sahassa sādaisau| syādimhi katai ‘‘pubbasmā+māditau’’ti syādīnaṃ laupai ca sampattai ‘‘nā+tau+mapañ‍camiyā’’ti apañ‍camiyā paṭisaidhau ca akārantaasaṃkhyasamāsatau parāsaṃ sabbavibhattīnaṃ aṃādaisau ca hauti| aiva+muparipi|

bhikkhānaṃ samiddhi subhikkhaṃ, aittha ghassa rassau| subhikkhaṃ tiṭṭhati bhau subhikkhaṃ, subhikkhaṃ passa| nā+smiṃsu ‘‘vā tatiyā sattamīnaṃ’’ti vikappaina aṃ, subhikkhaṃ subhikkhaina vā kataṃ, subhikkhaṃ subhikkhaina vā carati, subhikkhaṃ daihi| pañ‍camiyaṃ a+mabbhāvā subhikkhā apagaccha, subhikkha+māyattaṃ, subhikkhaṃ subhikkhai vā patiṭṭhitaṃ|

samīpatthai-kumbhassa samīpaṃ upakumbhaṃ, aivaṃ upanagaraṃ, bhau upakumbhaṃ ic‍cādi|

sākalyai-tiṇampi asaisaitvāti satiṇaṃ, tiṇampi asaisaitvā ajjhauharaṇīya+majjhauharatītyatthau| saisaṃ sabrahmasamaṃ| aggimpi asaisaitvāti sāggi, aggiganthaṃpi asaisaitvā adhītaityatthau| vāraggahaṇaṃ adhitthisamaṃ|

abhāvau sambandhībhaidā bahuvidhau, tatra iddhābhāvaivigatā iddhi=vibhūti saddikānanti dussaddikaṃ| atthābhāvaimakkhikānaṃ abhāvau nimmakkhikaṃ, niddarathaṃ, nimmasakaṃ| atik‍kamābhāvai-atigatāni tiṇāni nittiṇaṃ| upabhaugasambandhīvattamānakālassa abhāvai-atigataṃ lahupāvuraṇaṃ atilahupāvuraṇaṃ, lahupāvuraṇassa nā+yaṃ upabhaugatā lauti atthau|

yathātthau+naikavidhau, tatra yauggatāyaṃ-yauggaṃ rūpa+manurūpaṃ| vicchāyaṃ-addhamāsaṃ addhamāsaṃ anu anvaddhamāsaṃ, aivaṃ pac‍cattaṃ| atthānativattiyaṃ-sattiṃ anatik‍kamma yathāsatti| aivaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ, yathābalaṃ| bahulādhikārā yā yā parisā yathāparisā| sadisatthai-kikhiyā kaṇhavicchitadhainuyā sadisau sakikhi, sahassa sādaisau| ānupubbiyaṃ-jaiṭṭhānuk‍kamaina anujaiṭṭhaṃ|

pacchāatthai-rathassa pacchā anurathaṃ|

yugapadatthai-cak‍kaina saha=aikakālaṃ sacak‍kaṃ, cak‍kaina aikakkhaṇai nidhaitīti atthau|

3. yathā na tulyai

yathāsaddau tulyatthai vattamānau syādyantaina sahai+katthau na bhavati| yathā daivadattau, tathā yañ‍ñadattau| aittha upamānabhūtau yathāsaddau ‘tathā yañ‍ñadattau’ti upamaiyya+mapaikkhati, tasmā ‘‘sāpaikkha+masamatthaṃ bhavatī’’ti ñāyā asamāsai  ‘‘na tulyai’’ti paṭisaidhau kimattha+miti cai| yasmā ‘‘yathā daivadattau’’ti samudāyamaiva upamānaṃ bhavati, na visuṃ yathāsaddau, tasmā samudāyassaiva upamaiyyasāpaikkhattai na asāmatthiyatā na visuṃ yathāsaddassa, tasmā sādissai pattasamāsassa paṭisaidhattha+midaṃ| tulyatthaiti vattabbai tulyaṃ vinā tulyatā natthīti tulyatthaiti avatvā tulyaiti vuttaṃ|

yathākathañ‍ci sādissaṃ, ñāyatai yattha sambhavaṃ|

upamā nāma sā tassā, papañ‍cau bahudhā bhavai||

4. yāvā+vadhāraṇai

yāvasaddau avadhāraṇai vattamānau syādyantaina sahai+katthau bhavati| avadhāraṇaṃ=aittakatāparicchaidau| yāvantāni amattāni=bhājanāni yāvāmattaṃ, iminā samāsai katai saisaṃ pubbasamaṃ| jīvassa yattakau paricchaidau yāvajīvaṃ| yāvatāyukaṃ, ‘‘sakatthai’’ti kapac‍cayau| yattakaina atthau yāvadatthaṃ|

5. payyapā bahi tirau purai pacchā vā pañ‍camyā

pariādayau pañ‍camyantaina sahai+katthā haunti vā| pari pabbatā paripabbataṃ| vāssa vākyavikappatthattā pari pabbatā ic‍cādayaupi haunti| apa pabbatā apapabbataṃ, ā pāṭaliputtā āpāṭaliputtaṃ, bahi gāmā bahigāmaṃ, tirau pabbatā tiraupabbataṃ, purai bhattā puraibhattaṃ, pacchā bhattā pacchābhattaṃ, sabbaṃ upakumbhasamaṃ, iminā samāsau visaisau| aiva+muparisuttaipi|

vātya+dhikārau  –

6. samīpā+yāmaisva+nu

‘‘asaṃkhya’’mic‍cādinā nic‍casamāsassa vikappatthaṃ samīpaggahaṇaṃ| anusaddau sāmīpyai āyāmai ca vattamānau syādyantaina sahai+katthau vā hauti| aittha samīpaggahaṇassa bhāvappadhānattā sāmīpyamaiva gamyataiti sāmīpyaiti vuttaṃ| vanassa anu=samīpaṃ anuvanaṃ, gaṅgāya anu=āyāmau anugaṅgaṃ, gaṅgāya anu vā bārāṇasī|

7. tiṭṭhagvādīni

tiṭṭhaguādīni añ‍ñatthaina siddhāpi asmiṃ asaṃkhyasamāsai nipātiyanti| tiṭṭhanti gāvau yasmiṃ kālaiti tiṭṭhantasadda+gausaddaihi paṭhamāyaumhi katai ‘‘vā+naikañ‍ñatthai’’ti samāsau, iminā nipātanā laupai napuṃsakatthai ca katai simhi gaussa ‘‘gaussu’’ti ukārai ‘‘pubbasmā+māditau’’ti vibhattilaupau| aivaṃ vahantī gāvau yasmiṃ kālaiti vahaggu ic‍cādi| vailāppakāsakapātauādīnampi aitthaiva saṅgahau, pātau nahānanti sattamīamādisamāsai pātanahānaṃ, aivaṃ sāyanahānaṃ, pātakālaṃ, sāyakālaṃ, pātamaighaṃ, sāyamaighaṃ, pātamaggaṃ, sāyamaggaṃ, aittha ‘‘aiauna+ma vaṇṇai’’ti akārau, niggahītassa laupau ca hauti|

8. aurai+pari+paṭi+pārai+majjhai+haiṭṭhu+ddhā+dhau+ntau vā chaṭṭhiyā|

aurādayau saddā chaṭṭhiyantaina sahai+katthā vā haunti| auraṃ gaṅgāya auraigaṅgā, upari sikharassa uparisikharaṃ, paṭi=mukhaṃ sautassa  paṭisautaṃ, pāraṃ yamunāya pāraiyamunaṃ, majjhaṃ gaṅgāya majjhaigaṅgaṃ, haiṭṭhā pāsādassa haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ, uddhaṃ gaṅgāya uddhagaṅgaṃ, adhau gaṅgāya adhaugaṅgaṃ, antau pāsādassa antaupāsādaṃ| iminā nipātanāva niggahītalaupai ca aikārai ca katai auraic‍cādi hauti|

10. a+mādi

a+mādisyādyantaṃ syādyantaina saha bahula+maikattaṃ hauti|

uttarassa padassa+tthau, padhānaṃ liṅga+massa ca|

dutiyantādipadaikatthau, bahudhā taṃ vibhaj‍jatai||

paraisa+missatai tañ‍ca, bhiyyau tappurisā+khyayā|

taṃ yathā+tra rājāpac‍caṃ, katthacīti+mitīdisaṃ||

a+mādyantānaṃ kārakānaṃ akārakānañ‍ca samāsau katthacimaiva vā hauti| tañ‍ca bahulaṃvidhānaināti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tattha dutiyātappurisau amādi gata+nissitā+tītā+tik‍kanta+pattā+pan‍nādīhi bhavati| saraṇaṃ gatauti samāsai katai ‘‘aikatthatāyaṃ’’ti vibhattilaupādi upari sabbattha pubbasamaṃ| saraṇagatau, saraṇagatā| saraṇagatā, saraṇagatāyau| saraṇagataṃ kulaṃ, saraṇagatāni kulāni ic‍cādi| arañ‍ñagatau, bhūmigatau| dhammaṃ nissitau dhammanissitau, atthanissitau| bhavaṃ atītau bhavātītau, kālātītau| pamāṇaṃ atik‍kantaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ, laukātik‍kantaṃ| sukhaṃ pattau sukhappattau, dukkhappattau| sautaṃ āpan‍nau sautāpan‍nau, niraudhasamāpan‍nau, maggappaṭipan‍nau| rathaṃ āruḷhau rathāruḷhau| sabbarattiṃ saubhaṇau sabbarattisaubhaṇau, muhuttasukhaṃ| akārakānaṃ samāsau ac‍cantasaṃyaugai| vuttiyaivau+papadasamāsai, tassa nic‍cattā| yathā kammaṃ karautīti kammakārau, kumbhakārau, atthaṃ kāmaitīti atthakāmau, dhammakāmau, dhammaṃ dhāraitīti dhammadharau, vinayadharau| sānaṃ pacatīti sapākau, tantaṃ vāyatīti tantavāyau, varaṃ āharatīti varāharau| nta+māna+ktavantaihi vākyamaiva| dhammaṃ suṇantau, dhammaṃ suṇamānau, audanaṃ bhuttavā|

tatiyātappurisau kitaka+pubba+sadisa+samau+nattha+kalaha+nipuṇa+missa+sakhilādīhi| buddhaina bhāsitau buddhabhāsitau dhammau, aivaṃ jinadaisitau| satthārā vaṇṇitau satthuvaṇṇitau| viñ‍ñūhi garahitau viñ‍ñugarahitau, viñ‍ñuppasatthau, issarakataṃ, sayaṃ kataṃ, sukaihi āhaṭaṃ sukāhaṭaṃ, rañ‍ñā hatau rājahatau, rājapīḷitau| agginā daḍḍhau aggidaḍḍhau, sappaina daṭṭhau sappadaṭṭhau, sal‍laihi viddhau sal‍laviddhau, icchāya pakatau icchāpakatau, sīlasampan‍nau| aivaṃ sukhasahagataṃ, ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, mittasaṃsaggau, piyavippayaugau, jātithaddhau, guṇahīnau, guṇavuddhau, catuvaggakaraṇīyaṃ, catuvaggādikattabbaṃ| kākaihi paiyyā kākapaiyyā, nadī| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva, urasā gacchatīti uragau, pādaina pivatīti pādapau| k‍vaci vākyamaiva, pharasunā chin‍navā, kākaihi pātabbā, dassanaina pahātabbā| pubbādiyaugai-māsaina pubbau māsapubbau| aivaṃ mātusadisau, mātusamau| aikūnavīsati, sīlavikalau, asikalahau, vācānipuṇau, yāvakālikasammissaṃ, vācāsakhilau| satthārā sadisau satthusadisau, satthukappau, puñ‍ñaina atthikau puñ‍ñatthikau, guṇādhikau| dadhinā upasittaṃ bhaujanaṃ dadhibhaujanaṃ, guḷaina saṃsaṭṭhau audanau guḷaudanau| kārakasambandhau kriyāya katau, upasittādikriyānaṃ apañ‍ñāyanaipi vuttiyaivau+pasittādikriyāna+mākhyāpanatau natthā+yuttatthatā| aivaṃ khīraudanau| assaina yuttau rathau assarathau, maggacittaṃ, jambuyā pañ‍ñātau lakkhitau dīpau jambudīpau, aikaina adhikā dasa aikādasa, jātiyā andhau jac‍candhau, pakatiyā maidhāvī pakatimaidhāvī ic‍cādi|

catutthītappurisau tadattha+attha+hita+daiyyādīhi| tadatthai-kathinassa dussaṃ kathinadussaṃ, kathinacīvarassāti atthau| aivaṃ cīvaradussaṃ, cīvaramūlaṃ, yāguyā atthāya taṇḍulā yāgutaṇḍulā, bhattataṇḍulā, saṅghassa atthāya bhattaṃ saṅghabhattaṃ, āgantukabhattaṃ, aivaṃ gamikabhattaṃ, pāsādāya dabbaṃ pāsādadabbaṃ| aittha cā+yaṃ nic‍casamāsau, tassa tiliṅgatā ca-bhikkhusaṅghassa atthau vihārau bhikkhusaṅghatthau vihārau, bhikkhusaṅghatthā yāgu, bhikkhusaṅghatthaṃ cīvaraṃ| yassa atthau yadatthau, yadatthā, yadatthaṃ| aivaṃ tadatthau, tadatthā, tadatthaṃ| tathā laukahitau| buddhassa daiyyaṃ buddhadaiyyaṃ pupphaṃ| saṅghadaiyyaṃ cīvaraṃ| idha na hauti ‘‘saṅghassa dātabbaṃ’’|

pañ‍camītappurisau apagamana+bhaya+virati+maucanatthādīhi| maithunasmā apaitau maithunāpaitau, aivaṃ palāpagatau, nagaraniggatau, piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau, kāmatau nikkhantaṃ kāmanikkhantaṃ, rukkhaggā patitau rukkhaggapatitau, sāsanac‍cutau, āpatti- vuṭṭhānaṃ, dharaṇītalaggatau, sabbabhavaihi nissaṭau sabbabhavanissaṭau| bhayatādiyaugai-rājatau bhayaṃ rājabhayaṃ, caurabhayaṃ, amanussabhayaṃ, aggibhayaṃ, pāpabhītau, pāpabhīrukau| akattabbatau virati akattabbavirati, aivaṃ kāyaduc‍caritavirati, vacīduc‍caritavirati| bandhanā muttau bandhanamuttau, vanamuttau, bandhanamaukkhau| kammasamuṭṭhitaṃ, uk‍kaṭṭhuk‍kaṭṭhaṃ, aumakaumakaṃ| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva, kammatau jātaṃ kammajaṃ, aivaṃ cittajaṃ, utujaṃ, āhārajaṃ| idha na hauti ‘pāsādā patitau’|

chaṭṭhītappurisau rañ‍ñau puttau rājaputtau, aivaṃ rājapurisau, ācariyapūjakau, buddhasāvakau, buddharūpaṃ, jinavacanaṃ, samuddaghausau, dhañ‍ñānaṃ rāsi dhañ‍ñarāsi, pupphagandhau, phalarasau, kāyassa lahutā kāyalahutā| maraṇassati, rukkhamūlaṃ, ayassa pattau ayaupattau, aittha ‘‘manādyāpādīna+mau mayai cai’’ti au| aivaṃ suvaṇṇakaṭāhaṃ, pānīyathālakaṃ, sappikumbhau, daivānaṃ rājā daivarājā| pumassa liṅgaṃ pul‍liṅgaṃ, ‘‘puṃ pumassavā’’ti pumassa puṃ, niggahītalaupau, lassa dvibhāvau ca| hatthipadaṃ, itthirūpaṃ, bhikkhunisaṅghau, jambusākhā, aittha īkārūkārānaṃ rassau| bahulādhikārā nta+māna+niddhāriya+pūraṇa+bhāva+tittatthaihi na hauti| mamā+nukubbaṃ, mamā+nukurumānau, gun‍naṃ kaṇhā sampan‍nakhīratamā, sissānaṃ pañ‍camau, paṭassa suttatā| k‍vaci hautaiva ‘vattamānasāmīpyaṃ’| brāhmaṇassa suk‍kā dantāti sāpaikkhatāya na hauti| phalānaṃ tittau, phalāna+māsitau, phalānaṃ suhitau| ‘‘brāhmaṇassa uc‍caṃ gaihaṃ’’ti sāpaikkhatāya na hauti| ‘‘rañ‍ñau pāṭaliputtakassa dhanaṃ’’ti dhanasambandhai  chaṭṭhīti pāṭaliputtakaina sambandhābhāvā na hauti| ‘‘rañ‍ñau gau ca assau ca purisau cā’’ti bhin‍natthatāya vākyamaiva| rañ‍ñau gavassapurisā rājagavassapurisāti vutti hautaiva, aikatthībhāvā| sambandhīsaddānaṃ pana nic‍casāpaikkhattaipi gamakattā samāsau, gamakattampi hi samāsassa nibandhanaṃ, yathā daivadattassa gurukulaṃ, bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghautiādi|

sattamītappurisau rūpai sañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍ñā, aivaṃ rūpasañ‍caitanā, saṃsāradukkhaṃ| cakkhumhi san‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| dhammai ratau dhammaratau, dhammābhirati, dhammaruci, dhammagāravau, dhammaisu nirutti dhammanirutti, dānādhimutti, bhavantarakataṃ| dassanai assādau dassanassādau| arañ‍ñai vāsau arañ‍ñavāsau, vikālabhaujanaṃ, kālavassaṃ, vanapupphaṃ, vanamahisau, gāmasūkarau, samuddamacchā, āvāṭakacchapau, āvāṭamaṇḍūkau, kūpamaṇḍūkau, titthanāvā| itthīsu dhuttau itthidhuttau, akkhadhuttau| chāyāyaṃ sukkhau chāyāsukkhau, aṅgārapak‍kaṃ, cārakavanau| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva, vanai caratīti vanacarakau, kucchimhi sayantīti kucchisayā, thalai tiṭṭhatīti thalaṭṭhau, jalaṭṭhau, pabbataṭṭhau, maggaṭṭhau| paṅkai jātaṃ paṅkajaṃ, sarauruha+mic‍cādi| idha na hauti, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, āsanai nisin‍nau, āsanai nisīditabbaṃ|

11. visaisana+maikatthaina

visaisanaṃ syādyantaṃ visaissaina syādyantaina samānādhikaraṇainasahai+katthaṃ hauti|

samānatthai  padai yattha, bhaidyabhaidakavācakai|

visaisanasamāsau+yaṃ, visaissatthapadhānatau||

visaissagata+maiva+tra, liṅga+maitaṃ paraṃ tatau|

kammadhāraya+mic‍cai+sa, samāsau+ñ‍ñaihi sañ‍ñitau||

suttai visaissaināti avuttaipi visaisanassa sambandhīsaddattā sāmatthiyatau labbhamānaākaḍḍhitasaddaṃ pati ‘‘visaissainā’’ti vuttaṃ| vuttañ‍ca –

sāmañ‍ñavatthu yā vattha+ntaratau tu visaisiya|

aikappakārai ṭhapanā, visaisana+mitī+ritaṃ||

aikappakāragaṃ vatthu, visaissanti pavuc‍cati|

padāni yāni yānaiva, sambandha+mupayanti+ha|

gamyatai kāmacāraina, visaisana+visaissatāti||

aittha ca uppaladabbaṃ rattuppalāditau visaisayatīti nīlasaddau visaisanaṃ| taina visaisiyatīti uppalasaddau visaissaṃ| api ca bhamara+ṅgārādisāmañ‍ña nīlatthatau visaisiyatīti nīlaṃ visaissaṃ| na vatthādīnaṃ, uppalassaivāti visaisanatau uppalaṃ visaisanaṃti kāmacārainaiti vuttaṃ| api ca –

paritau ayantya+naina+tthā, pariyāyauti vuc‍cati|

gauvācāti pavuttai tu, vācatthau tu visaisanaṃ||

visaissai dissamānā yā, liṅga+saṃkhyā+vibhattiyau|

tulyādhikaraṇai bhiyyau, kattabbā tā visaisanaiti –

vuttattā mahantau+c‍cādīsu samānaliṅgādayau daṭṭhabbā| bhiyyauti kiṃ, daivā pamāṇaṃ ic‍cādi|

sau  ca chabbidhau visaisanapubbapadau, visaisanuttarapadau, visaisanaubhayapadau, upamānuttarapadau, sambhāvanāpubbapadau, avadhāraṇapubbapadauti|

tattha visaisanapubbapadai tāva-mahantau ca sau purisau cāti vākyai iminā suttaina samāsau| ‘‘ṭa nta+ntūnaṃ’’ti ntassa ṭādaisai dīghau hauti, mahāpurisau, mahāpurisā ic‍cādi|

vākyai tulyādhikaraṇabhāva pakāsanatthaṃ ca+ta-saddapayaugau| vuttiyantu samāsainaiva tappakāsanatau na tappayaugau| aiva+mañ‍ñatrāpi vuttatthāna+mappayaugau| aivaṃ mahāvīrau, mahāmuni| mahantañ‍ca taṃ balañ‍cāti mahābalaṃ, mahabbhayaṃ| santau ca sau purisau cāti sappurisau| tathā pubbapurisau, aparapurisau, paṭhamapurisau, majjhimapurisau, uttamapurisau, parapurisau, saitahatthī, kaṇhasappau, nīluppalaṃ, rattuppalaṃ, lauhitacandanaṃ| k‍vaci na hauti, puṇṇau mantānīputtau, cittau gahapati| pumā ca sau kaukilau cāti puṅkaukilau, uttarapadai pumassa puṃ hauti| aivaṃ pun‍nāgau|

khattiyā ca sā kañ‍ñā cāti khattiyakañ‍ñā|

67. itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumi+tthī pumaivai+katthaiti

bhāsitapumā itthī pumaiva hautīti pumbhāvā itthipac‍cayānaṃ nivatti hauti| aivaṃ rattalatā, dutiyabhikkhā| brāhmaṇī ca sā dārikā cāti brāhmaṇadārikā, nāgamāṇavikā| itthiyanti kiṃ, kumāriratanaṃ, samaṇipadumaṃ| bhāsitapumāti kiṃ, gaṅgānadī, taṇhānadī, pathavīdhātu| puratthimau ca sau kāyau cāti puratthimakāyau| aittha ca kāyaikadaisai kāyasaddau| aivaṃ pacchimakāyau, uparimakāyau, haiṭṭhimakāyau, sabbakāyau, navāvāsau, kataranikāyau, haitupac‍cayau| jīvitappadhānaṃ navakaṃ jīvitanavaka+mic‍cādi|

visaisanuttarapadai thairā+cariya+paṇḍitā visaisanaṃ parañ‍ca bhavati| yathā sāriputtau ca sau thairau cāti sāriputtatthairau, aivaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānatthairau, mahākassapatthairau, buddhaghausācariyau, dhammapālācariyau, ācariyaguttilau vā| mahausadhau ca sau paṇḍitau cāti mahausadhapaṇḍitau, aivaṃ vidhurapaṇḍitau|

visaisanaubhayapadai yathā-sītañ‍ca taṃ uṇhañ‍cāti sītuṇhaṃ, siniddhau ca sau uṇhau cāti siniddhuṇhau māsau| khañ‍jau ca sau khuj‍jau cāti khañ‍jakhuj‍jau, aivaṃ andhabadhirau, katākataṃ, chiddāvachiddaṃ, uc‍cāvacaṃ, chin‍nabhin‍naṃ, gatapac‍cāgataṃ| k‍vaci pubbakālassāpi paranipātau, vāsitau ca sau littau cāti littavāsitau, aivaṃ naggamūsitau, sittasammaṭṭhau, bhaṭṭhaluñ‍jitau|

upamanuttarapadai upamānabhūtaṃ visaisanaṃ paraṃ bhavati, yathā sīhauti vuttai upacaritā+nupacaritasīhānaṃ sāmañ‍ñappatītiyaṃ munisaddau visaisaiti| aittha ca –

upamānau+pamaiyyānaṃ, sadhammattaṃ siyau+pamā|

sā ca vatthu+vaṇṇa+ākārānaṃ sāmyaina hauti| sīhauva sīhau, muni ca sau sīhau cāti munisīhau, munivasabhau, munipuṅgavau, buddhanāgau, buddhādic‍cau| raṃsī viya raṃsī, saddhammau ca sau raṃsī cāti saddhammaraṃsī, aivaṃ vinayasāgarau| puṇḍarikamiva puṇḍarikau, samaṇau ca sau puṇḍarikau cāti samaṇapuṇḍarikau, samaṇapadumau| candau viya candau, mukhañ‍ca taṃ candau cāti mukhacandau, mukhapadumaṃ ic‍cādi|

sambhāvanāpubbapadai yathā-dhammauti buddhi dhammabuddhi, aivaṃ dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasaṅkhātau, dhammasammatau, pāṇasañ‍ñitā, asubhasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, dhātusañ‍ñā, attasañ‍ñā, attadiṭṭhi ic‍cādi|

avadhāraṇapubbapadai yathā-guṇau aiva dhanaṃ guṇadhanaṃ, aivaṃ saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, pañ‍ñāratanaṃ, cakkhu aiva indriyaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ, aivaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ, cakkhudhātu, cakkhudvāraṃ, rūpārammaṇa+mic‍cādi|

visaisana+visaissaihi, kriyāya ca sahai+ritau|

taisaṃ bhāvaṃ vivaicaitā, nipātau byavacchindati||

ayauga+mañ‍ñayaugañ‍ca, ac‍cantāyauga+maivi+ti|

vivakkhātau payuttaupi, aivatthau ñāyatai yatau||

byavacchaidaphalaṃ vākyaṃ, tatau cittau dhanuddharau|

pātthau dhanuddharau nīlu+ppala+matthīti taṃ yathā||

aittha nipātauti aiva-itinipātau, appayuttaupi aivasaddau aivaṃ yaujaitabbau – ‘‘cittau dhanuddharau aivā’’ti visaisanaina yuttau ayaugavivacchaidakau, dhanunā yaugai patiṭṭhāpanatau ‘‘pātthau aiva dhanuddharau’’ti visaissaina yuttau añ‍ñayaugavivacchaidakau, dhanuddharattassa pātthasaṃkhātaaj‍junai aiva patiṭṭhāpanatau| ‘‘nīluppala+matthaivā’’ti  kriyāya yuttau ac‍cantāyaugavivacchaidakau, nīluppalassa sabbhāvaiyaiva patiṭṭhāpanatau|

21. saṃkhyādi

aikatthai samāhārai saṃkhyādi napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ bhavati| tayau laukā samāhaṭā=cittaina sampiṇḍitā, tiṇṇaṃ laukānaṃ samāhārauti vā vākyai visaisanasamāsai katai iminā napuṃsakattaṃ bhavati| samāhārassai+kattā aikavacanamaiva, tilaukaṃ, bhau tilauka, tilaukaṃ, tilaukaina ic‍cādi| aivaṃ tayau daṇḍā tidaṇḍaṃ, tīṇi malāni samāhaṭāni, tiṇṇaṃ malānaṃ samāhārauti vā timalaṃ, tilakkhaṇaṃ, catusac‍caṃ, pañ‍casikkhāpadaṃ, chaṭṭhāyatanaṃ, sattāhaṃ, aṭṭhasīlaṃ, navalaukuttaraṃ, dasasīlaṃ, satayaujanaṃ| dvai rattiyau samāhaṭā dvirattaṃ|

12. naña

naūc‍caitaṃ syādyantaṃ syādyantaina sahai+katthaṃ hauti| ñ‍ñakārau ‘‘ṭa nañassā’’ti visaisanatthau ‘pāmanaputtādīsu nassa ṭau mā hautū’ti| na brāhmaṇau abrāhmaṇau, ‘‘ṭa nañassā’’ti nassa ṭādaisau| ñ‍ña-kārau aitthaiva visaisanatthau|

na-nisaidhau satau yuttau, daisādiniyamaṃ vinā|

asatau vā+phalau tasmā, katha+mabrāhmaṇauticai||

nisaidhatthānuvādaina, paṭisaidhavidhi k‍vaci|

parassa micchāñāṇattā+khyāpanāyau+papaj‍jatai||

duvidhau  ca+ssa nassa atthau pasaj‍japaṭisaidha+pariyudāsavasaina| tattha yau ‘‘asūrikapassārājadārā’’tiādīsu viya uttarapadatthassa sabbadā abhāvaṃ dīpaiti, sau pasaj‍japaṭisaidhavācī nāma| yau pana ‘‘abrāhmaṇa+mānayā’’tiādīsu viya uttarapadatthaṃ pariyudāsitvā paṭikkhipitvā taṃsadisai vatthumhi kāriyaṃ paṭipādayati, sau pariyudāsavācī nāma| vuttañ‍ca –

pasaj‍japaṭisaidhassa, lakkhaṇaṃ vatthunatthitā|

vatthutau+ñ‍ñatra yā vutti, pariyudāsalakkhaṇaṃ||

yatra abrāhmaṇādīsu, vatthuṃ pariyudassati|

takriyāyuttarājādiṃ, vadai sau pariyudāsakau||

pasaj‍japaṭisaidhau tu, vatthantara+manādiya|

kiñ‍civatthunisaidhassa, pasaṅgau na bhavaiyya sau||

tadañ‍ñau ca taṃviruddhau,

tadabhāvau ca nañ‍ñatthau||

tadañ‍ñatthai – abrāhmaṇau, brāhmaṇatau añ‍ñau taṃsadisauti vuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ amanussau, assamaṇau, na byākatā abyākatā dhammā| tabbiruddhatthai-na kusalā akusalā, kusalapaṭipakkhāti atthau| aivaṃ alaubhau, amittau, ayaṃ pariyudāsanayau| tadabhāvai-na katvā akatvā, akātuna puñ‍ñaṃ, akarauntau, abhāvau bhavati| ayaṃ pasaj‍japaṭisaidhanayau|

aittha  ca ubhausu pariyudāsai brāhmaṇā añ‍ñau brāhmaṇadhammai appatiṭṭhitau khattiyādi brāhmaṇasadisauva abrāhmaṇauti vuttai patīyatai| itarasmiṃ pana pakkhai kainaci saṃsayanimittaina khattiyādau brāhmaṇauti vuttassa micchāñāṇanivutti karīyati ‘‘brāhmaṇau+yaṃ na bhavati abrāhmaṇau’’ti, brāhmaṇattajjhāsitau na bhavatītyatthau| tattha sadisattaṃ vinā micchāñāṇāsambhavā payaugasāmatthiyā ca sadisapaṭipatti, taggatā ca liṅga+saṅkhyā bhavanti| atauyaiva uc‍catai ‘‘nañ‍ñivayutta+mañ‍ñasadisādhikaraṇai, kathā hi atthasampac‍cayau’’ti|

75. ana saraiti

nañasaddassa sarai ana, na assau anassau, na ariyau anariyau| aivaṃ anissarau, aniṭṭhau, anāsavau| na ādāya anādāya, anaulaukaitvā icchādi| bahulādhikārā ayuttatthaihi kaihici hauti| puna na gīyantīti apunagaiyyā gāthā, anaukāsaṃ kāraitvā, amūlāmūlaṃ gantvā, acandamul‍laukikāni mukhāni, asaddhabhaujī, alavaṇabhaujī|

13. kupādayau nic‍ca+masyādividhimhi

kusaddau pādayau ca syādyantaina sahai+katthā haunti nic‍caṃ syādividhivisayatau+ñ‍ñattha| aittha abyabhicāripādisahacaraṇatthaina kuiti nipātauva, na pathavīvācakau kusaddau| syādividhivisayau nāma ‘‘lakkhaṇitthambhūtā’’ dinā patiādīnaṃ visayai katadutiyā, tañ‍ca anvaddhamāsanti asaṃkhyasamāsa+miva mā hautūti ‘‘asyādividhimhī’’ti nisaidhau| kucchitau brāhmaṇau kubrāhmaṇau, nic‍casamāsattā asapadaina viggahau|

107. sarai kada kussu+ttaratthai

kussu+ttaratthai  vattamānassa sarādau uttarapadai kadādaisau hauti| īsakaṃ uṇhaṃ kaduṇhaṃ, kucchitaṃ an‍naṃ kadan‍naṃ, kadasanaṃ| saraiti kiṃ, kuputtā, kudārā, kudāsā, kudiṭṭhi|

108. kā+ppatthai

appatthai vattamānassa kussa kā hautu+ttarapadai| appakaṃ lavaṇaṃ kālavaṇaṃ| aivaṃ kāpupphaṃ|

109. purisai vāti

kussa kā vā| kucchitau purisau kāpurisau, kupurisau vā| pakaṭṭhau nāyakau panāyakau, padhānaṃ vacanaṃ pāvacanaṃ bhusaṃ vaddhaṃ pavaddhaṃ sarīraṃ, samaṃ sammā vā ādhānaṃ samādhānaṃ, vividhā mati vimati, vividhau visiṭṭhau vā kappau vikappau, adhikau daivau atidaivau, aivaṃ adhidaivau, adhisīlaṃ| sundarau gandhau sugandhau, kacchitau gandhau duggandhau, suṭṭhu kataṃ sukataṃ, duṭṭhu kataṃ duk‍kataṃ abhi siñ‍canaṃ abhisaikauti sanantau, atisayaina katvā, kataṃ pakaritvā, pakataṃ, atisayaina thutaṃ atitthutaṃ, atik‍kamma thutaṃ atitthutaṃ, īsaṃ kaḷārau ākaḷārau, suṭṭhu baddhau ābaddhau|

pādayau gatādyatthai paṭhamāya

pagatau ācariyau pācariyau, aivaṃ pantaivāsī|

ac‍cādayau  kantādyatthai dutiyāya

atik‍kantau mañ‍caṃ atimañ‍cau| atimālau, ‘‘ghapassa+ntassā+ppadhānassā’’ti mālāsaddai ghassa rassau| aiva+muparipi ghapānaṃ rassau|

avādayau kuṭṭhādyatthai tatiyāya

avakuṭṭhaṃ kaukilāya vanaṃ avakaukilaṃ, avamayūraṃ| avakuṭṭhanti paric‍cattaṃ|

pariyādayau gilānādyatthai catutthiyā

parigilānau+jjhaināya pariyajjhainau|

nyādayau kantādyatthai pañ‍camiyā

nikkhantau kausambiyā nik‍kausambi| asyādividhimhīti kiṃ, rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai|

14. cī kriyatthaihi

cīpac‍cayantau kriyatthaihi syādyantaihi sahai+katthau hauti| amalīnaṃ malīnaṃ karitvāti viggayha ‘‘abhūtatabbhāvai karā+sa+bhūyaugai vikārācī’’ti cīpac‍cayaikatai iminā samāsau| aittha ca-kārau ‘‘cī kriyatthaihī’’ti visaisanatthau| ‘‘pyau vā tvāssa samāsai’’ti pya hauti, pa-kārau ‘‘pyai sissā’’ti visaisanatthau| malinīkariya|

15. bhūsanā+darā+nādaraisva+laṃ+sā+sāti

bhūsanādīsva+tthaisva+la+mādayau saddā aikatthā haunti| alaṃ karitvā sak‍karitvā asak‍karitvāti viggayha samāsai  katai pyai ca ‘‘sā sādhikarā ca caric‍cā’’ti cādaisau pararūpañ‍ca| alaṃkariya, sak‍kac‍ca, asak‍kac‍ca|

16. añ‍ñaicā+ti suttaina samāsai katai… aittha yathā dvāraṃ vivarāti vuttai pakaraṇatau aggala+miti viñ‍ñāyati, aiva+midhāpi nipātapabhāvai añ‍ñai cāti sāmañ‍ñaṃ cai+ti āgamānusāraina labbhamānavibhatyantapaṭirūpanipātāva viñ‍ñāyanti| aggatau bhavitvā puraubhuyya, antarahitau hutvā tiraubhūya, antaradhānaṃ katvā tiraukariya, urasi katvā urasikariya, manasi katvā manasikariya, majjhai katvā majjhaikariya, tuṇhī bhavitvā tuṇhī bhūya|

17. vā+naika+ñ‍ñatthai

anaikaṃ syādyantaṃ añ‍ñassa padassa atthai aikatthaṃ vā hauti|

padantarassa yassa+tthau, padhānaṃ liṅga+massa ca|

samāsau sau+ya+mañ‍ñatthau, bahubbīhiparavhayau||

sau ca navavidhau dvipadau, bhin‍nādhikaraṇau, tipadau, na-nipātapubbapadau, sahapubbapadau, upamānapubbapadau, saṅkhyaubhayapadau, disantarālatthau, byatihāralakkhaṇau cāti|

1. tattha dvipadau tulyādhikaraṇau kammādīsu chasu vibhatyatthaisu bhavati|

(ka) tattha dutiyatthai tāva-āgatā samaṇā imaṃ saṅghārāmanti āgatasamaṇau saṅghārāmau| sau ca duvidhau tagguṇā+tagguṇavasaina| vuttañhi –

tagguṇau+tagguṇau  cai+ti,

sau samāsau dvidhā matau||

taṃ yathā ‘nīyataṃ lamba-

kaṇṇau+’ ‘yaṃ diṭṭhasāgarau’||

taisu yattha visaisanabhūtau atthau añ‍ñapadatthaggahaṇaina gayhati, sau tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, yathā ‘lambakaṇṇa+mānayā’ti| yattha pana na gayhati, sau atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, yathā ‘bahudhana+mānayā’ti|

idha visaisanassa pubbanipātau| aittha ca āgatasaddau ca samaṇasaddau ca attanau atthai aṭṭhatvā dutiyāvibhatyatthabhūtai saṅghārāmasaṅkhātai añ‍ñapadatthai vattanti, tatau samāsainaiva kammatthassa abhihitattā puna dutiyā na hauti| tathā āgatasamaṇā sāvatthi, āgatasamaṇaṃ jaitavanaṃ| paṭipan‍nā addhikā yaṃ paṭipan‍naddhikau pathau, abhirūḷhāva, ṇijāyaṃ nāvaṃ sā abhirūḷhavāṇijā nāvā|

(kha) tatiyatthai-jitāni indriyāni yaina sau jitindriyau samaṇau, aivaṃ diṭṭhadhammau, pattadhammau, katakic‍cau| vijitā mārā anaināti vijitamārau bhagavā, paṭividdhasabbadhammau| karaṇatthai-chin‍nau rukkhau yaina sau chin‍narukkhau pharasu|

(ga) catutthiyatthai-din‍nau suṅkau yassa sau din‍nasuṅkau rājā, din‍naṃ bhaujanaṃ assāti din‍nabhaujanau|

(gha) pañ‍camiyatthai-niggatā janā yasmā sau niggatajanau gāmau, niggatau ayau=sukhaṃ yasmāti nirayau, nik‍kilai- sau| apaitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ asmāti apaitaviñ‍ñāṇau matakāyau, apagatabhayabhairavau arahā|

(ṅa) chaṭṭhiyatthai-chin‍nā hatthā yassa sau chin‍nahatthau| aivaṃ paripuṇṇasaṅkappau khīṇāsavau, vītau rāgau assāti vītarāgau| dvai padāni assāti dvipadau, dvihatthau paṭau| taivij‍jauti aittha tivij‍jau aivāti sakatthai ṇau vuddhi ca| catuppadau, pañ‍ca cakkhūni assāti pañ‍cacakkhu bhagavā, chaḷabhiñ‍ñau, ‘‘ghapassā’’dinā rassattaṃ| navaṅgaṃ satthusāsanaṃ| dasabalau, anantañāṇau| tīṇi dasa parimāṇa+maisaṃti tidasā daivā, idha parimāṇasaddasan‍nimānatau dasasaddau saṅkhyānai vattatai| ayaṃ pac‍cayau aitaisanti idappac‍cayā, uttarapadai ‘‘imassi+daṃ vā’’ti imassa idaṃ| kau pabhavau assāti kiṃ pabhavau kāyau| vigataṃ malaṃ assāti vimalau, sundarau gandhau assāti sugandhaṃ candanaṃ, aivaṃ susīlau, sumukhau, kucchitau gandhau assāti duggandhaṃ kuṇapaṃ, dummukhau, duṭṭhu manau assāti dummanau, aivaṃ dussīlau| tapau aiva dhanaṃ assāti tapaudhanau| khantisaṅkhātaṃ balaṃ assāti khantibalau| indauti nāmaṃ aitassāti indanāmau|

chandajātādīsu visaisanavisaisitabbānaṃ yathicchitattā ubhayaṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, kamātik‍kamai payaujanābhāvā| jātau chandau assāti jātachandau, aivaṃ chandajātau| sañ‍jātapītisaumanassau, pītisaumanassasañ‍jātau| māsajātau, jātamāsau| chin‍nahatthau, hatthachin‍nau|

dīghājaṅghā  assāti dīghajaṅghau, aittha pumbhāvau, ‘‘ghapassā’’dinā rassau ca| tathā pahūtajivhau| mahantī pañ‍ñā assāti mahāpañ‍ñau| ‘‘itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumi+tthī pumai+vai+katthai’’ti vīpac‍cayābhāvaintassa ṭādaisau rassattañ‍ca| itthiyanti kiṃ, khamādhanau| bhāsitapumāti kiṃ, saddhādhurau| pañ‍ñāpakatikau, pañ‍ñāvisuddhikau, aittha ‘‘ltvitthiyūhi kau’’ti kau| gaṇḍīvadhanvāti pakatantaraina siddhaṃ|

nānā=ppakārā dumā nānādumā, nānādumaihi patitāni nānādumapatitāni, nānādumapatitāni ca tāni pupphāni caiti nānādumapatitapupphāni, taihi vāsitā nānādumapatitapupphavāsitā, nānādumapatitapupphavāsitāsānuyassasau nānādumapatitapupphavāsitasānu pabbatau, ayaṃ visaisana+amādisamāsagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇaañ‍ñapadatthau|

(ca) sattamyatthai-sampan‍nāni sassāni yasmiṃ sau sampan‍nasassau janapadau| sulabhau piṇḍau imasminti sulabhapiṇḍau daisau| ākiṇṇā manussā yassaṃ sā ākiṇṇamanussā rājadhānī| bahavau tāpasā aitasminti bahutāpasau assamau| upacitaṃ maṃsalauhitaṃ asminti upacitamaṃsalauhitaṃ sarīraṃ| bahavau sāminau asminti bahusāmikaṃ nagaraṃ, bahū nadiyau asminti bahunadikau, īkārantattā kapac‍cayau| aivaṃ bahujambukaṃ vanaṃ, bahavau kattārau asmiṃ assa vāti bahukattukau daisau, aivaṃ bahubhattukau, ‘‘ltvitthiyūhi kau’’ti kau|

2. bhin‍nādhikaraṇau  yathā-aikarattiṃ vāsau assāti aikarattivāsau, samānaina janaina saddhiṃ vāsau assāti samānavāsau purisau| ubhatau byañ‍jana+massāti ubhataubyañ‍janakau, vibhatyalaupau ‘‘vā+ñ‍ñatau’’ti kau ca, chattaṃ pāṇimhi assāti chattapāṇi, aivaṃ daṇḍapāṇi, satthapāṇi, vajirapāṇi, khaggahatthau, pattahatthau, dānai ajjhāsayau assāti dānajjhāsayau dānādhimuttikau, buddhabhattikau, saddhammagāravau ic‍cādi|

3. tipadau yathā-parak‍kamainā+dhigatā sampadā yaihi tai parak‍kamādhigatasampadā mahāpurisā| aivaṃ dhammādhigatabhaugā| aunītau pattatau pāṇi yaina sau aunītapattapāṇi| sīhassa pubbaddhamiva kāyau assāti sīhapubbaddhakāyau| mattā bahavau mātaṅgā asminti mattabahumātaṅgaṃ vanaṃ|

4. na-nipātapubbapadau yathā-natthi aitassa samauti assamau, ‘‘ṭa nañassā’’ti nassa ṭau| aivaṃ appaṭipuggalau, aputtakau, ahaitukau, kapac‍cayau, aiva+muparipi ñaiyyaṃ| natthi saṃvāsau aitaināti asaṃvāsau, na vij‍jatai vuṭṭhi aitthāti avuṭṭhikau janapadau, abhikkhukau vihārau| aivaṃ anuttarau ‘‘ana sarai’’ti ana, aivaṃ anantaṃ, anāsavau|

5. paṭhamātthai sahapubbapadau yathā-saha haitunā vattati sau sahaitukau sahaitu vā, ‘‘sahassa sau+ñ‍ñatthai’’ti sahassa sau, aivaṃ sappītikā, sappac‍cayā, sakilaisau, saupādānau, saparivārau sahaparivārau vā, saha mūlaina uddhaṭau samūluddhaṭau rukkhau|

6. upamānaupamaiyyajautakaivayuttau  upamānapubbapadau paṭhamāya yathā-nigraudhau iva parimaṇḍalau yau sau nigraudhaparimaṇḍalau| saṅkhau viya paṇḍarau ayanti saṅkhapaṇḍarau, kākau viya sūrau ayanti kākasūrau| cakkhu iva bhūtau ayaṃ paramatthadassanatauti cakkhubhūtau bhagavā| aivaṃ atthabhūtau, dhammabhūtau, brahmabhūtau, andhabhūtau| muñ‍japabbajamiva bhūtā ayaṃ muñ‍japabbajabhūtā kudiṭṭhi| tantākulamiva jātā ayaṃti tantākulajātā|

chaṭṭhyatthai-suvaṇṇassa vaṇṇau viya vaṇṇau yassa sau suvaṇṇavaṇṇau bhagavā, majjhapadalaupau| nāgassa gati viya gati assāti nāgagati| aivaṃ sīhagati, nāgavik‍kamau, sīhavik‍kamau, sīhahanu| aiṇissa viya jaṅghā assāti aiṇijaṅghau| brahmunau viya sarau assāti brahmassarau|

7. vāsaddatthai saṅkhyāubhayapadau yathā-dvai vā tayau vā dvatti, dvattayau ca tai pattā caiti dvattipattā, ‘‘tisva’’iti tisaddai parai dvissa attaṃ| dvīhaṃ vā tīhaṃ vā dvīhatīhaṃ, cha vā pañ‍ca vā vācā chappañ‍cavācā, aivaṃ sattaṭṭhamāsā, aikayaujanadviyaujanāni|

8. disantarālatthau yathā-pubbassā ca dakkhiṇassā ca disāya yadantarālaṃ sā pubbadakkhiṇā vidisā| aittha –

69. sabbādayau vuttimattaiti

itthivācakā sabbādayau vuttimattai pumaiva haunti| aivaṃ pubbuttarā, aparadakkhiṇā, pacchimuttarā| pubbā ca sā dakkhiṇā caiti vā|

9. byatihāralakkhaṇau  yathā – ‘‘tattha gahaitvā taina paharitvā yuddhai sarūpaṃ’’ti suttaina samāsai katai kaisaisu ca kaisaisu ca gahaitvā yuddhaṃ pavattaṃ kaisākaisī, daṇḍaihi ca daṇḍaihi ca paharitvā yuddhaṃ pavattaṃ daṇḍādaṇḍīti hauti| aittha ca ‘‘cī vītihārai’’ti cīpac‍cayai ‘‘cismiṃ’’ti ākārau, aivaṃ muṭṭhāmuṭṭhī|

saubhaṇau gandhau sugandhau, sau assa atthīti sugandhīhi atthiatthai īpac‍cayaina siddhaṃ| yasmā ca bhaddāya kāpilāniyā apadānai ‘‘punau pattaṃ gahaitvāna, saudhayitvā sugandhinā’’ti vuttaṃ, tasmā vuttiyaṃ ikārantassa abhāvadīpanatthaṃ ‘‘sugandhi duggandhīti payaugā na dissatī’’ti vuttaṃ| sugandhināti aikavacanai rassau|

19. catthai

anaikaṃsyādyantaṃ catthai aikatthaṃ vā hauti| samuc‍cayau anvācayau itarītarayaugau samāhārauti casaddassa atthau catubbidhau|

tattha samuc‍cayā+nvācayaisu samāsau na hauti, kriyāsāpaikkhatāya nāmānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ayuttatthattā, yathā-cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ‍ca pac‍cayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ adāsi, dānañ‍ca daihi, sīlañ‍carakkhāhi| itarītarayaugai samāhārai ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñāpaikkhattā samāsau|

ubhayatthapadhānai catthai katha+maikatthībhāvau sambhavai+ti cai, vuttañhi –

sappadhānāpi yattha+tthā, mithau sāpaikkhatā iva|

kriyāsambandhasāmañ‍ñā, catthai+katthaṃ ta+duc‍cataiti||

yasmā  aikatthībhāvaipi satīyasatīyatthai padhānaṃ, tasmā idaṃ vuc‍catai –

na+ñ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ visaisainti, catthai atthā padāniva|

satthavutyī atau taisaṃ, padhānatthaṃ+bhiyuj‍jatai||

itarītarayaugau ca, samāhārautya+yaṃ dvidhā|

samāsau tu imaṃ añ‍ñai, jānantai dvandanāmatau||

itarītarayaugasmiṃ+vayavatthassa sambhavau|

samudāyatiraubhāvau, paraṃva liṅga+massa ca||

samudāyabbhavau yasmiṃ+vayavā ca tirauhitā|

samāhārautya+yaṃ catthau, sau ca hauti napuṃsakai||

itarītarayaugau yathā-sāriputtau ca mauggal‍lānau ca sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, bhau sāriputtamauggal‍lānā ic‍cādi| avayavapadhānattā bahuvacanamaiva| samaṇā ca brāhmaṇā ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā, aivaṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikā, khattiyabrāhmaṇā, daivamanussā, candimasūriyā|

64. vij‍jāyaunisambandhīna+mā tatra catthaiti

vij‍jāsambandhīnaṃ yaunisambandhīnañ‍ca catthai ā hautīti ukārassa ā, hautā ca pautā ca hautāpautārau| aivaṃ mātāpitarau|

65. puttaiti

puttai uttarapadai pitādīna+mā hauti catthai| pitā ca puttau ca pitāputtā, aivaṃ mātāputtā|

78. jāyāya jayaṃ patimhi

patimhi  parai jāyāya jayaṃ hauti| jāyā ca pati ca jayampatayau| jānipatīti pakatantaraina siddhaṃ, jāni ca pati ca jānipati| aivaṃ jampati dampatīti|

k‍vaci appasaraṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, yathā-candau ca sūriyau ca candasūriyā, nigamā ca janapadā ca nigamajanapadā| aivaṃ surāsuragaruḍamanujabhujagagandhabbā|

k‍vaci ivaṇṇu+vaṇṇantānaṃ pubbanipātau, yathā-aggidhumā, gatibuddhibhujapaṭhaharakarasayā, dhātuliṅgāni|

k‍vaci sarādiakārantaṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, yathā-atthadhammā, atthasaddā, saddatthā vā|

añ‍ñamañ‍ñasāpaikkhānamaiva tirauhitāvayavabhaidau samudāyapadhānau samāhārau, yathā-chattañ‍ca upāhanā ca chattupāhanaṃ|

20. samāhārai napuṃsakanti

samāhārai sabbattha napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ bhavati, samāhārassai+kattā aikavacanamaiva|

23. syādīsu rassauti

napuṃsakai vattamānassa syādīsu rassau| bhau chattupāhana, chattupāhanaṃ, chattupāhanaina ic‍cādi|

tai  ca samāhāritarītarayaugā bahulaṃvidhānā niyatavisayāyaiva haunti, tatrā+yaṃ visayavibhāgau-niruttipiṭakāgatau-pāṇi+tūriya+yaugga+sainaṅgānaṃ, nic‍cavairīnaṃ, saṅkhyāparimāṇasañ‍ñānaṃ, khuddajantukānaṃ, pacanacaṇḍālānaṃ, caraṇasādhāraṇānaṃ, aikajjhāyanapāvacanānaṃ, liṅgavisaisānaṃ, vividhaviruddhānaṃ, disānaṃ, nadīnañ‍ca nic‍casamāhāraikatthaṃ bhavati|

pāṇaṅgānaṃ-cakkhu ca sautañ‍ca cakkhusautaṃ, mukhañ‍ca nāsikā ca mukhanāsikaṃ, ‘‘syādīsu rassau’’ti napuṃsakai vattamānassa rassau| hanu ca gīvā ca hanugīvaṃ, kaṇṇā ca nāsā ca kaṇṇanāsaṃ, pāṇi ca pādau ca pāṇipādaṃ, chavi ca maṃsañ‍ca lauhitañ‍ca chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ, nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca nāmarūpaṃ, jarā ca maraṇañ‍ca jarāmaraṇaṃ|

tūriyaṅgānaṃ-alasau ca tālambarau ca alasatālambaraṃ, murajau ca gaumukhau ca murajagaumukhaṃ, saṃkhau ca paṇavau ca daiṇḍimau ca, saṃkhā ca paṇavā ca daiṇḍimā cāti vā saṃkhapaṇavadaiṇḍimaṃ, paṇavādayau dvaipi bhairivisaisā, maddavikau ca pāṇavikau ca maddavikapāṇavikaṃ, gītañ‍ca vāditañ‍ca gītavāditaṃ, sammañ‍ca tāḷañ‍ca sammatāḷaṃ, sammaṃti kaṃsatālaṃ, tāḷaṃti hatthatāḷaṃ|

yauggaṅgānaṃ-phālau ca pācanañ‍ca phālapācanaṃ, yugañ‍ca naṅgalañ‍ca yuganaṅgalaṃ|

sainaṅgānaṃ-hatthinau ca assā ca hatthiassaṃ, rathā ca pattikā ca rathapattikaṃ, asi ca satti ca taumarañ‍ca piṇḍañ‍ca asisattitaumarapiṇḍaṃ, asi ca cammañ‍ca asicammaṃ, cammanti saravāraṇa-phalakaṃ| dhanu ca kalāpau ca dhanukalāpaṃ, kalāpau=tuṇīraṃ| paharaṇañ‍ca āvaraṇañ‍ca paharaṇāvaraṇaṃ|

nic‍cavairīnaṃ-ahi ca nakulau ca, ahī ca nakulā cāti vā ahinakulaṃ| aivaṃ biḷāramūsikaṃ, antassa rassattaṃ| kākaulukaṃ, sappamaṇḍūkaṃ, garuḷasappaṃ, nāgasupaṇṇaṃ|

saṅkhyāparimāṇasañ‍ñānaṃ-aikakañ‍ca dukañ‍ca aikakadukaṃ| aivaṃ, dukatikaṃ, tikacatuk‍kaṃ, catuk‍kapañ‍cakaṃ| dasakañ‍ca aikādasakañ‍ca dasaikādasakaṃ, ‘‘titālīsa’’ iti caka-bhāgalaupaniddaisaina kakārassa laupau|

khuddajantukānaṃ-kīṭā ca paṭaṅgā ca kīṭapaṭaṅgaṃ, kīṭā=kapālapiṭṭhikapāṇā| aivaṃ kunthakipil‍likaṃ, ḍaṃsā ca makasā ca ḍaṃsamakasaṃ, makkhikā ca kipil‍likā ca makkhikakipil‍likaṃ, kīṭā ca sariṃsapā ca kīṭasariṃsapaṃ| tattha kunthā=sukhumakipil‍likā|

khuddajantu anaṭṭhī vā, atha khau khuddakaupi vā|

sataṃ vā pasatau yaisaṃ, kaici ānatulā iti||

pacanacaṇḍālānaṃ-aurabbhikā ca sūkarikā ca aurabbhikasūkarikaṃ, aivaṃ sākuntikamāgavikaṃ| sapākau ca caṇḍālau ca sapākacaṇḍālaṃ, puk‍kusachavaḍāhakaṃ, vainarathakāraṃ, tattha vainā=tacchakā, rathakārā=cammakārā|

caraṇasādhāraṇānaṃ-atisau ca bhāradvājau ca atisabhāradvājaṃ, kaṭṭhau ca kapālau ca kaṭṭhakapālaṃ, sīlañ‍ca pañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sīlapañ‍ñāṇaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā ca samathavipassanaṃ, vij‍jā ca caraṇañ‍ca vij‍jācaraṇaṃ, aivaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, hirauttappaṃ, satisampa-jañ‍ñaṃ, laubhamauhaṃ, dausamauhaṃ, ahirikānauttappaṃ, thinamiddhaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍ca+mic‍cādi|

aikajjhāyanapāvacanānaṃ-dīghau ca majjhimau ca dīghamajjhimaṃ, aivaṃ aikuttara saṃyuttakaṃ, khandhakavibhaṅgaṃ|

liṅgavisaisānaṃ-itthī ca pumā ca itthipumaṃ, dāsī ca dāsau ca dāsidāsaṃ, cīvarañ‍ca piṇḍapātau ca saināsanañ‍ca gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārau ca cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhau ca sākhā ca palāsañ‍ca tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ| ‘‘lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ’’tipi dissati|

vividhaviruddhānaṃ-kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca kusalākusalaṃ, aivaṃ sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jaṃ, hīnapaṇītaṃ, kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ, chaikapāpakaṃ, sukhadukkhaṃ, adhamuttamaṃ, paṭighānunayaṃ, chāyātapaṃ, ālaukandhakāraṃ| rattiñ‍ca divā ca rattindivaṃ, ‘‘rattindivadāragavacaturassā’’ti apac‍cayai katai numaāgamau| ahau ca ratti ca ahaurattaṃ, ‘‘dīghā+hauvassaikadaisaihi ca ratyā’’ti apac‍cayai katai ‘‘manādyāpādīna+mau mayai cai’’ti aukārau|

disānaṃ-pubbā ca aparā ca pubbāparaṃ, aivaṃ puratthimapacchimaṃ, dakkhiṇuttaraṃ, pubbadakkhiṇaṃ, pubbuttaraṃ, aparadakkhiṇaṃ, aparuttaraṃ|

nadīnaṃ-gaṅgā ca yamunā ca gaṅgāyamunaṃ, aivaṃ mahīsarabhū|

tiṇa+rukkha+pasu+sakuṇa+dhana+khañ‍ña+byañ‍jana+janapadānaṃ vā| tiṇavisaisānaṃ-usīrāni ca bīraṇāni ca usīrabīraṇaṃ usīrabīraṇāni vā| aivaṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ muñ‍japabbajāni vā, kāsakusaṃ kāsakusā vā|

rukkhavisaisānaṃ-assatthā  ca kapiṭṭhā ca assatthakapiṭṭhaṃ assatthakapiṭṭhā vā, aivaṃ ambapanasaṃ ambapanasā, khadirapalāsaṃ khadirapalāsā, dhavāssakaṇṇaṃ dhavāssakaṇṇā, pilakkhanigraudhaṃ pilakkhanigraudhā, sākasālaṃ sākasālā|

pasuvisaisānaṃ-gajā ca gavajā ca gajagavajaṃ gajagavajā vā, ajā ca aiḷakā ca ajaiḷakaṃ ajaiḷakā, hatthī ca gāvau ca assā ca vaḷavā ca hatthīgavassavaḷavaṃ hatthīgavassavaḷavā, rassattaṃ| aivaṃ gaumahisaṃ gaumahisā, aiṇaiyyavarāhaṃ aiṇaiyyavarāhā, sīhabyagghataracchaṃ sīhabyagghataracchā, kuk‍kuṭasūkaraṃ kuk‍kuṭasūkarā, aiṇaiyyagaumahisaṃ aiṇaiyyagaumahisā|

sakuṇavisaisānaṃ-haṃsā ca bakā ca haṃsabakaṃ haṃsabakā| aivaṃ kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākaṃ kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākā, mayūrakauñ‍caṃ mayūrakauñ‍cā, sukasālikaṃ sukasālikā, bakabalākaṃ bakabalākā|

dhanānaṃ-hirañ‍ñañ‍ca suvaṇṇañ‍ca hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇāni| aivaṃ jātarūparajataṃ jātarūparajatāni, maṇisaṅkhamuttavaiḷuriyaṃ maṇisaṅkhamuttavaiḷuriyā|

dhañ‍ñānaṃ-sālī ca yavā ca sāliyavaṃ sāliyavā vā| aivaṃ tilamuggamāsaṃ tilamuggamāsāni, nippāvakulatthaṃ nippāvakulatthā|

byañ‍janānaṃ-sākau ca suvā ca sākasuvaṃ sākasuvā| aivaṃ gabyamāhisaṃ gabyamāhisā, aiṇaiyyavarāhaṃ aiṇaiya, varāhā| migamayūraṃ migamayūrā|

janapadānaṃ-kāsi  ca kausalā ca kāsikausalaṃ kāsikausalā, vaj‍jī ca mal‍lā ca vaj‍jimal‍laṃ vaj‍jimal‍lā, aṅgā ca magadhā ca aṅgamagadhaṃ aṅgamagadhā, aivaṃ caitivaṃsaṃ caitivaṃsā, macchasūrasainaṃ macchasūrasainā, kurupañ‍cālaṃ kurupañ‍cālā| nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca nāmarūpañ‍ca nāmarūpanāmarūpanti caturaikapañ‍cavaukāravasaina vattabbai bahulādhikārā sarūpaikasaisaṃ katvā nāmarūpanti vuttaṃ|

aitasmiṃ aikatthībhāvakaṇḍai yaṃ vuttaṃ pubbaṃ, tadaiva pubbaṃ nipatati, kamātik‍kamai payaujanassā+bhāvā| k‍vaci vipal‍lāsaupi hauti, bahulādhikāratau, dantānaṃ rājā rājadantau| catthai samāhārai ‘‘sabhāparisāyā’’ti ñāpakā k‍vaci napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ na bhavati, ādhipac‍cañ‍ca parivārau ca ādhipac‍caparivārau| aivaṃ chandapārisuddhi, paṭisandhipavattiyaṃ|

40. samāsantva

upari aya+madhikarīyati|

41. pāpādīhi bhūmiyā

pāpādīhi parā yā bhūmi, tassā samāsantau a hauti| pāpā bhūmi yasminti pāpabhūmaṃ, jātiyā upalakkhitā bhūmi jātibhūmaṃ|

42. saṃkhyāhiti

saṃkhyāhi parāya bhūmiyā a hauti| dvai bhūmiyau assa dvibhūmaṃ| aivaṃ tibhūmaṃ|

43. nadīgaudhāvarīnaṃ

saṃkhyāhi parāsaṃ nadīgaudhāvarīnaṃ samāsantau a hauti| pañ‍can‍naṃ nadīnaṃ samāhārau pañ‍canadaṃ| aivaṃ sattagaudhāvaraṃ|

44. asaṃkhyaihi cā+ṅgulyā+nāñ‍ñāsaṃkhyatthaisuti

asaṃkhyaihi  saṃkhyāhi ca parāya aṅguliyā samāsantau a hauti| niggata+maṅgulīhi niraṅgulaṃ, dvai aṅguliyau samāhaṭā dvaṅgulaṃ| anañ‍ñāsaṃkhyatthaisūti kiṃ, pañ‍ca aṅguliyau asmiṃ hatthaiti pañ‍caṅguli, aṅguliyā samīpaṃ upaṅguli|

45. dīghāhauvassaikadaisaihi ca rattyāti

dīghādīhi parāya rattiyā a hauti| dīghā ca sā ratti cāti dīgharattaṃ| ahau ca ratti ca ahaurattaṃ, āpādittā au| vassāsu ratti vassārattaṃ| pubbā ca sā ratti cāti pubbarattaṃ| aivaṃ apararattaṃ, aḍḍharattaṃ, atik‍kantau rattiṃ atirattau| dvai rattiyau samāhaṭā dvirattaṃ| anañ‍ñāsaṃkhyatthaisu tvaiva, dīghā ratti asminti dīgharatti, haimantau| rattiyā samīpaṃ uparatti| bahulaṃvidhānā k‍vaci hautaiva, rattiparimāṇānurūpaṃ yathārattaṃ|

46. gautva+catthai cā+laupai

gausaddā alaupavisayai samāsantau a hauti na cai catthādīsu samāsau| rañ‍ñau gau rājagavau| paramau ca sau gau cāti paramagavau| pañ‍can‍naṃ gun‍naṃ samāhārau pañ‍cagavaṃ, ‘‘gaussā+vaṅa’’ti avaṅa| taṃ dhana+massāti pañ‍cagavadhanau| dasagavaṃ|

47. rattindiva+dāragava+caturassā

aitai saddā a-antā nipac‍cantai| ratti ca divā ca rattindivaṃ, imināva numāga mā| dārā ca gavau ca dāragavaṃ| catassau assiyau assa caturassau|

48. āyāmai+nugavaṃ

anugavaṃti  nipac‍catai āyāmaigamyamānai| gavassa āyāmau anugavaṃ sakaṭaṃ, asaṅkhyasamāsau|

49. akkhismā+ñ‍ñatthai

akkhismā samāsantau a hauti añ‍ñatthai| visālāni akkhīni yassa sau visālakkhau|

50. dārumya+ṅgulyā

aṅgulantā añ‍ñatthai dārumhi samāsantau a hauti| dvai aṅguliyau avayavā assaiti dvaṅgulaṃ dāru, aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgulaṃ| aṅgulisadisāvayavaṃ dhañ‍ñādīnaṃ vikkhaipakaṃ dārūti vuc‍catai|

54. uttarapadai

idaṃ sabbattha adhikātabbaṃ|

55. imassi+danti

uttarapadai imassa idaṃ, imāya atthau idamaṭṭhau, thassa ṭhau, idamaṭṭhau assa atthīti idamaṭṭhī, idamaṭṭhinau bhāvau idamaṭṭhitā| imaisaṃ pac‍cayā idappac‍cayā, niggahītalaupau passa ca dvibhāvau|

57. ṭa ntantūnanti

ntantūnaṃ uttarapadai ṭa hauti| bhavantau patiṭṭhā amhanti bhavaṃpatiṭṭhā mayaṃ, niggahītāgamau, vaggantau, yaussa ṭā ca| bhagavā mūlaṃ aitaisaṃti bhagavaṃmūlakā nau dhammā|

58. a

iti  ntantūnaṃ a hauti| guṇavantau patiṭṭhā mamāti guṇavantapatiṭṭhau+smi|

60. parassa saṃkhyāsuti

saṃkhyāsu parassa au, parau satasmā adhikā parausataṃ|

61. janai puthassuti

puthassa janai u hauti| puthagaivā+yaṃ janauti puthuj‍janau, jassa dvittaṃ|

62. sau chassā+hā+yatanai vā

ahai āyatanai ca uttarapadai chassa sau hauti vā| sāhaṃ chāhaṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ chaḷāyatanaṃ| (sambaraṃ)

63. ltu+pitādīna+māravaraṅa

ltupac‍cayantānaṃ pitādīnañ‍ca yathāk‍kama+māravaraṅa vā hauntu+ttarapadai| satthunau dassanaṃ satthāradassanaṃ, kattāraniddaisau| mātarapitarau| vātvaiva, satthudassanaṃ, mātujāyau|

68. k‍vaci pac‍cayaiti

pac‍cayai pumabhāvai atisayaina byattā byattatarā, byattatamā| ‘‘tassaṃ tatra, tāya tatau, tassaṃ vailāyaṃ tadā’’‘‘… aitthantarai rūpāni ācariyasaṃgharakkhita mahāsāmitthairamataina imināva ‘‘k‍vaci pac‍cayaiti suttaina siddhāni, tainaiva tāni imasmiṃ suttai udāhaṭāni| ācariyamauggal‍lānamahāthairamataina panai+tāni ‘‘sabbādayau vuttimattaiti suttaina siddhāni| tauādīnaṃ vibhatyatthai vihitapac‍cayattā, tadanthānañ‍ca ṇādivuttittā dvin‍nampi thairānaṃ matā aviruddhā|

71. sañ‍ñāya+mudau+dakassa

sañ‍ñāya+mudakassu+ttarapadai  udādaisau hauti| udadhi, udapānaṃ|

72. kumbhādīsu vā

kumbhādīsu+ttarapadaisu udakassa udādaisau vā hauti| udakumbhau, udakakumbhau| udapattau udakapattau| udabindu udakabindu| ākatigaṇau+yaṃ|

73. sautādīsū+laupau

sautādīsu+ttarapadaisu udakassa ussa laupau hauti| dakasautaṃ, dakarakkhasau|

26. itthiya+matvā

itthiyaṃ vattamānatau akārantatau nāmasmā āpac‍cayau hauti| dhammadin‍nā|

27. nadāditau ṅī

nadādīhi itthiyaṃ vīpac‍cayau hauti| nadī mahī kumārī taruṇī vāruṇī gautamī|

gautau vā

gāvī gau| ākatigaṇau+yaṃ| va-kārau ‘‘ntantūnaṃ vīmhi tau vā’’ti visaisanatthau|

28. yakkhāditvi+nī ca

yakkhāditau  itthiyaṃ inī hauti vī ca| yakkhinī yakkhī, nāginī nāgī, sīhinī sīhī|

29. ārāmikādīhi

ārāmikāditau inī hauti+tthiyaṃ| ārāmikinī, anantarāyikinī|

sañ‍ñāyaṃ mānusau mānusinī, añ‍ñatra mānusī|

30. yuvaṇṇaihi nī

itthiya+mivaṇṇuvaṇṇantaihi nī hauti bahulaṃ| sadāpayatapāṇinī, daṇḍinī, bhikkhunī, khattabandhunī, paracittavidunī| mātuāditau kasmā na hauti, itthipac‍cayaṃ vināpi itthattābhidhānatau|

31. ttimhā+ñ‍ñatthai

ttimhā+ñ‍ñatthaiyaiva itthiyaṃ nī hauti bahulaṃ| sā+haṃ ahiṃsāratinī, tassā muṭṭhassatiniyā, sā gāvī vacchagiddhinī| añ‍ñatthaiti kiṃ, dhammarati|

32. gharaṇyādayauti

gharaṇipabhutayau nīpac‍cayantā sādhavau haunti| ghara+massā atthīti īmhi ‘‘yuvaṇṇaihi nī’’ti nī, gharaṇī| iminā nassa ṇau, īssa attañ‍ca|

ācariyā  vā yalaupau ca iti gaṇasuttaina niyāmitattā imināva nīmhi yalaupau ca, ācarinī ācariyā|

33. mātulāditvā+nī bhariyāyanti

mātulāditau bhariyāya+mānī hauti| mātulānī, varuṇānī, gahapatānī, ācariyānī|

abhariyāyaṃ khattiyā vā iti gaṇasuttaina niyamitattā iminā vā ānī, khattiyānī| nadādipāṭhā bhariyāyantu ī, khattiyī|

34. upamā+saṃhita+sahita+sañ‍ñata+saha+sapha+vāma+lakkhaṇāditū+rutūti

ūrusaddatau itthiya+mū hauti| karabhau viya ūru yassā sā karabhaurū, saṃhitau ūru assāti saṃhitaurū, aivaṃ sahitaurū, sañ‍ñataurū, sahaurū, saphaurū, vāmaurū, lakkhaṇaurū| ūti yaugavibhāgā brahmabandhū|

35. yuvāti

yuvasaddatauti hauti+tthiyaṃ| yuvati|

36. ntantūnaṃ vīmhi tau vāti

vīmhi ntantūnaṃ tau vā hauti| gacchatī gacchantī, sīlavata sīlavantī|

37. bhavatau bhautauti

vīmhi  bhavatau bhautādaisau vā hauti| bhautī bhavantī|

39. puthussa pathavaputhavāti

vīmhi puthussa pathavaputhavā haunti| pathavī puthavī, ṭhai pathavī|

iti payaugasiddhiyaṃ samāsakaṇḍau catutthau|

5. ṇādikaṇḍa

samāsau  padasaṃkhaipau, padapac‍cayasaṃhitaṃ|

taddhitaṃ nāma hautīti, viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ taisa+mantaraṃ||

1. ṇau vā+pac‍cai

chaṭṭhīyantā nāmasmā vā ṇapac‍cayau hauti apac‍cai+bhidhaiyyai| apac‍casaddasambandhittaina apac‍cavantatau katasarapac‍cayau samatthyatau chaṭṭhyantatau hautīti ‘‘chaṭṭhīyantā nāmasmā’’ti suttai avuttampi vuttaṃ| ṇādīnaṃ taddhitanti pubbācariyasañ‍ñā| ṇakārau vuddhyatthau| aiva+mañ‍ñatrāpi| ṇādivuttittā ‘‘aikatthatāyaṃ’’ti vibhattilaupau|

124. sarāna+mādissā+yuvaṇṇassā+aiau ṇānubandhai

sarāna+mādibhūtā yai akāri+vaṇṇu+vaṇṇā, taisaṃ ā+ai+au vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ ṇānubandhaiti akārassa ākārau| ā+ai+aunaṃ vuddhītipi pubbācariyasañ‍ñā| vasiṭṭhassā+pac‍caṃ vāsiṭṭhau| vīmhi vāsiṭṭhī| vaiti vasiṭṭhassā+pac‍caṃti vākyassa ca vasiṭṭhāpac‍caṃti samāsassa ca vikappatthaṃ| sau ca vāsaddau yāva ‘‘sakatthai’’ti adhikarīyati|

napuṃsakaina liṅgaina, saddau+dāhu pumaina vā|

niddissatīti ñātabba+mavisaisai pani+cchitaiti –

vuttatthā apac‍casaddassa napuṃsakatthaipi puttaputtīnaṃ dvin‍nampi vācakau hauti|

ṇādayau+bhidhaiyyaliṅgā, apac‍cai tva+napuṃsakā|

napuṃsakai sakatthai ṇyau, bhiyyau bhāvasamūhajā|

tā tu+tthiya+masaṃkhyānai, tvādicīpac‍cayantakā||

bhāradvājassa apac‍caṃ=puttau bhāradvājau, aivaṃ vaissāmittau, gautamau| aittha ca a+yuvaṇṇantābhāvā ā+ai+aunaṃ na vuddhi| vāsudaivassa apac‍caṃ vāsudaivau, baladaivau| cittakautiādīsu pana saṃyaugantattā ‘‘saṃyaugai k‍vacī’’ti k‍vaci na vuddhi| upagunau apac‍caṃ aupagavau aupagavī| aittha ‘‘uvaṇṇassā+vaṅa sarai’’ti ukārassa avaṅa|

vacchassā+pac‍canti viggayha pubbasuttaina ṇapac‍cayai katai puna vacchassā+pac‍caṃti viggahau|

2. vacchāditau ṇāna+ṇāyanā

vacchādīhi apac‍capac‍cayantaihi gauttādīhi ca saddaihi ṇāna+ṇāyanapac‍cayā vā haunti apac‍cai| vacchānau, vacchāyanau, ‘‘saṃyaugai k‍vacī’’ti na vuddhi| katissā+pac‍caṃ kac‍cau, ‘‘ṇya dic‍cādīhī’’ti ṇyau, ‘‘laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ’’ti akāralaupai cavaggapubbarūpāni| kac‍cassā+pac‍caṃ kac‍cānau, kac‍cāyanau, yāgamai kātiyānau| muggassā+pac‍caṃ=nattādīti vākyai vuddhi+ikāralaupa+pubbarūpāni| mauggal‍lassā+pac‍caṃti punaṇāna+ṇāyanā haunti, mauggal‍lānau mauggal‍lāyanau| imai cattārau apac‍capac‍cayantā| gauttāditau yathāsakaṭassā+pac‍caṃ nattādīhi sakaṭānau sakaṭāyanau| kaṇhassā+pac‍caṃ nattādi kaṇhānau kaṇhāyanau| aivaṃ aggivaissānau aggivaissāyanau, muñ‍jānau muñ‍jāyanau, kuñ‍jānau kuñ‍jāyanau| sabbattha saṃyaugattā na vuddhi| ākatigaṇau+yaṃ|

3. kattikāvidhavādīhi ṇaiyyaṇairā

kattikādīhi vidhavādīhi ca ṇaiyyaṇairappac‍cayā haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ| kattikāya apac‍caṃ kattikaiyyau, garuḷau vinatāya=supaṇṇamātuyā apac‍caṃ vainataiyyau| rauhiṇiyā apac‍caṃ rauhiṇaiyyau, gaṅgāya apac‍caṃ gaṅgaiyyau| aivaṃ bhāginaiyyau, nādaiyyau, antaiyyau, āhaiyyau, kāpaiyyau, suciyā apac‍caṃ saucaiyyau, bālaiyyau| ṇairai-vidhavāya apac‍caṃ vaidhavairau| bandhakiyā=abhisārikāya apac‍caṃ bandha-kairau| samaṇassa upajjhāyassā+pac‍caṃ sāmaṇairau, nāḷikairau ic‍cādi|

4. ṇya dic‍cādīhi

ditippabhutīhi ṇyau hauti apac‍cai|

125. saṃyaugai k‍vaci

saṃyaugavisayai k‍vaci ā+ai+auvuddhiyau haunti ṇānubandhai|

131. laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ

avaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ laupau hauti yakārādau pac‍cayai| ditiyā=asuramātuyā apac‍caṃ daic‍cau ādic‍cau| kuṇḍaniyā apac‍caṃ kauṇḍañ‍ñau, nassa ñ‍ñai pubbarūpaṃ| aditīti daivamātā|

bhātunau  apac‍caṃ bhātabyau, ‘‘yamhi gaussa cā’’ti yamhi ussa avaṅa akāralaupapubbarūpāni|

5. ā ṇi

akārantatau ṇi vā hauta+pac‍cai bahulaṃ| ā=ti nāmavisaisanattā a-kārantatauti vuttaṃ| dakkhassā+pac‍caṃ dakkhi, datti, dauṇi, vāsavi, vāruṇi| jinadattassā+pac‍caṃ jainadatti, suddhaudani, ānuruddhi ic‍cādi|

6. rājatau ñ‍ñau jātiyaṃ

rājatau ñ‍ñau vā hauta+pac‍cai khatthiyajātiyaṃ gamyamānāyaṃ| rañ‍ñau apac‍caṃ rājañ‍ñau| jātīyanti kiṃ, rājāpac‍caṃ|

7. khattā yi+yā

tijātiyaṃ apac‍cai ya+iyā haunti| khattassā+pac‍caṃ khatyau khattiyau| jātiyaṃ tvaiva, khatti|

8. manutau ssa+saṇa

manutau jātisamudāyai ssa+saṇa hauntu+pac‍cai| manunau apac‍caṃ manussau mānusau, manussī mānusī| jātiyaṃ tvaiva, mānavau, nassa ṇau, māṇavau|

9. janapadanāmasmā khattiyā rañ‍ñai ca ṇau

janapadassa yaṃ nāmaṃ, taṃ nāmasmā khattiyā apac‍cai rañ‍ñai ca ṇau hauti| pañ‍cālānaṃ apac‍caṃ rājā vā pañ‍cālau, kausalau, māgadhau, auk‍kākau| janapadanāmasmāti kiṃ, dāsarathi| khattiyāti kiṃ, pañ‍cālassa brāhmaṇassā+pac‍caṃ pañ‍cāli|

10. ṇya kurusivīhi

kurusivīhi apac‍cai rañ‍ñai ca ṇyau hauti| kurūnaṃ apac‍caṃ rājā vā kaurabyau| ‘‘yamhi gaussa cā’’timinā avaṅa, bakārapubbarūpāni| saibbau, ilaupau| (apac‍cataddhitaṃ)|

11. ṇa rāgā taina rattaṃ

rāgavācītatiyantatau ratta+mic‍cai+tasmiṃ attai ṇau hauti| kusumbhādīhi vaṇṇantarapattaṃ rattaṃ nāma| kasāvaina rattaṃ kāsāvaṃ| aivaṃ kusumbhaina rattaṃ kausumbhaṃ, hāliddaṃ, pattaṅgaṃ, mañ‍jaiṭṭhaṃ, kuṅkumaṃ| idha na hauti nīlaṃ pītanti, guṇavacanattā ṇaina vināpi dabbassā+bhidhānatau|

12. nakkhattaini+nduyuttaina kālai

tatiyantatau nakkhattā taina lakkhitai kālai ṇau hauti, taṃ cai nakkhatta+minduyuttaṃ hauti| phussaina induyuttaina lakkhitā puṇṇamāsī phussī ratti, phussau ahau, maghāya induyuttāya lakkhitā puṇṇamāsī māghī, māghau|

13. sā+ssa daivatā puṇṇamāsī

saiti paṭhamantā assaiti chaṭṭhiyatthai ṇau hauti, yaṃ paṭhamantaṃ, sā cai daivatā puṇṇamāsī vā| sugatau daivatā assa saugatau, māhindau, yāmau, vāruṇau| buddhau assa daivatāti  buddhau| phussī puṇṇamāsī assa sambandhinīti phussau māsau| aivaṃ māghau, phaggunau, cittau, vaisākhau, jaiṭṭhamūlau, āsāḷhau, sāvaṇau, pauṭṭhapādau, assayujau, kattikau, māgasirau| puṇṇamāsī ca bhatakamāsasambandhinī na hauti, puṇṇau mā assanti nibbacanā, atau aiva nipātanā ṇau, sāgamau ca|

14. ta+madhītai taṃ jānāti ka+ṇikā ca

dutiyantatau ta+madhītai taṃ jānātīti aitaisva+tthaisu ṇau hauti kau ṇikau ca| aittha casaddau kau caṇikau ca hautīti samuc‍cinau, nau ṇapac‍cayaṃ| byākaraṇa+madhītai jānātīti vā vaiyyākaraṇau, vi+ā+karaṇanti vicchij‍ja katayādaisassi+kārassa ‘‘tadādaisā tadīva bhavantī’’ti ñāyā ‘‘sarāna’’mic‍cādinā aikārai yāgamadvittāni| aivaṃ mauhuttau, naimittau, aṅgavij‍jau, vatthuvij‍jau, chandasau, ‘‘manā, dīnaṃ saka’’ iti sakāgamau| kamakau, padakau, vainayikau, suttantikau, ābhidhammikau| dvitaggahaṇaṃ ajjhainajānanai ca visuṃ visuṃ pac‍cayavidhānatthaṃ, ajjhainavisayadassanatthaṃ, pasiddhupasaṃharaṇatthañ‍ca|

15. tassa visayai daisai

chaṭṭhiyantā visayai daisasarūpai ṇau hauti| vasātīnaṃ visayau daisau vāsātau| daisaiti kiṃ, cakkhussa visayau rūpaṃ, daivadattassa visayau+nuvākau|

16. nivāsai tan‍nāmai

ti tan‍nāmai  nivāsai daisai ṇau hauti| sivīnaṃ nivāsau daisau saibbau| aittha ‘‘yavā sarai’’ti yakārai bakārapubbarūpāni| vāsātau|

17. adūrabhavaiti

ṇau, vidisāya adūrabhavaṃ vaidisaṃ|

18. taina nibbattaiti

tatiyantā nibbattatthai ṇau hauti| kusambaina nibbattā kausambī nagaraṃ, aivaṃ kākandī, mākandī, sahassaina nibbattā sāhassī parikhā, haitumhi kattari karaṇai ca yathāyaugaṃ tatiyā|

19. ta+midha+tthi

tanti paṭhamantā idhāti sattamyatthai daisai tan‍nāmai ṇau hauti, yaṃ taṃ paṭhamanta+matthi cai| udumbarā asmiṃ daisai santīti audumbarau, bādarau, pabbajau|

20. tatra bhavaiti

sattamyantā bhavatthai ṇau| udakai bhavau audakau, aurasau, jānapadau, māgadhau, kāpilavatthavau, kausambau, manasi bhavaṃ mānasaṃ sukhaṃ, sakāgamau| sārasau sakuṇau, sārasī sakuṇī, sārasaṃ pupphaṃ| mittai bhavā maittā maittī vā| purai bhavā paurī vācā| pāvusai bhavau pāvusau maighau| pāvusā  ratti, pāvusaṃ abbhaṃ| sāradau, sāradā, sāradaṃ pupphaṃ| mādhurau janau, mādhurā gaṇikā, mādhuraṃ vatthaṃ|

21. aj‍jādīhi tanauti

bhavatthai tanau| aj‍ja bhavau aj‍jatanau, svātanau, hiyyatanau| ‘‘aiauna+ma vaṇṇai’’ti aiaunaṃ a hauti|

22. purātau ṇau cati

bhavatthai ṇau tanau ca| aittha ṇakārau avayavau, naivā+nubandhau| purāṇau, purātanau|

23. amātva+c‍cauti

bhavatthai ac‍cau hauti| amā=saha bhavau amac‍cau|

24. majjhāditvi+mauti

bhavatthai imau, majjhai bhavau majjhimau| aivaṃ antimau, haiṭṭhimau, uparimau, aurimau, pārimau, pacchimau, abbhantaridhau, pac‍cantimau|

25. kaṇa+ṇaiyya+ṇaiyyaka+yi+yāti
