bhavatthai kaṇaādayau haunti| kaṇa-kusinārāyaṃ bhavau kausinārakau, māgadhakau, ārañ‍ñakau vihārau, rājagahakau, kausambakau, indapattakau, kāpilakau, bhārukacchakau, nāgarakau| aṅgaisu jātau aṅgakau, kausalakau, vaidaihakau, kambaujakau, gandhārakau, sauvīrakau, sindhavakau, assakau ic‍cādi| ṇaiyya-gaṅgaiyyau, pabbataiyyau, vānaiyyau| ṇaiyyaka-kausalaiyyakau, bārāṇasaiyyakau, campaiyyakau, silāya jātaṃ sailaiyyakaṃ, mithilaiyyakau| bārāṇasaiyyakautyādīsu ‘‘dissanta+ñ‍ñaipi pac‍cayā’’ti aiyyakau, aivaṃ upari suttai dassitapac‍cayatau visuṃ pac‍cayai dassitai iminā suttaināti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ya-gammau, yamhi akāralaupai pubbarūpaṃ rassau ca| dibbau| iya-udariyau, diviyau, pañ‍cāliyau, baudhipakkhiyau, laukiyau|

26. ṇikau

sattamyantā bhavatthai ṇikau hauti| saradai bhavau sāradikau divasau, sāradikā ratti|

27. ta+massa sippaṃ sīlaṃ paṇyaṃ paharaṇaṃ payaujanaṃ

paṭhamantā sippādivācakā assaiti chaṭṭhyatthai ṇikau hauti| vīṇāvādanaṃ sippa+massa vaiṇikau, vīṇāvādanaṃ abhaidaupacāraina vīṇā nāma| maudiṅgikau, vaṃsikau| paṃsukūladhāraṇaṃ sīla+massa paṃsukūlikau, taicīvarikau| gandhau paṇya+massa gandhikau, tailikau, gauḷikau, pūvikau, paṇṇikau, tambulikau, lauṇikau| cāpaupaharaṇa+massa cāpikau, taumarikau, muggarikau, mausalikau| upadhi=kkhandhādi payaujana+massa aupadhikaṃ, sātikaṃ, sāhassikaṃ|

28. taṃ hanta+rahati gacchatu+ñchati+carati

dutiyantā hantīti aivamādīsva+tthaisu ṇikau hauti| pakkhinau hantīti pakkhikau, sākuṇikau, māyūrikau, macchai hantīti macchikau, mainikau| migai hantīti māgavikau, vakārāgamau| migassa ‘‘tadaminā’’dinā magavādaisaipi māgavikau| aurabbhikau, hāriṇikau| sūkarikauti ikau| sata+marahatīti sātikaṃ, sandiṭṭhikaṃ, aihi passa vidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau| aittha tyādyantasamudāyatau anukaraṇattā vā taddhitassa abhidhānalakkhaṇattā vā bahulaṃvidhānaina vā pac‍cayau| sāhassikau, kumbhikau, dauṇikau, addhamāsikau, kahāpaṇikau, āsītikā gāthā, nāvutikā| sahassiyauti iyau| paradāraṃ gacchatīti pāradārikau, maggikau, pañ‍ñāsayaujanikau, pathikau| badarai uñchatīti bādarikau, sāmākikau| dhammaṃ caratīti dhammikau, adhammikau|

29. taina kataṃ kītaṃ baddha+mabhisaṅkhataṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ hataṃ hanti jitaṃ jayati dibbati khanati tarati carati vahati jīvatiti

katādīsva+tthaisu ṇikau| kāyaina kataṃ kāyikaṃ, vācasikaṃ, mānasikaṃti sakāgamau| vātaina katau ābādhau vātitau, saimhikau, pittikau| sataina kītaṃ sātikaṃ, sāhassikaṃ, vatthaina kītaṃ vatthikaṃ, kumbhikaṃ, sauvaṇṇikaṃ, ghātikaṃ| mūlatauva pac‍cayau, amūlavācittā daivadattaina kītauti na hauti, tadatthāppatītiyā| varatthāya baddhau vārattikau, āyasikau, pāsikau, suttikau| ghataina abhisaṅkhataṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ vā ghātikaṃ, gauḷikaṃ, dadhikaṃ, mārīcikaṃ| jālaina hatau hantīti vā jālikau, bālisikau| akkhaihi jitaṃ akkhikaṃ dhanaṃ, sālākikaṃ, tindukikau, ambaphalikau| akkhaihi jayati dibbati vā akkhikau| khaṇittiyā khanatīti  khāṇittikau, kuddālikau| daivadattaina jitaṃ, aṅgulyā khanatīti na hauti, tadatthānavagamā| uḷumpaina taratīti auḷumpikau, uḷumpikauti ikau| kul‍likau, gaupucchikau, nāvikau| sakaṭaina caratīti sākaṭikau, rathikau| parappikauti ikau| khandhaina vahatīti khandhikau| aṃsikau, sīsikauti ikau| vaitanaina jīvatīti vaitanikau, bhatikau, kayikau, vik‍kayikau, kayavik‍kayikauti ikau|

30. tassa saṃvattati

catutthyantā saṃvattatīti asmiṃ atthaiṇikau hauti| punabbhavāya saṃvattatīti paunaubbhavikau, itthiyaṃ paunaubbhavikā| laukāya saṃvattatīti laukikau| suṭṭhu aggauti saggau, saggāya saṃvattatīti sauvaggikau, sassau+vaka tadaminādīpāṭhā| dhanāya saṃvattatīti dhañ‍ñaṃ|

31. tatau sambhūta+māgataṃ

pañ‍camyantā sambhūta+māgatanti aitaisva+tthaisu ṇikau hauti| mātitau sambhūta+māgataṃvā+ti aittha ‘‘mātitau ca bhaginiyaṃcchau’’ti ‘‘mātitau’’ti bhāgaina ussa imhi vākyaṃ, rassadvittaisu mattikaṃ, paittikaṃ| ṇya+riyaṇa+ryapac‍cayāpi dissanti| surabhitau sambhūtaṃ saurabhyaṃ| yamhi thañ‍ñaṃ| ubhayattha ‘‘laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ’’ti laupau| riyaṇa-pitutau sambhūtau paittiyau, mātiyau, ‘‘rānubandhai+ntasarādissā’’ti ulaupau, mattiyau| ryamhi-ulaupau, cavaggapubbarūpāni, mac‍cau vā|

32. tattha vasati viditau bhattau niyuttau

sattamyantā  vasatītvaivamādīsva+tthaisu ṇikau hauti| rukkhamūlai vasatīti rukkhamūlikau, ārañ‍ñikau, rājagahikau, māgadhikau, sausānikau| laukai viditau laukikau| catumahārājaisu bhattā cātummahārājikā| dvārai niyuttau dauvārikau, dassauka tadaminādipāṭhā| bhaṇḍāgārikau| ikai-navakammikau, ādikammikau| kiyai-jātikiyau, andhakiyau|

33. tassi+daṃ

chaṭṭhiyantā ida+mic‍ca+smiṃ atthai ṇikau hauti| saṅghassa idaṃ saṅghikaṃ, puggalikaṃ, sakyaputtikau, nāṭaputtikau, jainadattikau| kiyai-sassa idaṃ sakiyau, parakiyau| niyaiattaniyaṃ| kai-sakau, rañ‍ñau idaṃ rājakaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ|

34. ṇau

chaṭṭhiyantā ida+mic‍ca+smiṃ atthai ṇau hauti| kac‍cāyanassa idaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ, byākaraṇaṃ, saugataṃ sāsanaṃ, māhisaṃ maṃsādi|

35. gavādīhi yau

gavādīhi chaṭṭhiyantaihi ida+mic‍ca+smiṃ atthai yau hauti| gun‍naṃ idaṃ gabyaṃ, avaṅa, maṃsādi| ilaupai kabbaṃ| dunau idaṃ dabbaṃ|

36. pititau bhātari raiyyaṇa

‘‘pititau  mātitau’’ti taina taina suttanipātainaiva ussa i| pitusaddā tassa bhātari raiyyaṇa| pitu bhātā paittaiyyau|

37. mātitau ca bhaginiyaṃ cchau

mātutau ca pitutau ca taisaṃ bhaginiyaṃ cchau hauti| mātu bhagini mātucchā, pitu bhagini pitucchā| kathaṃ ‘‘mātu bhātā mātulau’’ti, ‘‘mātulāditvānī’’ti nipātanā lapac‍cayau|

38. mātāpitūsvā+mahau

mātāpitūhi taisaṃ mātāpitūsvā+mahau hauti| mātu mātā mātāmahī, mātu pitā mātāmahau| pitu mātā pitāmahī, pitu pitā pitāmahau| na yathāsaṅkhyaṃ pac‍caikābhisambandhatau visuṃ visuṃ mātāpitusaddaihi taisaṃ mātāpitun‍naṃ atthai pac‍cayau hauti|

39. hitai raiyyaṇa

mātāpitūhi hitai raiyyaṇa hauti| mātu hitau mattaiyyau, paittaiyyau|

40. nindā+ñ‍ñāta+ppa paṭibhāga rassa dayā sañ‍ñāsu kau

nindādīsva+tthaisu nāmasmā kau hauti| nindāyaṃ-ninditau muṇḍau muṇḍakau, aivaṃ samaṇakau, paṇḍitakau, brāhmaṇakau, vaiyyākaraṇakau| añ‍ñātai-kassā+yaṃ assau assakau, payaugasāmatthiyā sambandhivisaisānāvagamau+vagamyatai| appatthai-appakaṃ tailaṃ tailakaṃ, ghatakaṃ| paṭibhāgatthai-hatthī viya hatthikau, assakau, balībaddhakau| ‘‘imai nau hatthikā assā, balībaddhā ca nau imai’’tyādipāṭhai ‘‘laupau’’ti kapac‍cayalaupaina vā abhaidaupacāraina vā daṭṭhabbaṃ, imai ca dāruādīhi katarūpāni| rassairassau mānusau mānusakau, rukkhakau, pilakkhakau| dayāyaṃ-dayitau=nukampitau puttau puttakau, vacchakau| sañ‍ñāyaṃ-maurau viya maurakau, katakau, bhatakau|

41. ta+massa parimāṇaṃ ṇikau ca

paṭhamantā assaiti asmiṃ atthai ṇikau hauti kau ca, tañ‍cai paṭhamantaṃ parimāṇaṃ bhavati| dauṇādīnaṃ parimitavīhādīnaṃ karaṇattā ‘‘parimīyantya+naināti parimāṇa’’nti hauti| dauṇau parimāṇa+massāti dauṇikau vīhi, khārasatikau, khārasahassikau, āsītikau vayau, upaḍḍhakāyau parimāṇa+massa upaḍḍhakāyikaṃ bimbauhanaṃ| pañ‍cakaṃ, chak‍kaṃ|

42. ya+tai+taihi+ttakau

yādīhi paṭhamantaihi assaiti chaṭṭhyatthai ttakau hauti, tañ‍cai paṭhamantaṃ parimāṇaṃ bhavati| yaṃ parimāṇa+massa yattakaṃ, tattakaṃ, ‘‘aitassaiṭa ttakai’’ti aitassa aiṭa, aittakaṃ| āvatakai-yaṃ parimāṇa+massa yāvatakau, tāvatakau|

43. sabbā cā+vantu

sabbatau  paṭhamantā yādīhi ca assaiti chaṭṭhyatthai āvantu hauti, tañ‍cai paṭhamantaṃ parimāṇaṃ bhavati| sabbaṃ parimāṇa+massa sabbāvantaṃ, ‘‘aṃṅaṃ napuṃsakai’’ti aṃ| yāvantaṃ, tāvantaṃ|

44. kimhā rati+rīva+rīvataka+rittakā

kimhā paṭhamantā assaiti chaṭṭhyatthai rati+rīva+rīvataka+rittakā haunti| kiṃsaṅkhyānaṃ parimāṇa+maisaṃ kati, rānubandhattā iṃbhāgalaupau| kīva, kīvatakaṃ, kittakaṃ| rīvantau sabhāvatau asaṃkhyau|

45. sañ‍jātaṃ tārakāditvi+tauti

sañ‍jātatthai itau| tārakā sañ‍jātā assa tārakitaṃ gaganaṃ, pupphāni sañ‍jātāni assa pupphitau rukkhau, pal‍lavitā latā|

46. mānai mattau

paṭhamantā mānavuttitau assaiti asmiṃ atthai mattau hauti| palaṃ ummāna+massa palamattaṃ| hatthau paṇāma+massa hatthamattaṃ| sataṃ māna+massa satamattaṃ| dauṇau parimāṇa+massa dauṇamattaṃ| abhaidaupacārā dauṇautipi hauti| mīyatai+naināti mānaṃ, mānassa sambandhittā chaṭṭhyantabhūtānamaiva vidhi hauti| aittha ca –

uddhamānantu  ummānaṃ, parimāṇantu sabbatau|

pamāṇaṃ hauti āyāmau, saṃkhyāsaisau tu sabbatau||

47. tagghau cu+ddhaṃ

uddhamānavuttitau tagghau hauti mattau ca| jaṇṇu parimāṇa+massa jaṇṇutagghaṃ, jaṇṇumattaṃ|

48. ṇau ca purisāti

purisā ṇau hauti mattādayau ca| purisau parimāṇa+massa paurisaṃ, purisamattaṃ+purisatagghaṃ|

49. ayu+bhadvitīhaṃ+sai

ubhadvitīhi avayavavuttīhi ayau hauti| ubhau aṃsā assa ubhayaṃ, dvayaṃ, tayaṃ| aṃsasambandhaina samudāyai vidhi hautīti na ubhayāditau bahuvacananti cai, ‘‘rāhunau sirau’’ tyādau abhaidaipi bhaidavivakkhāya labbhamānatau bahuvacanaṃ hautaiva|

50. saṅkhyāya sac‍cu+tī+sā+sa+dasantāyā+dhikā+smiṃ satasahassai ḍau

satyantāya utyantāya īsantāya āsantāya dasantāya saṅkhyāya paṭhamantāya asminti sattamyatthai ḍau hauti, sā cai  saṅkhyā adhikā hauti, ya+dasminti, taṃ cai sataṃ sahassaṃ satasahassaṃ vā hauti| vīsati adhikā asmiṃ sataiti vīsaṃsataṃ|

139. ḍai satissa tissati

ḍai parai satyantassa tikārassa laupau hauti| aikavīsaṃsataṃ sahassaṃ satasahassaṃ vā| tiṃsati adhikā asmiṃ satādikaiti tiṃsasataṃ aikatiṃsasataṃ ic‍cādi| utyantāyanavuti adhikā asmiṃ satādikaiti navutaṃsataṃ sahassaṃ satasahassaṃ vā| īsantāya-cattālīsaṃ adhikā asmiṃ satai sahassai satasahassaiti cattālīsasata+mic‍cādi| āsantāya aivaṃ, pañ‍ñāsaṃsata+mic‍cādi| dasantāya-aikādasaṃsata+mic‍cādi|

51. tassa pūraṇai+kādasāditau vā

chaṭṭhiyantāyai+kādasādikāya saṃkhyāya ḍau hauti pūraṇatthai vibhāsā| sā saṃkhyā pūriyatai yaina taṃ pūraṇaṃ| aikādasan‍naṃ pūraṇau aikādasau| añ‍ñatra ‘‘ma pañ‍cādikatīhī’’ti mau, aikādasamau| vīsatiyā pūraṇau vīsau vīsatimau, tiṃsau, tiṃsatimau| vāsaddassa vavatthitavibhāsattā nic‍caṃ cattālīsau pañ‍ñāsau|

52. ma pañ‍cādikatīhiti

mapac‍cayai pañ‍camau, pañ‍camī| sattamau, sattamī| aṭṭhamau, aṭṭhamī| katimau, katimī ic‍cādi|

53. sathādīna+mi cati

satādīnaṃ  mau ca antādaisau i ca| satassa pūraṇau satimau, sahassimau|

54. chā ṭṭha+ṭṭhamāti

chasaddā pūraṇatthai ṭṭha+ṭṭhamā| chan‍naṃ pūraṇau chaṭṭhau, chaṭṭhamau| itthiyaṃ chaṭṭhī, chaṭṭhamī| ‘‘catutthadutiyaisvai+saṃ tatiyapaṭhamā’’ti nipātanā pūraṇatthai dvitau tiyau dvissa du ca, ticatūhi tissa a, tiya+tthā ca yathāk‍kamaṃ, dutiyau tatiyau, catutthau|

55. aikā kā+kya+sahāyai

‘‘sati byabhicārai visaisanaṃ sātthakaṃ’’ti ñāyā saṃkhyātau visaisaituṃ ‘‘asahāyai’’ti vuttaṃ| aikasmā asahāyatthai ka+ākī haunti vā| aikauva aikakau, aikākī, aikau vā|

56. vacchādīhi tanuttai tarau

vacchādīnaṃ sabhāvassa tanuttai=kiñ‍cimattāvasaisai tarau hauti| sau=sakau bhāvau sabhāvau attaniyapavattinimittaṃ| susuttassa tanuttai vacchatarau, itthiyaṃ vacchatarī| yaubbanassa tanuttai aukkhatarau| aukkhā=dutiyavayaṭṭhagauṇau| assabhāvassa tanuttai assatarau| gāvauti jātisāmatthiyassa tanuttai usabhatarau| aittha tanuttaṃ appabalatā|

57. kimhā niddhāraṇai ratara+ratamā

kiṃsaddā  niddhāraṇai ratara+ratamā haunti| kau aivāti katarau bhavataṃ daivadattau, katarau bhavataṃ kaṭṭhau| katamau bhavataṃ daivadattau, katamau bhavataṃ kaṭṭhau| bhāradvājānaṃ katamau+si brahmai|

58. taina dattai li+yāti

dattai+bhidhaiyyai la+iyā haunti bahulādhikārā manussasañ‍ñāyaṃ| daivaina dattau daivalau daiviyau, brahmalau brahmiyau| sīvalau sīviyau sissa dīghau| daiva+brahma+sivāti tan‍nāmakā manussā| (rattamic‍cādianaikatthataddhitaṃ)|

59. tassa bhāvakammaisutta+tā+ttana+ṇya+ṇaiyya+ṇi+ya+ṇiyā

chaṭṭhiyantā bhāvai kammai ca ttādayau haunti bahulaṃ| na sabbai pac‍cayā sabbatau haunti añ‍ñatratta+tāhi| bhavanti aitasmā buddhisaddāti bhāvau saddapavattinimittaṃ| vuttañ‍ca –

hauntya+smā saddabuddhīti, bhāvau taṃ saddavuttiyā|

nimittabhūtaṃ nāmañ‍ca, jāti dabbaṃ kriyā guṇauti||

nīlassa paṭassa bhāvau nīlattaṃ nīlatāti guṇau bhāvau| aittha nīlaguṇavasaina paṭai nīlasaddassa vuttiyā paṭabuddhiyā nimittaṃ bhāvau nāma| nīlassa guṇassa bhāvau nīlattaṃ nīlatāti nīlaguṇajāti, aittha nīlaguṇajāti nimittaṃ hutvā nīlasaddassa guṇavuttiyā  nīlaguṇajāti nimittaṃ| gauttaṃ gautāti gaujāti, aittha jātisaddānaṃ dabbavuttiyā sati jāti nimittaṃ| pācakassa bhāvau pācakattaṃti kriyāsambandhittaṃ bhāvau, aittha pacanakriyāsambandhittaṃ bhāvau| daṇḍittaṃ visāṇittaṃ rājapurisattaṃti daṇḍa+visāṇa+rājadabbānaṃ sambandhittaṃ bhāvau, daṇḍītyādisaddapavattiyā nimittattā|

daivadattassa bhāvau daivadattattaṃ, candattaṃ, sūriyattaṃti tadavatthā visaisasāmañ‍ñaṃ, daivadattassa bālatādiavatthābhaidau, candassa kalādiavatthabhaidau ca, sūriyassa mandapaṭutādiavatthābhaidau ca sāmañ‍ñaṃ, tadaittha nimittaṃ| aittha vij‍jamānapadatthānaṃ visayabhūtasañ‍ñāsaddānaṃ pavattinimittaṃ vuttaṃ| ākāsattaṃ abhāvattaṃti, tattha ghaṭākāsa+pīṭharākāsa, paṭābhāva+ghaṭābhāvādinā upacaritabhaidasāmañ‍ñaṃ bhāvau|

ttana-puthuj‍janattanaṃ| vaidanāya bhāvau vaidanattanaṃ, rassau| aivaṃ jāyattanaṃ, jārattanaṃ|

ṇya-alasassa bhāvau kammaṃ vā ālasyaṃ, aittha ‘‘laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ’’ti a-laupai ‘‘sarāna+mādissā’’dinā ākārau, aiva+mupari ca| brāhmaṇassa bhāvau brahmañ‍ñaṃ, ñ‍ñakārapubbarūpāni| cāpalyaṃ, naipuñ‍ñaṃ, paisuñ‍ñaṃ, rañ‍ñau bhāvau raj‍jaṃ, ādhipac‍caṃ, dāyaj‍jaṃ, vaisammaṃ, vaisamaṃ, ṇapac‍cayau| sakhinau bhāvau sakhyaṃ, vāṇij‍jaṃ, āraugyaṃ, audagyaṃ, ānaṇyaṃ, dubbalyaṃ, balyaṃ, paṇḍic‍caṃ, bāhussac‍caṃ, paurauhic‍caṃ| muṭṭhassatissa bhāvau muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, ilaupau| kausal‍laṃ, vaipul‍laṃ| samānassa bhāvau sāmañ‍ñaṃ, pairissaṃ, saumanassaṃ, daumanassaṃ, sauvacassaṃ, dauvacassaṃ, nipakassa  bhāvau naipak‍kaṃ, ādhik‍kaṃ, dubhagassa bhāvau daubhaggaṃ, sarūpassa bhāvau sāruppaṃ, aupammaṃ, saukhummaṃ, tathassa bhāvau tacchaṃ| dummaidhassa bhāvau dummaijjhaṃ| bhaisajassa bhāvau bhaisaj‍jaṃ, byāvaṭassa kammaṃ vaiyyāvac‍caṃ yathā vaiyyākaraṇaṃ|

ṇaiyya-sucinau bhāvau saucaiyyaṃ, ādhipataiyyaṃ, kapissa bhāvau kāpaiyyaṃ| saṭhassa bhāvau kammaṃ vā sāṭhaiyyaṃ|

ṇa-garūnaṃ bhāvau gāravaṃ, u avaṅa| pāṭavaṃ, aj‍javaṃ, maddavaṃ, ‘‘kausaj‍jā’’dinā ussa attaṃ dvittañ‍ca| paramānaṃ bhāvau pāramī, vīpac‍cayau| samaggānaṃ bhāvau sāmaggī|

iya-adhipatinau bhāvau adhipatiyaṃ, paṇḍitiyaṃ, bahussutiyaṃ, naggiyaṃ, sūriyaṃ|

ṇiya-alasassa bhāvau kammaṃ vā ālasiyaṃ, tālusiyaṃ, mandiyaṃ, dakkhiyaṃ, paurauhitiyaṃ, vaiyyattiyaṃ|

kathaṃ rāmaṇīyakaṃti, sakatthai kantā ṇaina siddhā| kammaṃ=kriyā, tattha alasassa kammaṃ alasattaṃ alasatā alasattanaṃ ālasyaṃ ālasiyaṃ vā|

122. sakatthaiti

sakatthaipi yathāyaugaṃ ttādayau haunti| yathābhūtamaiva yathābhuc‍caṃ, kāruñ‍ñaṃ, pattakal‍laṃ| ākāsānantamaiva ākāsānañ‍caṃ, aittha akāralaupau, tassa cai pubbarūpai ca katai ‘‘tadaminā’’dinā nassa ñ‍ñau ca laupau ca hauti, kāyapāguñ‍ñatā|

60. bya vaddhadāsā vā

chaṭṭhiyantā  vaddhā dāsā ca byau hauti bhāvakammaisu| vaddhassa bhāvau kammaṃ vā vaddhabyaṃ, vaddhatā| dāsassa bhāvau kammaṃ vā dāsabyaṃ, dāsyaṃ, dāsatā| kathaṃ vaddhavaṃti, ṇai vāgamau|

61. nasa yuvā bau ca vassa

chaṭṭhiyantā yuvasaddā bhāvakammaisu naṇa vā hauti vassa bau ca| yuvassa bhāvau yaubbanaṃ, yuvattaṃ yuvatā vā|

62. aṇvāditvi+mauti

bhāvai vā imau| aṇunau bhāvau aṇimā, laghimā, mahatau bhāvau mahimā| kisassa bhāvau kasimā|

133. kisa+mahata+mimai kasa, mahāti

imamhi mahatau mahau ca kisassa kasādaisau ca hauti|

63. bhāvā taina nibbattaiti

kriyāvācakasaddatau imau, pākaina nibbattaṃ pākimaṃ, saikimaṃ|

127. kausaj‍jā+j‍java+pārisaj‍ja+sauhaj‍ja+maddavā+rissā+sabhā+jañ‍ña+thaiyya+ bāhusac‍cā

aitai saddā nipac‍cantai ṇānubandhai| kusītassa bhāvauti bhāvai ṇyau hauti, iminā īssa akārai ca tassa jai ca  katai yassa pubbarūpaṃ, kausaj‍jaṃ| ujunau bhāvai aj‍javaṃti ṇau, iminā ussa attaṃ, ‘‘uvaṇṇassā+vaṅa sarai’’ti avaṅaādaisai jassa dvittaṃ| parisāsu sādhūti vākyai ‘‘ṇyau tattha sādhū’’ti ṇyau, iminā jāgamau ca, ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ti āssa rassai ca katai jassa pubbarūpaṃ, pārisaj‍jau| iminā kamaina vākyainaiva saddasiddhi vaiditabbā| suhadayauva suhaj‍jau, tassa bhāvau sauhaj‍jaṃ, iminā ayalaupau| mudunau bhāvau maddavaṃ, iminā ussa attaṃ| isinau idaṃ bhāvau vā ārissaṃ, ṇyapac‍cayai iminā ārañāgamai ‘‘laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ’’ti antaikāralaupau ca| usabhassa idaṃ bhāvau vā āsabhaṃ, iminā ussa ā| ājānīyassa bhāvau ājañ‍ñaṃ, iminā yalaupai ‘‘laupau+vaṇṇi+vaṇṇānaṃ’’ti īkāralaupau| thainassa bhāvau thaiyyaṃ, iminā nassa yakārau| bahussutassa bhāvau bāhusac‍caṃ, iminā ussa akārau saṃyaugādilaupau| (bhāvataddhitaṃ)|

64. tara+tami+ssiki+yi+ṭṭhā+tisayai

atisayai vattamānatau hauntai+tai pac‍cayā| atisayaina pāpau pāpatarau pāpatamau pāpissitau pāpiyau pāpiṭṭhau, itthiyaṃ pāpatarā ic‍cādi| atisayappac‍cayantāpi atisayappac‍cayau, atisayaina pāpiṭṭhau pāpiṭṭhatarau pāpiṭṭhatamau| aivaṃ paṭutarau paṭutamau, paṭissikau, paṭiyau, paṭiṭṭhau, varatarau ic‍cādi paṇītatarau ic‍cādi ca|

135. jau vuddhassi+yi+ṭṭhaisu

vuddhassa  jau hauti iyaiṭṭhaisu| atisayaina vuddhau jaiyyau jaiṭṭhau, jādaisai pubbasaralaupai luttā sarā issa aikārai ca yassa dvittaṃ|

136. bāḷha+ntika+pasatthānaṃ sādha+naida+sā

iyaiṭṭhaisu bāḷha+ntika+pasatthānaṃ sādha+naida+sā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| atisayaina bāḷhau sādhiyau sādhiṭṭhau| atisayaina antikau naidiyau naidiṭṭhau| atisayaina pasatthau saiyyau saiṭṭhau, pubbaiva sarai luttai issa ai dvittañ‍ca|

137. kaṇa+kana+ppa+yuvānaṃ

iyaiṭṭhaisu appayuvānaṃ kaṇa+kanā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| atisayaina appau kaṇiyau kaṇiṭṭhau| atisayaina yuvā kaniyau kaniṭṭhau|

138. laupau vī+mantu+vantūnanti

iyaiṭṭhaisu vī+mantu+vantūnaṃ laupau| atisayaina maidhāvī maidhiyau maidhiṭṭhau| atisayaina satimā satiyau satiṭṭhau| atisayaina guṇavā guṇiyau guṇiṭṭhau| aittha yathāk‍kamaṃ mantvatthai vī+mantu+vantu hauti|

65. tan‍nissitai l‍lau

l‍lapac‍cayau  hauti dutiyantā tan‍nissitatthai| vaidaṃ nissitaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| duṭṭhu nissitaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ| vaidanti tuṭṭhi| il‍lai saṅkhāraṃ nissitaṃ saṅkhāril‍laṃ|

66. tassa vikārāvayavaisu ṇa+ṇika+ṇaiyya+mayā

pakatiyā uttara+mavatthantaraṃ vikārau| chaṭṭhiyantā nāmasmā vikārai+vayavai ca ṇādayau haunti bahulaṃ| ṇa-ayasau vikārau āyasaṃ bandhanaṃ, sakāgamau| udumbarassa avayavau vikārau vā audumbaraṃ bhasmaṃ paṇṇaṃ vā| kāpautaṃ maṃsaṃ satti vā| ṇika-kappāsassa vikārau kappāsikaṃ vatthaṃ| ṇaiyya-aiṇissa vikārau+vayavau vā aiṇaiyyaṃ| kausānaṃ vikārau kausaiyyaṃ vatthaṃ| maya-tiṇānaṃ vikārau tiṇamayaṃ, dārumayaṃ, naḷamayaṃ, mattikāmayaṃ, suvaṇṇamayau rathau, rūpiyamayaṃ| ‘‘añ‍ñasmiṃ’’ti mayau, gun‍naṃ karīsaṃ gaumayaṃ|

67. jatutau ssaṇa vā

vikārāvayavaisu jatutau ssaṇa vā hauti| aittha ‘‘vikārāvayavaisū’’ti vattantaisupi bahulādhikārā vikāraiyaiva hauti| jatunau vikārau jātussaṃ jatumayaṃ|

123. laupauti

bahulaṃ pac‍cayalaupaupi| ‘‘phalitassa rukkhassa phala+mavayavau vikārau ca, pal‍lavitassaiva pal‍lavaṃ’’ti vuttattā phalādayau  avayavā vikārā ca haunti, tasmā idaṃ vuc‍cati phalapupphamūlaisu vikārāvayavaisu-piyālassa phalāni piyālāni, mal‍likāya pupphāni mal‍likā, usīrassa mūlaṃ usīraṃ| taṃsaddaina vā tadabhidhānaṃ abhaidaupacāraina, tasmā pac‍cayalaupaṃ vināpi sijjhati|

68. samūhai kaṇa+ṇa+ṇikā

chaṭṭhiyantā samūhai kaṇa+ṇa+ṇikā haunti| gauttapac‍cayantā kaṇa-rājañ‍ñānaṃ samūhau rājañ‍ñakaṃ, mānusakaṃ| ukkhādīhi ukkhānaṃ samūhau aukkhakaṃ, ‘‘saṃyaugai k‍vacī’’ti aukārau| auṭṭhakaṃ| urabbhānaṃ samūhau aurabbhakaṃ| rājakaṃ, rājaputtakaṃ, hatthikaṃ, dhainukaṃ, māyūrakaṃ, kāpautakaṃ, māhisakaṃ| ṇa-kākānaṃ samūhau kākaṃ, bhikkhaṃ| ṇika acittā-apūpānaṃ samūhau āpūpikaṃ, saṃkulikaṃ|

69. janādīhi tāti

samūhatthai tā| janānaṃ samūhau janatā, gajatā, bandhutā, gāmatā, sahāyatā, nāgaratā| tāntā sabhāvatau itthiliṅgā|

madanīyanti karaṇai+dhikaraṇai vā anīyaina siddhaṃ| dhūmāyitattantiktantā nāmadhātutau ttaina siddhaṃ, dhūmau viya ācaratīti ‘‘kattutā+yau’’ti āyai ‘‘gamanatthākammakādhārai cā’’ti ktapac‍cayai ūāgamai ca katai dhūmāyitassa bhāvauti dhūmāyitattaṃ|

70. iyau hitaiti

hitatthai  iyau| upādānānaṃ hitaṃ upādāniyaṃ|

71. cakkhāditau ssauti

hitatthai ssau| cakkhunau hitaṃ cakkhussaṃ, āyussaṃ|

72. ṇyau tattha sādhu

sattamyantā tattha sādhūti asmiṃ atthai ṇyau hauti| sādhūti kusalau yauggau hitau vā| sabhāyaṃ kusalau sabbhau, ākāralaupau| parisāyaṃ sādhu pārisaj‍jau, ‘‘kausaj‍jā’’dinā jāgamai akāravuddhi| maidhāya hitaṃ maijjhaṃ ghataṃ| pādānaṃ hitaṃ paj‍jaṃ tailaṃ| ‘‘añ‍ñasmiṃ’’ti ṇyau, rathaṃ vahatīti racchā|

73. kammā niya+ñ‍ñāti

kammasaddā sādhvatthai niya+ñ‍ñā haunti| kammai sādhu kammaniyaṃ kammañ‍ñaṃ|

74. kathāditvi+kauti

ikau| kathāyaṃ kusalau kathikau| dhammakathikau, saṅgāmikau, pavāsikau, upavāsikau|

75. pathādīhi ṇaiyyauti

ṇaiyyau| pathai hitaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ, sapatismiṃ hitaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ dhanaṃ| padīpaiyyaṃ tailaṃ|

76. dakkhiṇāyā+rahaiti

arahatthai  ṇaiyyau| dakkhiṇaṃ arahatīti dakkhiṇaiyyau|

77. rāyau tumantāti

arahatthai tumantā rāyau vā| ghātaituṃ arahatīti ghātaitāyaṃ, ‘‘rānubandhai+ntasarādissā’’ti uṃlaupau| jāpaitāyaṃ, pabbājaitāyaṃ| vāti kiṃ, ghātaituṃ| (saṃkiṇṇataddhitaṃ)|

78. ta+maittha+ssa+tthīti mantu

paṭhamantā aittha assa atthīti aitaisva+tthaisu mantu hauti| gāvau aittha daisai assa vā purisassa santīti gaumā, gaumantau ic‍cādi guṇavantusamaṃ|

atthīti vattamānakālaupādānatau bhūtāhi bhavissantīhi vā gauhi na gaumā| kathaṃ gaumā āsi, gaumā bhavissatīti| tadāpi vattamānāhiyaiva gauhi gaumā, āsi bhavissatīti padantarā kālantaraṃ| itisaddatau visayaniyamau| vuttañhi –

pahūtai ca pasaṃsāyaṃ, nindāyañ‍cā+tisāyanai|

nic‍cayaugai ca saṃsaggai, haunti+mai mantuādayauti||

134. āyussā+yasa mantumhi

mantumhi āyussa āyasādaisau hauti| āyu assa atthīti āyasmā| gau assauti jātisaddānaṃ dabbābhidhāna- sāmatthiyā  mantvādayau na haunti, tathā guṇasaddānaṃ saitau paṭauti| yaisaṃ tu guṇasaddānaṃ dabbābhidhānasāmatthiyaṃ natthi, taihi hauntaiva, buddhi assa atthīti buddhimā| ‘‘vantva+vaṇṇā’’ti vantumhi rūpavā rasavā gandhavā saddavā| ‘‘daṇḍāditvi+kaī vā’’ti ika+ī, rasī rasikau, rūpī rūpikau, gandhī gandhikauti|

79. vantva+vaṇṇāti

vantu| pasatthaṃ sīla+massa atthīti sīlavā| pahutā pasatthā vā pañ‍ñā assa atthīti pañ‍ñavā|

80. daṇḍāditvi+ka+ī vāti

ika+ī haunti vā mantatthai| bahulaṃvidhānā kutaucisaddatau dvai haunti, kutau cai+kamaikaṃva| nic‍cayuttau daṇḍau assa atthīti daṇḍikau daṇḍī| gandhikau gandhī| vātvaiva, daṇḍavā|

‘‘uttamiṇaiva dhanā ikau’’ti gaṇasuttaina ikau dhanikau| añ‍ñau dhanī dhanavā|

‘‘asan‍nihitai atthā’’ asan‍nihitau atthau assa atthīti atthikau atthī| san‍nihitai atthavā| ‘‘tadantā ca’’ puñ‍ñatthau assa atthīti puñ‍ñatthikau puñ‍ñatthī|

‘‘vaṇṇantā īyaiva’’| brahmavaṇṇaṃ assa atthīti brahmavaṇṇī, daivavaṇṇī|

‘‘hatthadantaihi  jātiyaṃ’’| hattha+massa atthīti hatthī, dantī| añ‍ñatra hatthavā dantavā|

‘‘vaṇṇatau brahmacārimhi’’| vaṇṇau assa atthīti vaṇṇikau brahmacārī|

‘‘paukkharāditau daisai’’| paukkharaṃ jalaṃ padumaṃ vā assa atthīti paukkharī, ‘‘yuvaṇṇaihi nī’’ti nīmhi ‘‘gharaṇyādayau’’ti īssa attaṃ, nassa ṇau ca, paukkharaṇī, uppalinī, kumudinī, bhisinī, mulālinī, sālukinī|

‘‘k‍vacā+daisaipi’’| paduma+massa atthīti padumī paduminī paduminīpaṇṇaṃ| daisatau+ñ‍ñatra paukkharavā hatthī|

‘‘nāvāyi+kau’’ nāvikau| sikhī, bālī, sīlī, balī|

‘‘sukhadukkhā ī’’| sukhī dukkhī| ‘‘balā bāhūrupubbā ca’’| bāhubalī, ūrubalī|

81. tapādīhi ssīti

ssī| tapau assa atthīti tapassī, yasassī, taijassī, manassī, payassī| vātvaiva, yasavā|

82. mukhāditau rauti

rau| ninditaṃ mukha+massa atthīti mukharau| susi=chiddaṃ assa atthīti susirau| ūsau=khāramattikā asmiṃ atthīti ūsarau| madhurau guḷau, madhurā sakkharā, madhuraṃ khīraṃ| khaṃ=gīvāya vivaraṃ assa atthīti kharau gadrabhau| kuñ‍jau=hanu assa atthīti kuñ‍jarau| nagarau|

‘‘dantassa ca un‍natadantai’’ti gaṇasuttaina dantassa u ca, un‍nataṃ danta+massa atthīti danturau|

83. tuṭṭhyādīhi bhauti

bhau vā| tuṭṭhi assa atthīti tuṭṭhibhau, sālibhau, vālibhau|

84. saddhāditva

iti vā a hauti| atisayā saddhā assa atthīti saddhau, pañ‍ñau, pañ‍ñavā, saddhā kañ‍ñā, saddhaṃ kulaṃ|

85. ṇau tapāti

ṇau, tāpasau, sakāgamau| tāpasī|

86. ālva+bhijjhādīhiti

ālu vā| abhijjhā assa atthīti abhijjhālu, sītālu, dhajālu, dayālu, dayāvā| abhijjhālu aiva abhijjhālukau|

87. picchāditvi+lauti

ilau vā| piccha+massa atthīti picchilau, picchavā| phaiṇilau, jaṭilau, tuṇḍilau| ninditā vācā assa atthīti vācālauti parassaralaupau|

88. sīlāditau vauti

vau  hauti vā| sīlavau, sīlavā| kaisavau, kaisavā|

‘‘aṇṇā nic‍caṃ’’ aṇṇavau| ‘‘gaṇḍīrājīhi sañ‍ñāyaṃ’’ gaṇḍī=maiṇḍasiṅgaṃ assa atthīti gaṇḍīvaṃ dhanu, rājīvaṃ paṅkajaṃ|

89. māyā maidhāhi vīti

vī| māyāvī, maidhāvī pumā| nīmhi maidhāvinī| maidhāvī kulaṃ, ‘‘aikavacanayausva+ghaunaṃ’’ti simhi rassau| aivaṃ māyāvī|

90. si+ssarai āmyu+vāmī

issarai+bhidhaiyyai sasaddā āmī+uvāmī haunti mantvatthai| saṃ=āyattaṃ assa atthīti sāmī suvāmī| suvāminī kañ‍ñā|

91. lakkhyā ṇau a ca

lakkhīsaddā ṇau hauti mantvatthai a ca+ntassa| lakkhī assa atthīti lakkhaṇau|

92. aṅgā nau kalyāṇaiti

aṅgasmā nau hauti| kalyāṇāni saubhaṇāni aṅgāni assā atthīti aṅganā|

93. sau laumāti

laumā  sau, pahūtā laumā assa atthīti laumasau, laumasā kañ‍ñā|

94. imi+yāti

ima+iyā haunti, puttau assa atthīti puttimau, kittimau, puttiyau, kappiyau, jaṭiyau, hānabhāgiyau, sainiyau| (atthyatthataddhitaṃ)|

95. tau pañ‍camyā

pañ‍camyantā bahulaṃ tau hauti vā| tauādipac‍cayantā nipphan‍nanipātā, taihi parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ ‘‘asaṃkhyaihi sabbāsaṃ’’ti laupauva| gāmatau āgacchatīti gāmasmā āgacchati, cauratau bhāyatīti cauraihi bhāyati, satthatau parihīnau satthā parihīnau| aivaṃ purisatau, rājatau, aggitau, hatthitau, haitutau, yuttitau, itthitau, bhikkhunitau, yāgutau, jambutau, cittatau, āyutau| sabbāditau-sabbatau, yatau, tatau ic‍cādi|

96. itau+tai+ttau kutau

taumhi imassa ṭi nipac‍catai aitassa ṭa+aiṭa kiṃ saddassa kuttañ‍ca| itau imasmā, atau aittau aitasmā, kutau kasmā|

97. abhyādīhiti

tau| abhitau, paritau, pacchatau, haiṭṭhatau|

98. ādyādīhiti

sattamyantaihi  tau hauti| ādau āditau, majjhatau, antatau, piṭṭhitau, passatau, mukhatau| paṭhamantā yatau+dakaṃ ta+dādittaṃ, yaṃ udakaṃ, tadaivā+dittanti atthau|

99. sabbāditau sattamyā tra+tthā

sabbādīhi sattamyantaihi tra+tthā vā haunti| sabbatra sabbattha sabbasmiṃ, yatra yattha, tatra tattha ic‍cādi| bahulādhikārā na tumhaamhaihi|

100. katthai+ttha+kutrā+tra k‍vai+hi+dha

aitaihi ‘‘sabbāditau’’tiādinā tra+tthā| saisādaisā ca pac‍cayā ca imināva nipac‍cantai| iminā kissa ka+ku ca, aitassa ṭai+ṭā ca, vapac‍cayai kissa ilaupau ca, ha+dhapac‍cayaisu imassa ṭi ca nipac‍catai| kasmiṃ kattha kutra k‍va, aitasmiṃ aittha atra, imasmiṃ iha idha|

101. dhi sabbā vāti

sabbasmā dhi vā| sabbasmiṃ sabbadhi sabbatra|

102. yā hinti

yasaddā hiṃ, yasmiṃ yahiṃ yatra|

103. tā hañ‍cati

tasaddā haṃ hauti hiñ‍ca| tahaṃ tahiṃ tatra|

104. kuhiṃ kahanti

hiṃ  haṃ nipac‍cantai kissa ku+kā ca| kuhiṃ kahaṃ| kuhiñ‍canaṃti nipātantaraṃ|

105. sabbai+ka+ñ‍ña+ya+taihi kālai dā

aitaihi kālai dā hauti vā| sabbasmiṃ kālai sabbadā, aikadā, añ‍ñadā, yadā, tadā|

106. kadā kudā sadā+dhunai+dāni

aitai saddā nipac‍cantai| kasmiṃkālai kadā kudā, sabbasmiṃ kālai sadā, imasmiṃ kālai adhunā idāni|

107. aj‍ja saj‍jva+paraj‍jvai+tarahi karahā

pakati pac‍cayau ādaisau kālavisaisauti sabba+maitaṃ nipātanā labbhati| imassa ṭau j‍jau cā+hani nipac‍cantai, asmiṃ ahani aj‍ja| samānassa sa-bhāvau j‍ju cā+hani| samānai ahani saj‍ju| aparasmā j‍ju, aparasmiṃ ahani aparaj‍ju| imassa aitau, kālai rahi ca, imasmiṃkālai aitarahi| kiṃ saddassa kau, raha cā+naj‍jatanai| kasmiṃ kālai karaha|

108. sabbādīhi pakārai thā

sāmañ‍ñassa bhaidakau visaisau pakārau, tasmiṃ thāpac‍cayau hauti| sabbaina pakāraina sabbathā, yathā, tathā|

109. katha+mitthaṃ

ki+mimaihi  thaṃpac‍cayau, ka+ittaṃ taisaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ| kaina pakāraina kathaṃ, iminā pakāraina itthaṃ|

110. dhā saṅkhyāhiti

pakārai dhā hauti| dvīhi pakāraihi dvai vā pakārai karauti dvidhā karauti, bahudhā karauti, aikaṃ rāsiṃ pañ‍cappakāraṃ karauti pañ‍cadhā karauti| pañ‍cappakāra+maikappakāraṃ karauti aikadhā karauti|

111. vai+kā+jjhanti

aikasmā pakārai jjhaṃ vā hauti| aikaina pakāraina aikaṃ vā pakāraṃ karauti aikajjhaṃ karauti, aikadhā karauti vā|

112. dvitīhai+dhāti

aidhā vā| dvīhi pakāraihi dvai vā pakārai karauti dvaidhā, taidhā| dvidhā tidhā|

113. tabbati jātiyau

pakāravati taṃsāmañ‍ñavācakā saddā jātiyau hauti| paṭujātiyau, mudujātiyau|

114. vārasaṅkhyāya kkhattuṃ

vārasambandhiniyā saṃkhyāya kkhattuṃ hauti| dvai vārai bhuñ‍jati dvikkhattuṃ divasassa bhuñ‍jati| vāraggahaṇaṃ kiṃ, pañ‍ca bhuñ‍jati| saṅkhyāyāti kiṃ, pahūtai vārai bhuñ‍jati|

115. katimhāti

kkhattuṃ  hauti| kati vārai bhuñ‍jati katikkhattuṃ bhuñ‍jati|

116. bahumhā dhā ca pac‍cāsattiyaṃ

vārasambandhiniyā bahusaṃkhyāya dhā hauti kkhattuñ‍ca, vārānaṃ cai pac‍cāsatti hauti| bahuvārai bhuñ‍jati bahudhā divasassa bhuñ‍jati, bahukkhattuṃ vā| pac‍cāsattiyaṃti kiṃ, bahukkhattuṃ māsassa bhuñ‍jati|

117. sa kiṃ vāti

kiṃpac‍cayau aikassa sādaisau ca nipac‍catai| aikaṃ vāraṃ bhuñ‍jati sakiṃ bhuñ‍jati, aikakkhattuṃ vā|

118. sau vicchā+pakāraisu

vicchāyaṃ pakārai ca sau hauti| khaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍaṃ karauti khaṇḍasau karauti| puthuppakāraina puthusau| sabbaina pakāraina sabbasau|

119. abhūtatabbhāvai karā+sa+bhūyaugai vikārā cī

avatthāvatau+vatthantarainā+bhūtassa tāyā+vatthāya bhāvai karā+sa+bhūhi sambandhai sati vikāravācakācī hauti| adhavalaṃ dhavalaṃ karauti dhavalī karauti| adhavalau dhavalau siyā dhavalī siyā| adhavalau dhavalau bhavati dhavalī bhavati| abhūtatabbhāvaiti kiṃ, ghaṭaṃ karauti, dadhi atthi, ghaṭau bhavati| karāsabhūyaugaiti kiṃ, adhavalau dhavalau jāyatai| vikārāti kiṃ, pakatiyā mā hautu, suvaṇṇaṃ kuṇḍalī karauti| (nipātataddhitaṃ)|

120. dissanta+ñ‍ñaipi pac‍cayāti

vuttatau+ñ‍ñaipi pac‍cayā dissanti| vividhā mātarau vimātarauti visaisanasamāsau, tāsaṃ puttā vaimātikāti rikaṇapac‍cayai rānubandhattā ulaupai vuddhimhi katai vaimātikā| pathaṃ gacchantīti pathāvinau, āvī| issā assa atthīti issukī, ukī| dhuraṃ vahantīti dhaurayhā, yhaṇa|

sāmañ‍ñañ‍ca visaisau ca, bhāvajau ca nipātajau|

iti viñ‍ñūhi viñ‍ñaiyyau, taddhitau tu catubbidhau||

iti payaugasiddhiyaṃ ṇādikaṇḍau pañ‍camau|

6. tyādikaṇḍa

atha  tyādayau kriyāvācīhi dhātūhi vuc‍cantai|

kriyaṃ ācikkhatīti ākhyātanti kriyāpadassa pubbācariyasañ‍ñā| kāla+kāraka+purisaparidīpakaṃ kriyālakkhaṇaṃ tyādyantaṃ aliṅgañ‍ca, vuttampi cai+taṃ –

yaṃ tikālaṃ tipurisaṃ, kriyāvācī tikārakaṃ|

atiliṅgaṃ dvivacanaṃ, ta+dākhyātanti vuc‍cati||

14. kriyatthā bahulaṃ –ti

ca sabbattha vattatai| kriyā atthau aitassāti kriyatthau dhātu, sau ca duvidhau sakammakā+kammakavasaina| tattha yasmiṃ kriyatthai kattuvācimhā kammaṃ gavaisīyatai, sau sakammakau| itarau akammakau|

tatra sakammakā kammāpaikkhaṃ kriyaṃ vadanti, yathā kaṭaṃ karauti, gāmaṃ gacchati, audanaṃ pacati| akammakā kammanirapaikkhaṃ kriyaṃ vadanti, yathā acchati saiti tiṭṭhati| kriyāti ca gamanapacanādikau asattasammatau kattari kammai vā patiṭṭhitau kārakasamūhasādhiyau padatthau vuc‍cati| api ca –

karaṇaṃ bhavanaṃ cāpi, kara+bhūhi kathīyatai|

tatau kriyādivācattaṃ, pākādīnaṃ kathaṃ bhavai||

kara+bhūdhātūhi karaṇañ‍ca bhavanañ‍ca vuc‍cati, pāka+gamanādīhi taisaṃ kriyābhavanānaṃ vāc‍cattaṃ kathaṃ bhavatīti vuttaṃ hauti| punapi –

pākādīnañhi  vāc‍cattaṃ, karabhūsu na yuj‍jati|

taṃ bahutaravāc‍cattaṃ, pākādīsu na yuj‍jati||

taṃ nāma –

kāriyarūpā hi dhātvatthā, sattāyuttā ca tai+khilā|

tatau kriyā ca bhāvau ca, sāmañ‍ñaṃ taisu gamyatai||

1. vattamānaiti anti si tha mi ma tai antai sai vhai ai mhai|

vattamānai āraddhāparisamattai atthai vattamānatau kriyatthā tyādayau haunti| kriyādhikārattā ‘‘kriyatthā’’ti vuttaṃ| taisa+maniyamai –

14. pubbaparacchak‍kāna+maikānaikaisu tumhā+mha+saisaisu dvai dvai majjhimu+ttama+paṭhamā

aikānaikaisu tumhaamhasaddavacanīyaisu tadañ‍ñasaddavacanīyaisu ca kārakaisu pubbacchak‍kānaṃ paracchak‍kānañ‍ca majjhimu+ttama+paṭhamā dvai dvai haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| tatthati antīti paṭhamapurisau, ādau niddiṭṭhattā| si tha iti majjhimapurisau, majjhai niddiṭṭhattā| mi ma iti uttamapurisau| uttamasaddau+yaṃ sabhāvatau tipabhutīna+manta+māha| paracchak‍kaipi tai antaitiādinā aivaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ| aivaṃ saisaisu bhavissatiādīsu sattasu pac‍cayavidhānasuttaisupi yaujaitabbaṃ|

idāni bhūvādīna+maṭṭhagaṇānaṃ bhūvādigaṇaisu bhū=sattāya+miti paṭhamadhātutau parā tyādayau yaujīyantai| tattha ‘‘aikamhi vattabbai  aikavacanaṃ’’ti tyādīsu parabhūtaisu kattu+kamma+bhāvaisuyaiva kyavikaraṇalavikaraṇā hauntīti ‘‘kyau bhāvakammaisva+paraukkhaisu māna+nta+tyādīsu’’ ‘‘kattari lau’’ ic‍cādinā taisaṃ vikaraṇānaṃ vidhānā tyādayau kattu+kamma+bhāvaisvaiva viñ‍ñāyantīti kattari timhilau| kai tai vikaraṇā –

pubbāparabhāgaṭṭhānā, bhin‍nadhātuvibhattiyau|

nissāya pac‍cayā haunti, aitai vikaraṇā siyuṃ||

5,82. yuvaṇṇāna+mai, au pac‍cayai

ivaṇṇu+vaṇṇantānaṃ kriyattānaṃ ai+au haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ pac‍cayaiti ūssau+kārau|

5,89. aiauna+maya+vā sarai

sarai parai aiauna+mayavā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃti aussa avādaisau| sau purisau sādhu bhavati, sā itthī sādhu bhavati, cittaṃ sādhu bhavati|

uttattā kattu ākhyātai, tatiyā na ca kattari|

paṭhamāvibhatti hautaiva, atthamattaṃ apaikkhiya||

161. k‍vaci vikaraṇānanti

vikaraṇānaṃ k‍vaci laupau hautīti lavikaraṇassa laupau, saisaṃ purimasadisaṃ| tai purisā bhavanti| majjhimapuri- saikavacanaṃ  si, tvaṃ bhavasi| bahuvacanaṃ thapac‍cayau, tumhai bhavatha| uttamapurisaikavacanaṃ mipac‍cayau|

57. himimaisva+ssa

akārassa dīghau hauti himimaisu| ahaṃ bhavāmi| bahuvacanaṃ mapac‍cayau, mayaṃ bhavāma| aivaṃ paracchaik‍kaipi-bhavatai, bhavantai| bhavasai, bhavavhai| bhavai, bhavamhai| kaici dīghaṃ katvā paṭhanti, ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ti dīghau, bhavāmhai|

aittha ca –

laj‍jā sattā ṭhiti jāgaraṇaṃ,

vuddhi kkhaya jīvita maraṇaṃ|

kīḷā ruci raucatai ityaivaṃ,

vuttā akammakadhātu sabbai||

–ti vuttattā kammaṃ dutiyā na|

paca=pākai, akārau uc‍cāraṇatthau, aiva+muparipi| paca iti ṭhitai lavikaraṇaṃ, vikaraṇalaupādi purimasamaṃ| sau audanaṃ pacati, tai pacanti| tvaṃ pacasi, tumhai pacatha| ahaṃ pacāmi, mayaṃ pacāma| paracchak‍kai-pacatai, pacantai| pacasai, pacavhai| pacai, pacāmhai|

1,22. vippaṭisaidhai

vippaṭisaidhanaṃ=añ‍ñamañ‍ñapaṭisaidhanaṃ vippaṭisaidhau| paṭhamamajjhimapurisānaṃ dvin‍naṃ aikattha pasaṅgai majjhimapurisabahuvacanaṃ thapac‍cayau| sau  ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacasi, tumhai pacatha| tulyabalaviraudhinau hi vippaṭisaidhā paṭhamamajjhimauttamapurisānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ aikattha pasaṅgai uttamapurisabahuvacanaṃ hauti, sau ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacasi, ahañ‍ca pacāmi mayaṃ pacāmāti bhavati|

ama+gama=gamanai –

5,173. gama yami+sā+sa disānaṃ vā cchaṅ

aitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ cchaṅ vā hauti nta+māna+tyādīsu| sau gāmaṃ gacchati| anti –

74. garupubbā rassā rai+ntaintīnaṃ

garupubbasmā rassā ntai+ntīnaṃ rai vā hauti|

akkharaniyamau chandaṃ,

garulahuniyamau bhavai vutti|

dīghau saṃyaugādipubbau,

rassau garu lahu tu rassau||

gaccharai gacchanti| tvaṃ gacchasi, tumhai gacchatha| ahaṃ gacchāmi, mayaṃ gacchāma| cchādaisābhāvapakkhai ‘‘ū lassai’’ti aikārai gamaiti| paralaupai gamainti| gamaisi, gamaitha| gamaimi, gamaima| paracchak‍kaipi sau gacchatai, gaccharai gacchantai| gacchasai, gacchavhai| gacchai, gacchāmhai|

purai adhammau dibbati, purā marāmīti ca taṃsamīpai tabbauhārūpacāraina vattamānavacanaṃ| vuttañhi –

āraddhā+niṭṭhitaṃ  kic‍caṃ, vattamānanti vuc‍cati|

vattamānasamīpañ‍ca, vattamānanti vuc‍cati||

kimiva tai –

gaṅgāca taṃsamīpañ‍ca, yatau gaṅgāti ñāyatai|

gaṅgāyaṃ pātu+māgaccha, ghausau gaṅgāya+mityapi||

mukhyā+mukhyatthabhaidaina, vattamānaṃ tatau dvidhā|

mukhyañhi ruḷhi+māpan‍naṃ, ta+dāraupā amukhyatāti||

purai+purāsaddaihi vā anāgatatā gamyatai, tadā tassa vattamānattā| kālabyattayau vā aisau, bhavantyaiva hi kālantaraipi tyādayau bāhulakā ‘‘santaisu parigūhāmi’’ ‘‘kāyassa bhaidā abhisamparāyaṃ, sahabyataṃ gacchati vāsavassa’’ ‘‘ativailaṃ namassissaṃ’’ti| ‘‘kutau nu tvaṃ āgacchasi, rājagahatau āgacchāmī’’tiādīsu pana pac‍cuppan‍nasamīpai vattamānavacanaṃ|

5,175. gama vada dānaṃ ghamma vaj‍ja daj‍jā

gamādīnaṃ ghammādayau vā haunti nta+māna+tyādīsu| ghammati, ghammanti ic‍cādi|

kammai –

upasaggavasā kauci, akammaupi sakammakau|

yathā+bhibhūyatai rāgau, tāpasaina mahiddhināti –

vuttattā akammakatau kammani anupubbā bhūdhātutau tyādayau haunti|

5,17. kyau  bhāvakammaisva+paraukkhaisu māna+nta+tyādīsu

bhāvakammavihitaisu paraukkhāvaj‍jitaisu māna+nta+tyādīsu paraisu kyau hauti kriyatthā| kakārau+nubandhakāriyatthau| ‘‘na tai kānubandha+nāgamaisū’’ti paṭisaidhā aukārābhāvau| anubhūyati sukhaṃ daivadattaina|

aittha ca –

ākhyātaina avuttattā, kattutau tatiyā na tu|

dutiyā hauti kammassa, vuttattā paṭhamāpi+dha||

anubhūyanti sampattiyau tayā| anubhūyasi tvaṃ daivadattaina| anubhūyatha tumhai| ahaṃ anubhūyāmi, tayā mayaṃ anubhūyāma| dvittai anubhuyyati+c‍cādi| aivaṃ paracchak‍kai|

bhāvai adabbavuttinau bhāvassa aikattā aikavacanamaiva| tañ‍ca paṭhamapurisaikavacanaiyaiva sambhavati, naiva+ñ‍ñaṃ, bahulaṃvidhānā| bhūyati bhūyatai daivadattaina, sampati bhavanantyatthau|

pacadhātutau kammai kyau|

37. kyassati

kriyatthā kyassa īña vā hauti| ñau ādyāvayavatthau| daivadattaina audanau pacīyati pac‍cati, raiādaisai audanā pacīyarai pacīyanti, pac‍carai pac‍canti| cavaggapubbarūpāni| tvaṃ pacīyasi pac‍casi, tumhai pacīyatha pac‍catha| ahaṃ pacīyāmi pac‍cāmi, mayaṃ pacīyāma pac‍cāma| paracchak‍kai daivadattaina audanau pacīyatai ic‍cādi|

gamitau  kammai cchaṅ ca īñaāgamai ca katai taina gāmau gacchīyati, gāmā gacchīyanti| gacchīyasi, gacchīyatha| gacchīyāmi, gacchīyāma| aivaṃ paracchak‍kai| gamīyati gammati, gamīyanti gammanti| gamīyasi gammasi, gamīyatha gammatha, gamīyāmi gammāmi, gamīyāma gammāma paracchak‍kaipi aivaṃ| tathā ghammīyati, ghammīyanti ic‍cādi|

kriyatthā kattari tyādi, kammasmiñ‍ca sakammakā|

bhāvai vā+kammakā kammā+vacanicchāya mañ‍ñatai||

tathā hi vij‍jamānassāpi kammāssa avacanicchāyaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati –

sataupi na vivakkhā+ssa, asataupi ca sā bhavai|

taṃ yathā+nudarā kañ‍ñā, vañjhāvaddhitakau yathā||

vivakkhā laukikā aisā, asakya+mativattituṃ|

katha+maisa vipariyāsau, laukau aivā+nayuj‍jatai||

(vattamānapac‍cayanayau)|

2. bhavissati ssati ssanti ssati ssatha ssāmi ssāma ssatai ssantai ssasai ssavhai ssaṃ ssāmhai

35. a+ī+ssādīnaṃ byañ‍janassiña

kriyatthā paraisaṃ aādīnaṃ īādīnaṃ ssādīnañ‍ca byañ‍janassi+ña hauti vibhāsā| vavatthitavibhāsā+yaṃ| ssaiti ssādīnaṃ ssatiādīnañ‍cāvayavau gahitau, ‘‘ūbyañ‍janassā’’ti siddhaipi  tyādīsu aitaisamaivāti niyamatthau+ya+mārambhau| lassākāralaupai au+avādaisai bhavissati, bhavissanti| bhavissasi, bhavissatha| bhavissāmi, bhavissāma| bhavissatai, bhavissantai| bhavissasai, bhavissavhai| bhavissaṃ, bhavissāmhai|

49. kyassa ssai

kyassa vā laupau hauti ssai| sukhaṃ tayā anubhavissati, anubhavissanti| anubhavissasi, anubhavissatha| anubhavissāmi, anubhavissāma| añ‍ñatra anubhūyissati, anubhūyissanti ic‍cādi| aivaṃ paracchak‍kai| bhāvai-bhūyissati bhūyissatai|

kattari-pacissati, pacissanti ic‍cādi| kammai-pacissati audanau daivadattaina, pacīyissati, pac‍cissati| pac‍cissanti audanā pacīyissanti, pacīyissarai, pac‍cissarai, pac‍cissanti|

gamitau kattari-sau gacchissati, tai gacchissanti| tvaṃ gacchissasi, tumhai gacchissatha| ahaṃ gacchissāmi, mayaṃ gacchissāma| gacchissatai, gacchissantai| gacchissasai, gacchissavhai| gacchissaṃ, gacchissāmhai| saggaṃ gamissati, gamissanti| gamissasi, gamissatha| gamissāmi, gamissāma ic‍cādi| kammai-gacchiyissati, gacchiyissanti ic‍cādi| kyalaupai gamissati, gamissanti ic‍cādi| tathā ghammissati ghammīyissati, ghammissanti, ghammīyissanti ic‍cādi|

3. nāmai garahāvimhayaisu

nāmasaddai  nipātai sati garahāyaṃ vimhayai ca gamyamānai ssatyādayau haunti| imai hi nāma kalyāṇadhammā paṭijānissanti| aittha ‘‘jyādīhi knā’’ti knāvikaraṇai ‘‘ñāssanai jā’’ti jādaisau| na hi nāma bhikkhavai tassa maughapurisassa pāṇaisu anuddayā bhavissati| kathañhi nāma sau bhikkhavai maughapurisau sabbamattikāmayaṃ kuṭikaṃ karissati| vimhayai-acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau, santaina vata bhau pabbajitā vihāraina viharanti, yatra hi nāma sañ‍ñī samānau jāgarau pañ‍camattāni sakaṭasatāni nissāya nissāya atik‍kantāni naiva dakkhiti, na pana saddaṃ saussati| acchariyaṃ andhau nāma pabbata+mārauhissati, badhirau nāma saddaṃ saussati| (bhavissantipac‍cayanayau)|

4. bhūtai ī uṃ au ttha iṃ mhā ā ū sai vhaṃ a mhai

bhūtai parisamattai atthai vattamānatau kriyatthā īādayau haunti| bhūtānaj‍jatanai upari vakkhamānattā imai īādayau bhūtaj‍jatanai| ‘‘suvau ahausi ānandau’’tiādīsu bhūtasāmañ‍ñaiva bhavanti|

ahassu+bhayatau aḍḍha-rattaṃ vā tadupaḍḍhataṃ|

antaukatvāna viñ‍ñaiyyau, ahau aj‍jatanau iti|

tadañ‍ñau pana yau kālau, sau+naj‍jatanasañ‍ñitau||

iti paṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ ī,

15. ā+ī+ssādīsva+ña vā

āādau  īādau ssādau ca kriyatthassa vā aña hautīti dhātutau pubbaṃ aña| ‘‘kattari lau’’ti lau, aikāra+avādaisā ca|

38. aiyyātha+ssai+a+ā+ī+thānaṃ au+a+aṃ+ttha+tthau+vhauka

aiyyāthādīnaṃ auādayau vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃti īssatthau, abhavitthau, bhavitthau, abhavatthau, bhavatthau| ‘‘ā ī ū mhā ssā ssamhānaṃ vā’’ti īssa rassattaṃ| sau abhavi bhavi, abhavī bhavī|

39. uṃssiṃ+svaṃ+su

tiuṃssa iṃsu+aṃsu vā haunti| abhaviṃsu bhaviṃsu, abhavaṃsu bhavaṃsu, abhavuṃ bhavuṃ|

42. aussa a+i+ttha+tthau

aussa aādayau vā haunti| abhava bhava, abhavi bhavi, iñāgamai abhavittha bhavittha, abhavattha bhavattha, abhavitthau bhavitthau, abhavatthau bhavatthau|

43. si

aussa si vā hauti| abhavisi bhavisi, abhavasi bhavasi| abhavau bhavau|

45. mhātthāna+muña

aisaṃ  uña vā hauti| īādisambandhīnamaiva gahaṇaṃ| abhavuttha bhavuttha|

46. iṃssa ca siña

iṃssa ca siña vā hauti mhātthānañ‍ca bahulaṃ| ikārañ‍ñakārā uc‍cāraṇaādyāvayavatthā| abhavasittha bhavasittha, abhavittha bhavittha, abhavattha bhavattha, abhavisiṃ bhavisiṃ, abhavasiṃ bhavasiṃ, abhaviṃ bhaviṃ| uñāgamai āssa rassau| abhavumha bhavumha abhavumhā bhavumhā| siñāgamai abhavasimha bhavasimha abhavasimhā bhavasimhā abhavimha bhavimha abhavimhā bhavimhā abhavamha bhavamha abhavamhā bhavamhā| paracchak‍kai – ‘‘aiyyāthā’’dinā tthai abhavittha bhavittha abhavattha bhavattha| rassai abhavatha bhavatha, abhavā bhavā, abhavu bhavu| abhavisai bhavisai, abhavasai bhavasai, abhavivhaṃ bhavivhaṃ abhavavhaṃ bhavavhaṃ, assa a+mādaisai abhavaṃ bhavaṃ abhava bhava, abhavimhai bhavimhai abhavamhai bhavamhai| kammai sukhaṃ tayā anubhūyitthau anvabhūyatthau anubhūyatthau anvabhūsi anubhūsī anvabhūyī anubhūyī, anvabhūyiṃsu anubhūyiṃsu anvabhūyaṃsu anubhūyaṃsu anvabhūyuṃ anubhūyuṃ ic‍cādi| bhāvai-taina abhūyitthau bhūyitthau abhūyatthau bhūyatthau abhūyi bhūyi abhūyī bhūyī| taina abhūyattha bhūyattha abhūya bhūya abhūyā bhūyā|

sau apacitthau pacitthau ic‍cādi kattusamaṃ| kammai-apacīyitthau pacīyitthau apacīyatthau pacīyatthau| cavaggapubba- rūpai  apac‍citthau pac‍citthau apac‍catthau pac‍catthau apacīyi pacīyi apacīyī pacīyī apac‍ci pac‍ci apac‍cī pac‍cī, apacīyiṃsu pacīyiṃsu apaciṃsu paciṃsu apacīyaṃsu pacīyaṃsu apac‍caṃsu pac‍caṃsu apacīyuṃ pacīyuṃ apac‍cuṃ pac‍cuṃ ic‍cādi kattusamaṃ, pubbarūpauva visaisau|

sau gāmaṃ agacchitthau gacchitthau ic‍cādi purimasamaṃ|

30. ḍaṃsassa ca ñchaṅ

ḍaṃsassa ca gamissa ca ñchaṅ vā hauti ā+īādīsu| agañchitthau gañchitthau agañchatthau gañchatthau agañchi gañchi agañchī gañchī ic‍cādi|

29. gamissā

āādau īādau ca gamissa ā hauti vā| sau agā agami gami agamitthau gamitthau agamatthau gamatthau| īpac‍cayai lassākārassa ‘‘himimaisva+ssā’’ti aittha ‘‘assā’’ti yaugavibhāgā dīghai ‘‘dīghā īssā’’ti īssa siādaisau, agamāsi agami gami agamī gamī, agamiṃsu gamiṃsu agamaṃsu gamaṃsu agamuṃ gamuṃ| aussa aādaisai ajjhaguṃ| tvaṃ ajjhagā parassa laupau| tumhai ajjhaguttha| ahaṃ ajjhagaṃ, mayaṃ ajjhagamha| paracchak‍kai – ‘‘aiyyāthā’’dinā tthai sau agacchittha gacchittha agañchittha gañchittha agamittha gamittha agama gama agamā gamā| tai agamū gamū, aussa āādaisai ajjhaguṃ| tvaṃ agamisai gamisai| tumhai agamivhaṃ| ‘‘aiyyāthā’’ dinā a+mādaisai ahaṃ agamaṃ gamaṃ ajjhagaṃ| mayaṃ agamimhai gamimhai| kammaiagacchīyi agamīyi, agacchiyuṃ agamiyuṃ ic‍cādi| tathā aghammī ghammī ic‍cādi|

13. māyaugai ī+āādī

māyaugai  sati īādayau āādayau ca vā haunti| sakakālatau kālantaraipi pac‍cayavidhānatthau+yaṃ| mā bhavaṃ abhavitthau ic‍cādi| vāvidhānāssatyādi+aiyyādi+tvādayaupi haunti, mā bhavaṃ bhavissati, mā bhavaṃ bhavaiyya, mā bhavaṃ bhavatu ic‍cādi| (īādipac‍cayanayau)|

5. anaj‍jatanai ā ūau ttha a mhā ttha tthuṃ sai vhaṃ iṃ mhasai

avij‍jamānaj‍jatanai bhūtatthai vattamānatau kriyatthā āādayau haunti vā| ‘‘aiyyāthā’’dinā tthai ‘‘ā ī ū mhā’’ ic‍cādinā rassai ca sau abhavattha bhavattha abhava bhava abhavā bhavā, tai abhavu bhavu abhavū bhavū| au, ‘‘aussā’’tiādinā aādayau, abhava bhava abhavi bhavi abhavattha bhavattha abhavatthau bhavatthau, siādaisai abhavasi bhavasi abhavau bhavau| tumhai abhavattha bhavattha| ahaṃ abhavaṃ bhavaṃ abhava bhava, mayaṃ abhavamha bhavamha abhavamhā bhavamhā| sau abhavattha bhavattha| tai abhavatthuṃ bhavatthuṃ| tvaṃ abhavasai bhavasai| tumhai abhavavhaṃ bhavavhaṃ| ‘‘issa ca siña’’ti siṃ, ahaṃ abhavasiṃ bhavasiṃ abhaviṃ bhaviṃ| mayaṃ abhavamhasai bhavamhasai| kammai-tthai sukhaṃ tayā anubhūyittha, iñāgamābhāvai anubhūyattha ic‍cādi| bhāvai-abhūyattha bhūyattha ic‍cādi|

sau  audanaṃ apacattha pacattha ic‍cādi kattusamaṃ| kammai-apacīyattha pacīyattha apac‍cattha pac‍cattha apacīya pacīya apacīyā pacīyā apac‍ca pac‍ca apac‍cā pac‍cā| apacīyu pacīyu apacīyū pacīyū apac‍cu pac‍cu apac‍cū pac‍cū ic‍cādi| ‘‘māyaugai ī+āādī’’ti māyaugaipi āādayau, mā bhavaṃ apac‍cattha ic‍cādi|

tathā agacchattha gacchattha ic‍cādi purimasamaṃ| tathā agamattha gamattha ic‍cādipi aghammattha ghammattha ic‍cādipi| (anaj‍jatanapac‍cayanayau)|

6. paraukkhai a u ai ttha a mha ttha rai tthau vhau i mhai

apac‍cakkhai bhūtānaj‍jatanai vattamānatau kriyatthā aādayau haunti| akkhānaṃ=indriyānaṃ paraṃ paraukkhaṃ| tasmiṃ| ‘‘aparaukkhaisū’’ti vacanā kya+lādivikaraṇā na haunti|

5,71. paraukkhāyañ‍ca

paraukkhāyaṃ paṭhama+maikassaraṃ saddarūpaṃ dvai bhavati| ‘‘bhūssa vuka’’ itiādīsu vuka, bhūva bhūva iti dvittai –

5,75. laupau+nādibyañ‍janassa

dvittai pubbassa āditau+ñ‍ñassa byañ‍janassa laupau hauti|

18. pubbassa a

aādīsu dvittai pubbassa bhūssa a hauti|

5,78. catutthadutiyānaṃ tatiyapaṭhamā

dvittai  pubbaisaṃ catutthadutiyānaṃ tatiyapaṭhamā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃti bakārai sau babhūva kira, tai babhuvu kira| tvaṃ babhuvai kira, iña tumhai babhuvittha kira| ahaṃ babhuva kira, mayaṃ babhuvimha kira| sau babhuvittha kira, tai babhuvirai kira| tvaṃ babhuvitthau kira, tumhai babhuvivhau kira| ahaṃ babhuvi kira, mayaṃ babhuvimhai kira| kammai-anupabhuva kira ic‍cādi| bhāvaibabhuva babhuvittha kira|

papaca, papacu| papacai, iñāgamai papacittha| añ‍ñatra ‘‘saṃyaugādilaupau’’ti laupau, papacittha| papaca, papacimha| papacittha, papacirai| papacitthau papacithau, papacivhau| papaci, papacimhai| aivaṃ kammai|

aādipac‍cayai dvibhāvai anādibyañ‍janalaupai pubbassa ‘‘kavaggahānaṃ cavaggajā’’ti cavaggajakārai ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ti dīghai ca katai sau gāmaṃ jagāma kira, jagamu| jagamai, jagamittha| jagama, jagamimha| jagamittha, jagamirai| jagamitthau, jagamivhau| jagami, jagamimhai| aivaṃ kammai|

mūḷhavikkhittabyāsattacittaina attanāpi kriyā katā abhinibbattikālai+nupaladdhāsamānā phalainā+numīyamānā paraukkhāva vatthutau| taina uttamavisayaipi payaugasambhavau| (paraukkhāpac‍cayanayau)|

7. yyaudau  vā+tipattiyaṃ ssā ssaṃsu ssai ssatha ssaṃ ssamhā ssatha ssiṃsussasaissavhaissiṃ ssāmhasai|

aiyyādau visayai kriyātipattiyaṃ ssādayau haunti| vidhurapac‍cayaupanipātatau kāraṇavaikal‍latau vā kriyātipatana+manipphatti kriyātipatti| aitai ca ssādayau sāmatthiyā atītānāgataisvaiva haunti, na vattamānai, tatra kriyātipatyasambhavā| iñāgamai ssāssa vā rassai ca sacai sau paṭhamavayai pabbaj‍jaṃ alabhissa, arahā abhavissa bhavissa abhavissā bhavissā vā, tai ca taṃ alabhissaṃsu, arahantau abhavissaṃsu bhavissaṃsu| ‘‘aiyyāthā’’dinā ssaissa vā akārai tvaṃ abhavissa bhavissai| tumhai abhavissatha| ahaṃ abhavissaṃ| rassai mayaṃ abhavissamha bhavissamha abhavissamhā bhavissamhā| paracchak‍kai-sau abhavissatha, abhavissiṃsu| abhavissasai, abhavissavhai| abhavissiṃ, abhavissāmhasai|

kammai-taina sukhaṃ anvabhavissa, anvabhavissaṃsu kyalaupau| anvabhūyissa anvabhūyissā ic‍cādi| bhāvai-taina abhūyissa bhūyissa abhūyissā bhūyissā| abhūyissatha bhūyissatha|

aivaṃ taṇḍulādisādhanaṃ alabhissa, audanaṃ apacissa pacissa ic‍cādi| kammai-taina audanau apacissa pacissa apacissā pacissā apacīyissa pacīyissa apacīyissā pacīyissā apac‍cissa pac‍cissa apac‍cissā pac‍cissā ic‍cādi|

sau agacchissa gacchissa agacchissā gacchissā ic‍cādi| cchādaisā+bhāvai agamissa gamissa agamissā gamissā ic‍cādi| kammai-agacchissa  gacchissa agacchīyissa ic‍cādi| agamissa gamissa agamīyissa ic‍cādi| tathā aghammissa ic‍cādi| (kriyātipattipac‍cayanayau)|

8. haituphalaisvai+yya aiyyuṃ aiyyāsi aiyyātha aiyyāmi aiyyāma aitha airaṃ aithau aiyyāvhau aiyyaṃ aiyyāmhai|

haitubhūtāyaṃ phalabhūtāyañ‍ca kriyāyaṃ vattamānatau kriyatthā aiyyādayau haunti vā| lavikaraṇavuddhiādi pubbasamaṃ| parikappai –

75. aiyyai+yyāsai+yyan‍naṃ ṭai

aiyya+aiyyāsi+aiyya+mic‍caisa+mai vā hauti| sau dāni kin‍nu khau bhavai, yadi sau paṭhamavayai pabbajaiyya, arahā bhavaiyya|

47. aiyyuṃssuṃ

aiyyu+mic‍cassa ñuṃ vā hauti| sacai saṃkhārā nic‍cā bhavuṃ bhavaiyyuṃ, na nirujjhaiyyuṃ| yadi tvaṃ bhavai bhavaiyyāsi| ‘‘aiyyāthā’’ dinā assa aukārai tumhai bhavaiyyāthau bhavaiyyātha katha+mahaṃ daivau bhavaiyyāmi|

78. aiyyāmassai+mu ca

aiyyāmassa aimu vā hauti u ca| kin‍nu khau mayaṃ bhavaimu bhavaiyyāmu bhavaiyyāma| paracchak‍kai-bhavaitha, bhavairaṃ| bhavaithau, bhavaiyyāvhau| bhavai bhavaiyyaṃ, bhavaiyyāmhai| kammai-sukhaṃ  tayā anubhuyyai anubhuyyaiyya, anubhuyyuṃ anubhūyaiyyuṃ| taina tvaṃ anubhūyai anubhūyaiyyāsi, tumhai anubhūyaiyyāthau anubhūyaiyyātha| tainā+haṃ anubhūyaiyyāmi, mayaṃ anubhūyaimu anubhūyaiyyāmu anubhūyaiyyāma| paracchak‍kai-anubhūyaitha ic‍cādi| bhāvai-bhūyai bhūyaiyya bhūyaitha|

vidhimhi-sau audanaṃ pacai pacaiyya ic‍cādi| kammai-pacīyai pacīyaiyya pac‍cai pac‍caiyya ic‍cādi|

anumatiyaṃ-sau gāmaṃ gacchai gacchaiyya ic‍cādi| añ‍ñatra gamai gamaiyya ic‍cādi| tathā ghammai ghammaiyya ic‍cādi|

pātau pacaiyya cai bhuñ‍jai, ic‍cai+ttha pacanakriyā|

haitubhūtāti viñ‍ñaiyyā, phalaṃ tva+nubhavakriyā||

11. sattya+rahaisvai+yyādī

sattiyaṃ arahatai ca kriyatthā aiyyādayau haunti| bhavaṃ khalu pattaṃ pacaiyya, bhavaṃ sattau, bhavaṃ arahau|

12. sambhāvanai vā

sambhāvanai gamyamānai dhātunā vuc‍camānai ca aiyyādayau haunti vibhāsā| api bhavaṃ gilitaṃ pāsāṇaṃ pacaiyya udaragginā, sambhāvaimi saddahāmi bhavaṃ pacaiyya, bhavaṃ pacissati, bhavaṃ apaci|

9. pañha+patthanā+vidhīsu

pañhādīsu kriyatthatau aiyyādayau haunti|

pañhā  saṃpucchanaṃ, iṭṭhā-siṃsanaṃ yācanaṃ duvai|

patthanā, bhattiyā vā+tha, na vā byāpāraṇā vidhi||

pañhai-kiṃ sau bhattaṃ pacaiyya, udāhu byañ‍janaṃ| patthanāyaṃ-ahau vata sau pacaiyya cai| vidhimhi-bhavaṃ pattaṃ pacaiyya| (aiyyādipac‍cayanayau)|

10. tu antu hi tha mi ma taṃ antaṃ ssu vhau ai āmasai

pañhapatthanāvidhisvai+tai haunti kriyatthatau| āsiṃsanatthai-sau sukhī bhavatu, tai sukhitā bhavantu|

48. hissa+tau laupau

atau parassa hissa vā laupau hauti| tvaṃ sukhī bhava bhavāhi, himhi dīghau| ‘‘aiyyāthā’’dinā vhauka, tumhai sukhitā bhavathavhau bhavatha| ahaṃ sukhī bhavāmi, mayaṃ sukhinau bhavāma| paracchak‍kai-tathā bhavataṃ, bhavantaṃ| bhavassu, bhavavhau| bhavai bhavāmasai| kammai-tayā anubhūyatu, anubhūyantu ic‍cādi| bhāvai-bhūyatu, bhūyataṃ|

āṇattiyaṃ-daivadattau dāni audanaṃ pacatu, pacantu ic‍cādi| kammai-taina audanau pac‍catu ic‍cādi| tathā sau gāmaṃ gacchatu ic‍cādi| ‘‘ū lassai’’ti lassai+kārai gamaitu, gamaintu ic‍cādi| ghammādaisai ghammatu, ghammantu ic‍cādi| kammai-gacchīyatu ic‍cādi| tathā gamīyatu ic‍cādi|

vidhimhi-lassa laupai luttai vuddhi, idha pabbatau hautu|

nimantanai-adhivāsaitha  tumhai bhantai bhagavā bhaujanaṃ, vasatissa ‘‘curāditau ṇī’’ti ṇimhi ‘‘assā ṇānubandhai’’ti ā, ‘‘yuvaṇṇāna+maiau pac‍cayai’’ti ai ca hauti| idha nissīdatu bhavaṃ, sadassa ‘‘jara+sadāna+mīma vā’’ti īma, manubandhattā antasaratau īma|

ajjhaisanai-daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ, aittha curādittā purimasamaṃ|

anumatiyaṃ-puccha vāsava maṃ pañhaṃ, pavisatu bhavaṃ| aittha pucchatau hissa laupau| aivaṃ nisīda|

patthanā=yācanā, tatra-dada=dānai, dadāhi mai gāmavarāni pañ‍ca, dātau lassa aikārai ca aikaṃ mai nayanaṃ daihi|

pattakālai-sampattau tai kālau kaṭakaraṇai, kaṭaṃ karautu bhavaṃ| aittha ‘‘tanāditvau’’ti au|

tvādī aiyyādayau vatta-mānā sampati,+nāgatai|

bhavissati, paraukkhādi-cattārau+tītakālikā||

5,173. gama yami+sā+sa disānaṃ vā cchaṅ

aitaisaṃ vā cchaṅ hautinta+māna+tyādīsu| sau saggaṃ gacchati gamaiti, gacchanti ic‍cādi| kammai-gacchīyati gamīyati ic‍cādi| yama=uparamai, paraukkhāanaj‍jatanarūpāni sabbattha payauga+manugamma yaujaitabbāni| nipubbau, niyacchati niyamati, niyacchanti niyamanti| saṃpubbau, ‘‘yai saṃssā’’ti ñ‍ñattaṃ, sañ‍ñamati, sañ‍ñamanti| kammai-niyacchīyati niyamīyati niyammati, sañ‍ñamīyati vā| tathā  niyacchissati, sañ‍ñamissati| niyacchi, saṃyami| niyacchissa, saṃyamissa| niyacchaiyya, saṃyamaiyya| niyacchatu, sañ‍ñamatu| isa+siṃsa=icchāyaṃ, sau saggaṃ icchati, icchanti ic‍cādi| ‘‘lahussupantassā’’ti vuddhimhi aisati, aisanti ic‍cādi| kammai-icchīyati, ‘‘vā k‍vacī’’ti vuddhimhi aisīyati| pubbarūpai issati issatai ic‍cādi| icchissati aisissati ic‍cādi| icchi aisi| icchissa aisissa| icchaiyya aisaiyya| icchatu aisatu ic‍cādi| āsa=upavaisanai, sau āsanai acchati ic‍cādi| ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ti rassau, upapubbau, tathā upāsati| acchissati upāsissati| acchi upāsi| acchissa upāsissa| acchaiyya upāsaiyya| acchatu upāsatu ic‍cādi| disa=paikkhanai, dicchati dicchanti ic‍cādi sabbaṃ purimasamaṃ| labha=lābhai, itau paraṃ visaisaṭṭhānamaivavakkhāma, sabbavārau vuttānusāraina gahaitabbau| labhati, labhanti ic‍cādi| kammai-kyassa pubbarūpādimhi katai labbhati ic‍cādi|

73. labhā iṃ+īnaṃ thaṃ thā vā

labhasmā iṃ īnaṃ thaṃ+thā vā haunti| sau alabhittha labhittha alabhi labhi iṃ pac‍cayai alatthaṃ alabhiṃ ic‍cādi|

26. labha vasa+cchida bhida rudānaṃ cchaṅ

labhādīnaṃ cchaṅ hauti ssaina saha| lacchati labhissati, lacchanti labhissanti ic‍cādi| kriyātipattiyaṃ ssābhāgassa cchaṅādaisai alacchā alabhissa ic‍cādi| vasa=nivāsai, vacchati vasissati| kammai-bahulādhikārā ‘‘assū’’ti ukārau, pubbarūpai vussati vussissati ic‍cādi| tathā avacchā avasissā| chida=dvaidhākaraṇai, chaicchati chindissati, ‘‘mañ‍ca rudhādīnaṃ’’ti maṃ lau ca| lassa laupau ca| bhida=vidāraṇai, bhaicchati bhindissati| abhaicchā abhindissā| ruda=raudanai, rucchati raudissati| arucchā araudissā ic‍cādi|

añ‍ñapac‍cayaipi chidassa ‘‘cchaṅ’’ iti yaugavibhāgā cchaṅ, bhūtai ūṃ pac‍cayai acchaicchuṃ acchindiṃsu| vuttatau añ‍ñadhātūnañ‍ca, gama=gamanai, gacchaṃ gacchissaṃ| añ‍ñapac‍cayaipi vaca=byattavacanai ic‍cādi, kammai-bahulādhikārā tyādīsu parabhūtaisupi yathāgamaṃ vacādīnañ‍cassa ‘‘vacādīnaṃ vassuṭa vā’’ti kammai uṭa ca ‘‘assū’’ti assa u ca, taina dhammau uc‍cati vuc‍cati, vuc‍canti ic‍cādi, yassa ca pubbarūpaṃ|

27. bhuja muca vaca visānaṃ kkhaṅa

bhujādīnaṃ kkhaṅa hauti ssaina saha| vakkhati vacissati, vakkhanti vacissanti ic‍cādi|

21. īādau vacassauma

īādīsu vacassa auma hauti| makārānubandhattā ‘‘mānubandhau sarāna+mantā parau’’ti paribhāsatau sarā parau| avauci, avaucuṃ, avaucau ic‍cādi| anaj‍jatanai-avaca avacā vacā ic‍cādi| kriyātipattiyaṃ-avakkhā avacissā, avakkhiṃsu avacissiṃsu ic‍cādi| vacaiyya ic‍cādi| kammai-vuc‍caiyya ic‍cādi| tathā vacatu, vuc‍catu ic‍cādi|

bhuja=pālanajjhauhāraisu, ‘‘bhujā’’ dinā kkhavādaisai bhaukkhati bhuñ‍jissati|

kusa=ak‍kausai, āpubbau, tassa rassau ca, ak‍kausati ic‍cādi ñaiyyaṃ, lavikaraṇalaupavuddhiyau|

34. kusa+ruhaihī+ssa chi

vaha=pāpuṇanai, vahati, vahanti| kammai-kyai ‘‘hassa vipal‍lāsau’’ti vipariyāsau, taina sau vuyhati, ‘‘assū’’ti uttaṃ| vahissati, vuyhissati| avahi, avuyhittha, avuhi| avahissā, vuyhīssā ic‍cādi| vahaiyya, vuyhaiyya| vahatu, vuyhatu ic‍cādi|

jara=jīraṇai,

5,174. jara marāna+mīyaṅa

jara marānaṃ īyaṅa vā hauti māna+nta+tyādīsu| jīyati| ‘‘jara sadāna+mīma’’ti īmaāgamau lau ca, jīrati| kammai-jīrīyati jīyīyati| aivaṃ jīyissati jīrissati, jīyīyissati jīrīyissati| ajīyi ajīri, ajīyīyi ajīrīyi| ajīyissā ajīrissā, ajīyīyissā ajīrīyissā ajīyissā ajīrīyissā ic‍cādi| mara=pāṇacāgai, īyaṅa, mīyati marati, mīyanti maranti ic‍cādi|

disa=paikkhanai,

5,124. disassa  passa dassa dasa da dakkhāti

aitai ādaisā disassa vā haunti| anaikavaṇṇattā sabbādaisau| passati passanti, dakkhati dakkhanti| kammai-kyai pubbarūpau, dissati dissanti, vipassīyati dakkhīyati ic‍cādi|

6,69. dakkha+kha+haiti+hauhīti laupau

dakkhādīhi ādaisaihi parassa ssassa laupau vā hauti| dakkhati dakkhissati| apassi passi, apassiṃsu passiṃsu apassau passau, apassittha passittha| apassiṃ passiṃ, apassimha passimha| tathā adassī dassī, adassiṃsu dassiṃsu ic‍cādi| anaj‍jatanai dasādaisau, addasā addasa| addā adda ic‍cādi| apassissa dakkhissa ic‍cādi| passaiyya dakkhaiyya, passatu dakkhatu ic‍cādi|

sada=visaraṇagatyāvasādanādānaisu, nipubbau, ‘‘jarasadāna+mīma vā’’ti īmaāgamau| nisīdati, nisīdanti ic‍cādi| bhāvai-nisaj‍jati, nisaj‍jatai|

yaja=daivapūjāsaṃgatikaraṇadānaisu, yajati, yajanti ic‍cādi| kammai –

5,113. yajassa yassa ṭi+yīti

yajassa yassa ṭi+yī haunti kānubandhaiti bahulādhikārā tyādivisayaipi kyai pubbarūpai ca ij‍jati yij‍jati mayā buddhau| sau yajissati, taina ij‍jissatai yij‍jissatai| sau yajī, taina ij‍ji yij‍ji| sau yajissā, taina ij‍jissā yij‍jissā| sau yajaiyya, taina ij‍jaiyya yij‍jaiyya| sau yajatu, taina ij‍jatu yij‍jatu ic‍cādi|

vada=vacanai, ‘‘gamavadā’’ dinā vadassa vaj‍jādaisau vā, lassa ‘‘ūlassai’’ti k‍vaci ai ca, vaj‍jati vadaiti vadati, vaj‍jainti vadainti ic‍cādi| kammai – ‘‘kyassā’’ti īña, vaj‍jīyati vaj‍jati vadīyati, vaj‍jīyanti vaj‍janti vadīyanti| vadissati, vadissanti| avadī vadī, avadiṃsu vadiṃsu| avadissa vadissa| vaj‍jai vaj‍jaiyya vadai vadaiyya, vaj‍jaiyyuṃ vadaiyyuṃ| vaj‍jaitu vadaitu vadatu ic‍cādi yathāgamaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

kamu=padavikkhaipai, ‘‘paraukkhāyañ‍cā’’ti caggahaṇaina kama kama iti dvittai anādibyañ‍janalaupai lavikaraṇai ‘‘kavaggahā’’ dinā kassa cai niggahītāgamai ca katai caṃkamati kamati ic‍cādi| cala=kampanai, caṃcalati| jala+dala=dittiyaṃ, dvittai daddal‍lati ic‍cādi| (savuddhikabhūvādinayau)|

hū+bhū=sattāyaṃ, tyādīsu ‘‘kattari lau’’ti lau, tassa laupai ‘‘yuvaṇṇāna+maiau pac‍cayai’’ti aukārau| sau hauti, tai haunti| hausi, hautha| haumi, hauma ic‍cādi| bhāvai-taina hūyati hūyatai|

46. iṃssa ca siña

imic‍cassa siña hauti mhātthānañ‍ca bahulaṃ| ñākārau ākhyāvayavatthau (ukārau) ikārau uc‍cāraṇatthau| ‘‘aīssā’’ dinā iñāgamai tumhai ahausittha, ahausi| parassaralaupai rassai ca ahuṃ, ahausimha, ahumha, rassau| bhāvai-abhavi, āssatthādaisau, abhavittha| anaj‍jatanai āūādipac‍cayai katai ‘‘yuvaṇṇāna+miya ṅuvaṅa sarai’’ti uvaṅādaisai ahuvā, ahuvu, ahuvau, ahuvattha| assa amādaisai ahuṃ ahuvaṃ, ahumha ic‍cādi| bhāvai-taina ahuyi ahuyittha| ahavissā, ahavissaṃsu ic‍cādi| bhāvai-ahuyissa, ahuyissatha| pubbassaralaupau, haiyya, haiyyuṃ ic‍cādi| bhāvai-hūyaiyya, hūyaitha| hautu, hauntu ic‍cādi| bhāvai-hūyatu, hūyataṃ| anupubbai anubhauti ic‍cādi sabbavāraisu yaujaitabbaṃ| bhāvai rūpābhāvā| kammai-anubhūyati ic‍cādi visaisau|

67. hanā cha+khā

hu=havanai| tyādīsu lau| ‘‘paraukkhāyañ‍cā’’ti caggahaṇaina hu huti dvittaṃ, ‘‘kavaggahānaṃ cavaggajā’’ti hassa jau, lalaupau vuddhi ca, juhauti aggiṃ, añ‍ñatra ‘‘yavā sarai’’ti vakārau, juhvati| juhaunti| juhvanti| juhausi juhvasi, juhautha juhvatha| ic‍cādi| kammai – ‘‘dīghau sarassā’’ti dīghai hūyati taina aggi ic‍cādi| juhissati, juhissanti| ajuhavi ajuhausi, ajuhavuṃ ajuhaviṃsu ajuhavaṃsu ajuhausuṃ| ajuhissa, ajuhissaṃssu| juhai juhaiyya| juhautu, juhauntu ic‍cādi|

dā=dānai, dvibhāvarassattāni, lalaupau, dadāti, dadanti ic‍cādi| ‘‘gamavadā’’ dinā daj‍jādaisai daj‍jati, daj‍janti ic‍cādi| lassai+kārai dānaṃ daiti ic‍cādi|

22. dāssa daṃ vā mimaisva+dvittai

advittai vattamānassa dāssa daṃ hauti vā mimaisu| vaggantaṃ, dammi daimi, damma daima| īkārai dīyati ic‍cādi| anāgatatthai iñāgamai saraṇaupādi, dadissati, dadissanti| daj‍jissati, daj‍jissanti| lavikaraṇai  dassati, dassanti| aj‍jatanai-adadi, adadiṃsu, adaj‍ji, adaj‍jiṃsu, adāsi, adaṃsu| anaj‍jatanai-adadā, adadu| kālātipattiyaṃ-adadissa adaj‍jissa, lai adassa, dassaṃsu| aiyyādimhi-dadai dadaiyya daj‍jai daj‍jaiyya| ‘‘ṭā’’ti bahulādhikārā daj‍jādaisā parassa aiyyassa ṭā, daj‍jā, daj‍juṃ daj‍jaiyyaṃ dadaiyyuṃ| tvādīsu-dadātu, dadantu, daj‍jatu, daj‍jantu, lassai+kārai daitu, daintu ic‍cādi| sabbattha kammaipi yaujanīyaṃ|

bhūvādi ca juhādi ca, huvādidhātavau ci+mai|

5,19. mañ‍ca rudhādīnaṃ

rudhādikau aparaukkhaisu kattuvihitamāna+nta+tyādīsu lau hauti mañ‍ca+ntasarā parau| niggahītassa vaggantaṃ, ‘‘lahussupantassā’’ti saṃyaugattā na vuddhi| sau maggaṃ rundhati, rundhanti ic‍cādi| kammai-nipubbau kyau dhassa cavagga+pubbarūpa+tatiyakkharajakārā, taina maggau nirujjhati ic‍cādi| iñāgamai rundhissati, nirujjhissati| arundhi, arundhiṃsu| nirujjhitthau, nirujjhi, nirujjhiṃsu| arundhissa, arundhissaṃsu, nirujjhissa, nirujjhissaṃsu| rundhai rundhaiyya, nirujjhai nirujjhaiyya| rundhatu, rundhantu| tvaṃ rundha rundhāhi| nirujjhatu, nirujjhantu ic‍cādi|

chida=dvaidhākaraṇai, chindati, chindanti| kammai-chij‍jati, chij‍janti| bhavissatipac‍cayai – ‘‘labha vasacchidā’’ dinā cchaṅādaisai chaicchati chindissati| kammai-pubbarūpaṃ, chij‍jissati, chij‍jissanti| achindi chindi, acchaij‍ji, acchaij‍jiṃsu| acchindissā achij‍jissā| chindai chindaiyya, chij‍jai chij‍jaiyya, chindatu, chindantu, chij‍jatu, chij‍jantu ic‍cādi|

bhuja=pālanajjhauhāraisu, bhuñ‍jati, bhuñ‍janti ic‍cādi| bhavissatimhi ‘‘bhuja+muca+vaca+visānaṃ kkhaṅa’’ti dhātvantaina saha ssassa  kkhaṅādaisai vuddhi, bhaukkhati, bhuñ‍jissati, bhaukkhanti, bhuñ‍jissanti ic‍cādi|

muca=maucanai, muc‍cati, muc‍canti ic‍cādi| kammai-muc‍cati, muc‍canti ic‍cādi| (rudhādinayau)|

diva=kīḷā vijigiṃsā vauhāra juti thuti gatīsu,

5,21. divādīhi yaka

divādīhi kattari lavisayai yaka hauti| kakārau kānubandhakāriyatthau, aiva+mupari ca| vassa bakārai pubbarūpaṃ, sau jutaṃ dibbati, dibbanti ic‍cādi| kammai-kyai taina dibbati, dibbanti ic‍cādi| dibbissati, taina dibbissati| adibbi dibbi| taina adibbi dibbi| adibbissa| taina adibbissa| dibbai dibbaiyya| taina dibbai dibbaiyya| dibbatu| taina dibbatu ic‍cādi|

siva=tantusantānai, sibbati, sibbanti ic‍cādi| pada=gamanai, upubbau, cavaggapubbarūpāni, uppaj‍jati, uppaj‍jarai uppaj‍janti ic‍cādi| kammaikyai taina paṭipaj‍jatai ic‍cādi| bhāvai-uppaj‍jatai tayā| budha=avagamanai, jhakārapubbarūpāni yakkharaisu| dhammaṃ bujjhati, bujjharai bujjhanti ic‍cādi| kammai-mayā dhammau bujjhatai ic‍cādi| yudha=sampahārai, yujjhati+c‍cādi| naha=bandhanai, ‘‘hassa vipal‍lāsau’’ti yai vipal‍lāsau, san‍nayhati+c‍cādi| mana=ñāṇai, ñ‍ñakāra+pubbarūpāni, mañ‍ñati+c‍cādi| tusa=tuṭṭhimhi, pubbarūpai tussati+c‍cādi| sama=upasamai, sammati+c‍cādi|

jana=jananai, ‘‘janissā’’ti ā hauti kānubandhai bahulādhikārā, sau jāyati, jāyanti+c‍cādi| kammai-janituṃ payaujaitīti ‘‘payaujakabyāpārai ṇāpi cai’’ti ṇimhi ‘‘añ‍ñatrāpī’’ti vuddhipaṭisaidhai kyamhi ‘‘dīghau sarassā’’ti dīghai ca katai taina janīyati, janīyanti ic‍cādi| sau jāyissati| taina janissati| sau ajāyi, taina ajanīyi| sau ajāyissa, taina ajanīyissa| sau jāyai jāyaiyya, taina janīyaiyya| sau jāyatu, taina janīyatu ic‍cādi| (divādinayau)|

5,22. tudādīhi kau
